Śrī Navadvīpa-dhāma Māhātmya was composed by Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura in 1890. This work consists of 18 chapters of Bengali verse wherein Bhaktivinoda describes Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu taking a young Jīva Gosvāmī on a pilgrimage of the nine islands of Navadvīpa-dhāma, and describing the various pastimes that occurred in those holy places. Preluding the actual text is a Sanskrit prayer of 20 verses composed by Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura’s called Śrīman Navadvīpa-dhāma Vandanā.

# Sri Navadvipa-dhama Mahatmya

## Śrīman Navadvīpa-dhāma-vandanā

(A summary of Śrī Navadvīpa-dhāma-māhātmya in twenty Sanskrit verses)

### Text 1

*śrutic-chāndogyākhyā vadati paramaṁ brahma-purakaṁ*  
*smṛtir vaikuṇṭhākhyāṁ vadati kila yaṁ viṣṇu-sadanam*  
*sitadvīpaṁ cānye virala-rasiko yaṁ vraja-vanaṁ*  
*navadvīpaṁ vande tam iha paramānanda-nilayam*

**Перевод:**

**The** ***śruti*** **known as** ***Chāndogya*** **calls it ‘the city of the Supreme Absolute’. The** ***smṛti*** **calls it ‘Vaikuṇṭha’, the abode of Lord Viṣṇu. Others know it as ‘the white island’. The rare relishers of** ***rasa*** **call it ‘the forest of Vraja.’ I bow here to Navadvīpa, the abode of supreme joy**

### Text 2

*yad ekāṁśe brahmā nija-kucaritāt moha-janitāt*  
*kṛpā-sindhuṁ gauraṁ satata-manu-taptaḥ samabhajat*  
*prabhus tasmai gūḍhāṁ nija-hṛdaya-vāñchāṁ samavadat*  
*navadvīpaṁ vande tam iha paramānanda-nilayam*

**Перевод:**

**In one of its regions, Brahmā, constantly repenting for his misbehaviour caused by bewilderment, worshipped Gaura, the ocean of mercy. The Lord then fulfilled the deepest desire in Brahmā’s heart. I bow here to Navadvīpa, the abode of supreme joy.**

### Text 3

*yad ekāṁśe gaurī giri-vara-sutā viśva-jananī*  
*śacī-sūnor dṛṣṭvā bhajana-viṣayaṁ rūpam atulam*  
*svasīmante prādāt prabhu-caraṇa-reṇuṁ bhagavatī*  
*navadvīpaṁ vande tam iha paramānanda-nilayam*

**Перевод:**

**In one of its regions, Bhagavatī, the golden-complexioned daughter of the Himalayas, the mother of the universe, beheld the unparalleled form of the object of her worship, the son of Śacī. She then placed the dust of the Lord’s feet upon the parting in her hair. I bow here to Navadvīpa, the abode of supreme joy.**

### Text 4

*yad ekāṁśe vajrī nija-kumati-taptaḥ susurabhiṁ*  
*samāśritya premṇā druma-tala-samīpe hari-padam*  
*bhajan sākṣād gaurād varam ati-śubhaṁ prāpa vibudho*  
*navadvīpaṁ vande tam iha paramānanda-nilayam*

**Перевод:**

**In one of its regions, the wielder of the thunderbolt, Lord Indra, remorseful for his impudence, took shelter of beautiful Surabhi, lovingly worshipped the feet of the Lord beside a tree, and attained a most auspicious boon from Gaura Himself. I bow here to Navadvīpa, the abode of supreme joy.**

### Text 5

*yad ekāṁśe saptarṣi-gaṇa-bhajanākṛṣṭa-hṛdaya*  
*ḥaho! gauraḥ sārddha-prahara-samaye prādurabhavat*  
*varaṁ tebhyaḥ prādāc carama-samaye yad dhitakaraṁ*  
*navadvīpaṁ vande tam iha paramānanda-nilayam*

**Перевод:**

**In one of its regions, His heart attracted by their service, Gaura appeared to the seven sages at noon, and, oh, bestowed upon them a pleasing boon at the perfect time! I bow here to Navadvīpa, the abode of supreme joy.**

### Text 6

*yad ekāṁśe kaśchid dvija-kula-patiḥ puṣkara-matiḥ*  
*svavārddhakyāt tīrtha-bhramaṇa-viṣaye śakti-rahitaḥ*  
*dadarśāgre tīrthaṁ parama-śubhadaṁ puṣkaram api*  
*navadvīpaṁ vande tam iha paramānanda-nilayam*

**Перевод:**

**In one of its regions, a** ***brāhmaṇa*** **chief, who longed for Puṣkara Tirtha but because of his old age lacked the ability to make the pilgrimage there, saw the supremely auspicious Puṣkara Tīrtha before him. I bow here to Navadvīpa, the abode of supreme joy.**

### Text 7

*yad ekāṁśe kolākṛti-dhṛg ati-citraṁ makha-patiṁ*  
*svabhaktāya prītyā ratim ati-viśuddhāṁ tribhuvane*  
*dadau śrī-gaurāṅge svabhajana-balākṛṣṭa-hṛdayo*  
*navadvīpaṁ vande tam iha paramānanda-nilayam*

**Перевод:**

**In one of its regions, the Lord of sacrifice, who bears the dazzling form of a boar, His heart was drawn by the power of His devotee’s service, affectionately bestowed upon His devotee the purest thing in the three worlds—love for Śrī Gaurāṅga. I bow here to Navadvīpa, the abode of supreme joy.**

### Text 8

*yad ekāṁśe kuñje nija-bala-vṛto ’yaṁ ṛtu-patiḥ*  
*naṭantaṁ caitanyaṁ svagaṇa-pariyuktaṁ samabhajat*  
*latā-gulmākīrṇe phala-kusuma-bhāra-praṇamite*  
*navadvīpaṁ vande tam iha paramānanda-nilayam*

**Перевод:**

**In one of its regions, the king of the seasons, checking his own power, worshipped Śrī Caitanya, who, along with His associates, was dancing in a grove whose creepers and shrubs, laden with fruits and flowers, bowed to Him. I bow here to Navadvīpa, the abode of supreme joy.**

### Text 9

*yad ekāṁśe jahnur bhajana-samaye śubhra-salilāṁ*  
*samāyātāṁ dṛṣṭvā pratikūla-taraṅgāṁ samapibat*  
*amuñcat tāṁ bhaktyā punar api munir jahnu-tanayāṁ*  
*navadvīpaṁ vande tam iha paramānanda-nilayam*

**Перевод:**

**In one of its regions, Jahnu Muni, upon seeing the Gaṅgā’s glistening waters approaching during his worship, drank her distracting waves. He then released her again as his daughter because of the devotion (of King Bhagīratha). I bow here to Navadvīpa, the abode of supreme joy.**

### Text 10

*yad ekāṁśe rāmo daśaratha-suto lakṣmaṇa-yutaḥ*  
*purā sītā-sārddhaṁ katipaya-dinaṁ gāṅga-puline*  
*avātsīt tretāyāṁ muni-nikaro modadruma-tale*  
*navadvīpaṁ vande tam iha paramānanda-nilayam*

**Перевод:**

**In one of its regions, in the Tretā-yuga of a previous era, the son of Daśaratha, the wealth of the sages, Rāma, with his other half, Sītā, and Lakṣmaṇa, resided for a few days on the bank of the Gaṅgā beneath a pleasure tree. I bow here to Navadvīpa, the abode of supreme joy.**

### Text 11

*yad ekāṁśe nārāyaṇam api paraṁ nārada-munir*  
*dadarśāyaṁ sākṣāt sakala-bhajanīyaṁ sura-varam*  
*apaśyat taṁ paścāt parama-puruṣaṁ gaura-vapuṣaṁ*  
*navadvīpaṁ vande tam iha paramānanda-nilayam*

**Перевод:**

**In one of its regions, the sage Nārada directly beheld the greatest of Gods, Nārāyaṇa, who is supremely worshippable to all, and then saw the Supreme Lord in His golden form. I bow here to Navadvīpa, the abode of supreme joy.**

### Text 12

*yad ekāṁśe pārtho drupada-tanayā-sevita-padaḥ*  
*avātsīt sabhrātaḥ katipaya-dinaṁ gaura-kṛpayā*  
*mahāraṇye puṇye muni-nikara-sevye hari-sakhaḥ*  
*navadvīpaṁ vande tam iha paramānanda-nilayam*

**Перевод:**

**In one of its regions, by the grace of Gaura, the Lord’s friend Arjuna, served by Draupadī and accompanied by his brothers, resided for a few days in a large, auspicious forest that is worshipped by hosts of sages. I bow here to Navadvīpa, the abode of supreme joy.**

### Text 13

*yad ekāṁśe rudraḥ svagaṇa-sahitaḥ prema-galitaḥ*  
*naṭan mandaṁ mandaṁ kara-ḍamaru-vādya-pramuditaḥ*  
*aho! gāyatyuchhaiḥ satatam api viśvambharam asau*  
*navadvīpaṁ vande tam iha paramānanda-nilayam*

**Перевод:**

**In one of its regions, Rudra, lost in divine love, gently dances with his associates, enlivened by the sound of** ***ḍamarus*****. Oh! How he continuously sings about Viśvambhara in this place! I bow here to Navadvīpa, the abode of supreme joy.**

### Text 14

*yathā sthāne sthāne jala-parivṛtās tīrtha-nikarāḥ*  
*virājante śaśvat sakala-muni-sevyā hy agha-harāḥ*  
*tathā devāḥ sarve girīśa-parameṣṭhi-prabhṛtayo*  
*navadvīpaṁ vande tam iha paramānanda-nilayam*

**Перевод:**

**An abundance of holy places, surrounded by water, shine eternally throughout this land. Attended by Śiva, Brahmā, and all the other gods, these places destroy all sin and are worshippable to all the sages. I bow here to Navadvīpa, the abode of supreme joy.**

### Text 15

*yathā prauḍhā māyā svapati-sahitā vaiṣṇava-ripūn*  
*jaḍānandaṁ datvā hari-niyama-kartrī chalayati*  
*mṛṣā-śāstrācārair mada-vicalitān mohayati ca*  
*navadvīpaṁ vande tam iha paramānanda-nilayam*

**Перевод:**

**Here, Prauḍhā Māyā, the Lord’s warden, along with her husband, deceives the enemies of the Vaiṣṇavas by giving them material happiness and bewilders those who are blinded by pride in their futile study of the scriptures. I bow here to Navadvīpa, the abode of supreme joy.**

### Text 16

*yathā vaiṣā kālī danuja-dalanī śambhu-ramaṇī*  
*harer bhaktān snehāt kapaṭa-rahitā pālayati ca*  
*parānandaṁ gauraṁ bhajati niyataṁ prema-galitā*  
*navadvīpaṁ vande tam iha paramānanda-nilayam*

**Перевод:**

**Here, Kālī, the wife of Śambhu and destroyer of the demons, sincerely protects the devotees of the Lord out of affection for them. Lost in divine love, she eternally worships Gaura in ecstasy. I bow here to Navadvīpa, the abode of supreme joy.**

### Text 17

*yathā vāṇī sākṣāt prabhu-caraṇa-sevāśaya-ratā*  
*dvijātibhyo vidyāṁ nikhila-naya-śāstrādi-viṣayām*  
*dadāty eṣā nityaṁ vibudha-taṭinī-tīra-viṣaye*  
*navadvīpaṁ vande tam iha paramānanda-nilayam*

**Перевод:**

**Sarasvatī, the goddess of speech, whose heart is given to the service of the Lord’s feet, eternally resides on the bank of the Gaṅgā in this land and personally bestows upon the** ***brāhmaṇas*** **knowledge of all the scriptures on conduct and other subjects. I bow here to Navadvīpa, the abode of supreme joy.**

### Text 18

*hariḥ śrīmad-rādhā-dyuti-kavalitaḥ pārṣada-vṛtaḥ*  
*śacī-garbhodbhūtaḥ kali-kaluṣa-nāśodyata-manaḥ*  
*yathā nāmnaḥ saṅkīrtanam ati-pavitraṁ samakarot*  
*navadvīpaṁ vande tam iha paramānanda-nilayam*

**Перевод:**

**Adorned with the lustre of Śrī Rādhā, the Lord appeared here from the womb of Śrī Śacī. Intent on purging the filth of Kali, He engaged in supremely purifying chanting of the Name, accompanied by His associates. I bow here to Navadvīpa, the abode of supreme joy.**

### Text 19

*aho! bhaktāḥ kecit parama-ramaṇīye jana-pade*  
*naṭantaṁ gaurāṅgaṁ nija-jana-balākā-parivṛtam*  
*yathā paśyanty addhā hari-bhajana-siddhau svanayanair*  
*navadvīpaṁ vande tam iha paramānanda-nilayam*

**Перевод:**

**Oh! Here, some devotees, expert in the service of the Lord, directly see with their own eyes Śrī Gaurāṅga dancing amidst His associates and their wives in this supremely beautiful city! I bow here to Navadvīpa, the abode of supreme joy.**

### Text 20

*navadvīpe yo vai kṛta-nivasatir dvaidha-rahitaḥ*  
*idaṁ stotraṁ bhaktyā paṭhati hari-pūjādi-samaye*  
*cidānande sākṣāt praṇaya-sukha-bhāvaṁ bhagavati*  
*śacī-sūnau kṛṣṇe parama-ramaṇīyaṁ sa labhate*

**Перевод:**

**One who resides in Navadvīpa without duplicity and recites this prayer with devotion at the time of worshipping the Lord, genuinely attains the supremely delightful joy of love for the Lord, the embodiment of spiritual ecstasy—the son of Śacī, who is Kṛṣṇa Himself.**

## Chapter One – The Glories of the Dhāma

### Text 1

*jaya jaya navadvīpa-candra śacī-suta*  
*jaya jaya nityānanda-rāya avadhūta*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to the son of Śacī, the moon of Navadvīpa! All glory to the** ***avadhūta*** **Nityānanda Rāy!**

### Text 2

*jaya jaya śrī-advaita prabhu mahāśaya*  
*gadādhara śrīvāsa paṇḍita jaya jaya*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to the worshippable Śrī Advaita Prabhu! All glory to Gadādhara Paṇḍita and Śrīvāsa Paṇḍita!**

### Text 3

*jaya navadvīpa-dhāma sarva-dhāma-sāra*  
*jaya navadvīpa-vāsī gaura-parivāra*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to Navadvīpa Dhāma, the best of all abodes! All glory to the residents of Navadvīpa, the associates of Śrī Gaura!**

### Text 4

*sakala bhakata-pade kariyā praṇāma*  
*saṁkṣepe varṇiba āmi navadvīpa-dhāma*

**Перевод:**

**Bowing at the feet of all the devotees, I will briefly describe Navadvīpa Dhāma.**

### Text 5

*navadvīpa-maṇḍalera mahimā apāra*  
*brahmā ādi nāhi jāne varṇe sādhya kāra*

**Перевод:**

**The glories of Navadvīpa Maṇḍala are unlimited. Brahmā and the other gods do not know them. So, who can describe them?**

### Text 6

*sahasra vadane śeṣa varṇite akṣama*  
*kṣudra-jīva āmi kise ha-iba sakṣama*

**Перевод:**

**Ananta Śeṣa is unable to describe them with thousands of mouths. So, how will I, a tiny soul, be able to?**

### Text 7

*satya vaṭe navadvīpa mahimā ananta*  
*deva-deva mahādeva nāhi pāya anta*

**Перевод:**

**It is true that the glories of Navadvīpa are unlimited. The god of gods, Lord Śiva, cannot reach their end.**

### Text 8

*tathāpi caitanya-candra-icchā balavan*  
*sei icchā-vaśe bhakta ājñāra vidhāna*

**Перевод:**

**Still, the will of the moon-like Śrī Caitanya is all-powerful, and under its control, the devotees give orders.**

### Text 9

*bhakta-gaṇe ājñā dila caitanya icchāya*  
*nadīyā-māhātmya varṇi bhaktera kṛpāya*

**Перевод:**

**By the will of Śrī Caitanya, the devotees have ordered me, and so I describe the glories of Nadia by their mercy.**

### Text 10

*āra eka kathā āchhe gūḍha atiśaya*  
*kahite nā icchā haya nā kahile naya*

**Перевод:**

**There is another very confidential subject, which I do not want to mention, yet cannot but mention.**

### Text 11

*ye avadhi śrī-caitanya aprakaṭa haila*  
*dhāma-līlā prakāśite bhakte ājñā dila*

**Перевод:**

**When Śrī Caitanya disappeared, He ordered the devotees to reveal His abode and Pastimes.**

### Text 12

*sarva-avatāra haite gūḍha avatāra*  
*śrī-caitanya-candra mora vidita saṁsāra*

**Перевод:**

**Of all** ***avatāras*****, our moon-like Śrī Caitanya is the most confidential known in this world.**

### Text 13

*gūḍha-līlā śāstre gūḍha-rūpe ukta haya*  
*abhakta janera citte nā haya udaya*

**Перевод:**

**His confidential Pastimes are mentioned confidentially in the scriptures. They are not revealed in the hearts of non-devotees.**

### Text 14

*se līlā sambandhe yata gūḍha śāstra chila*  
*māyā-devī bahu-kāla ācchādi’ rākhila*

**Перевод:**

**For a long time, Māyā Devī hid the confidential scriptures that existed about His Pastimes.**

### Text 15

*aprakaṭa śāstra bahu rahe yathā tathā*  
*prakaṭa śāstreo yata caitanyera kathā*

### Text 16

*se sakala māyā-devī paṇḍita nayana*  
*āvariyā rākhe gupta-bhāve anukṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Māyā Devī covered the eyes of the scholars and at all times kept secret all the descriptions of Śrī Caitanya throughout both the manifest and unmanifest scriptures.**

### Text 17

*gaurera gambhīra līlā haile aprakaṭa*  
*prabhu-icchā jāni’ māyā haya akapaṭa*

**Перевод:**

**When the deep Pastimes of Śrī Gaura became unmanifest, Māyā understood the will of the Lord and withdrew her deception.**

### Text 18

*uṭhāiyā laila jāla jīva-cakṣu haite*  
*prakāśila gaura-tattva e jaḍa jagate*

**Перевод:**

**Māyā lifted her veil from the eyes of the souls and revealed the truth about Śrī Gaura to the material world.**

### Text 19

*gupta-śāstra anāyāse ha-ila prakaṭa*  
*ghucila jīvera yata yuktira saṅkaṭa*

**Перевод:**

**The hidden scriptures then appeared clearly and dispelled the doubts of all souls.**

### Text 20

*baḍa-i dayālu prabhu nityānanda-rāya*  
*gaura-tattva prakāśila jīvera hiyāya*

**Перевод:**

**The extremely merciful Nityānanda Prabhu revealed the truth about Śrī Gaura in the hearts of the souls.**

### Text 21

*tāṅra ājñā peye māyā chāḍe āvaraṇa*  
*subhakta paṇḍita-gaṇa pāya śāstra-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**Receiving His order, Māyā removed her veil, and the wise devotees discovered the wealth of the scriptures.**

### Text 22

*ihāte sandeha yāra nā haya khaṇḍana*  
*se abhāgā vṛthā kena dharaya jīvana*

**Перевод:**

**Those whose doubts are not dispelled by this are unfortunate. Why do such persons meaninglessly continue their lives?**

### Text 23

*ye kāle īśvara yei kṛpā vitaraya*  
*bhāgyavanta jana tāhe baḍa sukhī haya*

**Перевод:**

**Whenever and howsoever the Lord bestows His mercy, the fortunate souls become extremely happy.**

### Text 24-25

*durbhāgā lakṣaṇa ei jāna sarva-jana*  
*nija buddhi baḍa bali’ kariyā gaṇana*

*īśvarera kṛpā nāhi karaya svīkāra*  
*kutarke māyāra garte paḍe bāra-bāra*

**Перевод:**

**Everyone should know the symptoms of misfortune: to consider your own intelligence to be great and not accept the mercy of the Lord. Through false arguments, souls fall into Māyā’s traps again and again.**

### Text 26

*“esa he kalira jīva chāḍa kuṭināṭi*  
*nirmala gaurāṅga-prema laha paripāṭi”*

**Перевод:**

**“O souls of Kali-yuga, come! Give up duplicity, and accept pure divine love for Śrī Gaurāṅga in full!”**

### Text 27

*ei bali’ nityānanda ḍāke bāra-bāra*  
*tabu ta durbhāgā jana nā kare svīkāra*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this, Nityānanda calls out again and again. Still, unfortunate souls do not accept.**

### Text 28

*kena ye emana preme kare anādara*  
*vicāra kariyā dekha ha-iyā tatpara*

**Перевод:**

**Let us consider carefully why souls disregard divine love.**

### Text 29

*sukha-lāgi sarva-jīva nānā yukti kare*  
*tarka kare yoga kare saṁsāra bhitare*

**Перевод:**

**In various ways, all souls pursue happiness. In this world, some souls study logic, and others practise yoga.**

### Text 30

*sukha-lāgi saṁsāra chāḍiyā vane yāya*  
*sukha-lāgi yuddha kare rājāya rājāya*

**Перевод:**

**For the sake of happiness, some leave home and go to the forest, and others wage wars between nations.**

### Text 31

*sukha-lāgi kāminī-kanaka pāche dhāya*  
*sukha-lāgi śilpa āra vijñāna cālāya*

**Перевод:**

**For the sake of happiness, some run after women and wealth, and others pursue the arts and sciences.**

### Text 32

*sukha-lāgi sukha chāḍe kleśa śikṣā kare*  
*sukha-lāgi arṇava madhyete ḍubi mare*

**Перевод:**

**For the sake of happiness, some give up on happiness and learn to be miserable, and others drown themselves in the ocean.**

### Text 33

*nityānanda bale ḍāki’ duhāta tuliyā*  
*“esa jīva karma-jñāna-saṅkaṭa chāḍiyā*

**Перевод:**

**Raising His arms, Nityānanda calls, “O souls! Give up the misery of exploitation and renunciation, and come with Me!**

### Text 34

*sukha-lāgi ceṣṭā tava āmi tāhā diba*  
*tāra vinimaye āmi kichu nā la-iba*

**Перевод:**

**“You are searching for happiness. I will give that to you, and I will not take anything from you in return.**

### Text 35

*kaṣṭa nāi vyaya nāi nā pābe yātanā*  
*‘śrī-gaurāṅga’ bali’ nāca nāhika bhāvanā*

**Перевод:**

**“You will not undergo any trouble, loss, or pain. Simply chant, ‘Śrī Gaurāṅga’ and dance without a care.**

### Text 36

*ye sukha āmi ta diba tāra nāi sama*  
*sarvadā vimalānanda nāhi tāra bhrama”*

**Перевод:**

**“The happiness I will give you is unparalleled. It is pure, eternal joy, free from all illusion.”**

### Text 37

*ei rūpe prema yāce nityānanda-rāya*  
*abhāgā karama-doṣe tāhā nāhi cāya*

**Перевод:**

**In this way, Nityānanda Prabhu begs everyone to accept divine love, but the unfortunate, as a result of their sins, do not want it.**

### Text 38

*“gaurāṅga nitāi” yei bale eka-bara*  
*ananta karama-doṣa anta haya tāra*

**Перевод:**

**The unlimited sins of anyone who once chants, “Gaurāṅga! Nitāi!”are destroyed.**

### Text 39

*āra eka gūḍha kathā śuna sarva-jana*  
*kali-jīve yogya-vastu gaura-līlā-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**Everyone, please listen to another confidential subject. The wealth of Gaura’s Pastimes is appropriate for the souls of Kali-yuga.**

### Text 40

*gaurahari rādhā-kṛṣṇa-rūpe vṛndāvane*  
*nitya-kāla vilāsa karaye sakhī-sane*

**Перевод:**

**Gaurahari, as Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, plays with the** ***sakhīs*** **eternally in Vṛndāvana.**

### Text 41

*śāstrete jānila jīva vraja-līlā-tattva*  
*rādhā-kṛṣṇa-nitya-līlā vrajera mahattva*

**Перевод:**

**Through the scriptures, the souls understood the nature of Pastimes in Vraja, and the greatness of Vraja and Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa’s eternal Pastimes there.**

### Text 42

*kṛṣṇa-nāma kṛṣṇa-dhāma māhātmya apāra*  
*śāstrera dvārāya jāne sakala saṁsāra*

**Перевод:**

**Through the scriptures, the whole world understood the unlimited glories of Kṛṣṇa’s Name and Kṛṣṇa’s abode.**

### Text 43

*tabu kṛṣṇa-prema sādhāraṇe nāhi pāya*  
*ihāra kāraṇa kibā cintaha hiyāya*

**Перевод:**

**Still, in general, souls did not attain divine love for Kṛṣṇa. What is the reason for this? Consider it within your heart.**

### Text 44

*ihāte āchhe ta eka gūḍha-tattva-sāra*  
*māyā-mugdha jīva tāhā nā kare vicāra*

**Перевод:**

**In this regard, there is a highly confidential subject that souls bewildered by Māyā do not consider.**

### Text 45

*bahu janma kṛṣṇa bhaji’ prema nāhi haya*  
*aparādha-puñja tāra āchaya niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**When souls serve Kṛṣṇa for many births but do not develop divine love, then certainly they have made offences.**

### Text 46

*aparādha-śūnya haye laya kṛṣṇa-nāma*  
*tabe jīva kṛṣṇa-prema labhe avirāma*

**Перевод:**

**When souls, free from offences, chant the Name of Kṛṣṇa, they quickly attain divine love for Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 47

*śrī-caitanya-avatāre baḍa vilakṣaṇa*  
*aparādha-sattve jīva labhe prema-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**The special characteristic of Śrī Caitanya’s Pastimes is that souls attain the wealth of divine love even while they commit offences.**

### Text 48

*“nitāi caitanya” bali’ yei jīva ḍāke*  
*suvimala kṛṣṇa-prema anveṣaye tāke*

**Перевод:**

**Pure divine love for Kṛṣṇa searches for souls who call out “Nitāi-Caitanya!”**

### Text 49

*aparādha bādhā tāra kichu nāhi kare*  
*niramala kṛṣṇa-preme tāra āṅkhi jhare*

**Перевод:**

**Offences do not obstruct such souls, and their eyes cry with pure love for Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 50

*svalpa-kāle aparādha āpani palāya*  
*hṛdaya śodhita haya prema bāḍe tāya*

**Перевод:**

**Within a short time, offences leave on their own. The souls’ hearts become purified, and divine love develops within them.**

### Text 51

*kali-jīvera aparādha asaṅkhya durvāra*  
*gaura-nāma vinā tāra nāhika uddhāra*

**Перевод:**

**The offences of the souls in Kali-yuga are unlimited and unavoidable. There is no deliverance for them without the Name of Gaura.**

### Text 52

*ataeva gaura vinā kalite upāya*  
*nā dekhi kothāo āra śāstra phukāraya*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, in Kali-yuga, I do not see any way without Gaura, and the scriptures proclaim this.**

### Text 53

*navadvīpe gaura-candra ha-ila udaya*  
*navadvīpa sarva-tīrtha avataṁsa haya*

**Перевод:**

**Gaura-candra appeared in Navadvīpa, the crown atop all the holy places.**

### Text 54

*anya tīrthe aparādhī daṇḍera bhājana*  
*navadvīpe aparādha sadāi mārjana*

**Перевод:**

**In other holy places, offenders are punished, but in Navadvīpa offences are always forgiven.**

### Text 55

*tāra sākṣī jagāi-mādhāi dui bhāi*  
*aparādha kari’ pāila caitanya-nitāi*

**Перевод:**

**The brothers Jagāi and Mādhāi are evidence of this. They committed offences but attained Nitāi and Caitanya.**

### Text 56

*anyānya tīrthera kathā rākha bhāi dūre*  
*aparādhī daitya daṇḍa pāya vraja-pure*

**Перевод:**

**Brother! Let alone other holy places, offenders and demons are punished even in Vraja.**

### Text 57

*navadvīpe śata śata aparādha kari’*  
*anāyāse nitāi kṛpāya yāya tari’*

**Перевод:**

**In Navadvīpa, souls who commit hundreds and hundreds of offences are easily delivered by the mercy of Nitāi.**

### Text 58

*hena navadvīpa-dhāma ye gauḍa-maṇḍale*  
*dhanya dhanya sei deśa ṛṣi-gaṇa bale*

**Перевод:**

**The sages say that the land of Śrī Navadvīpa Dhāma in Śrī Gauḍa Maṇḍala is most glorious.**

### Text 59

*hena navadvīpe bhāi yāṅhāra vasati*  
*baḍa bhāgyavāna sei labhe kṛṣṇa-rati*

**Перевод:**

**Brother, souls who reside in Navadvīpa are very fortunate. They attain devotion to Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 60

*navadvīpe yebā kabhu karaya gamana*  
*sarva-aparādha mukta haya sei jana*

**Перевод:**

**Souls who once visit Navadvīpa are freed from all offences.**

### Text 61

*sarva-tīrtha bhramiyā tairthika yāhā pāya*  
*navadvīpa-smaraṇe sei lābha śāstre gāya*

**Перевод:**

**The scriptures say that by remembering Navadvīpa you attain that which pilgrims attain by visiting all the other holy places.**

### Text 62

*navadvīpa daraśana kare yei jana*  
*janme janme labhe sei kṛṣṇa-prema-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**Souls who see Navadvīpa attain the wealth of divine love for Kṛṣṇa birth after birth.**

### Text 63

*karma-buddhi-yogeo ye navadvīpe yāya*  
*nara janma āra sei jana nāhi pāya*

**Перевод:**

**Even souls who go to Navadvīpa with a materialistic motivation do not take a human birth again.**

### Text 64

*navadvīpa bhramite se pade pade pāya*  
*koṭi aśvamedha phala sarva-śāstre gāya*

**Перевод:**

**All the scriptures say that while walking through Navadvīpa, such souls attain the result of ten million horse sacrifices with each step.**

### Text 65

*navadvīpe vasi’ yei mantra japa kare*  
*śrī-mantra caitanya haya, anāyāse tare*

**Перевод:**

**Those who reside in Navadvīpa and chant their mantra—that holy mantra comes alive and easily delivers them.**

### Text 66

*anya tīrthe yogī daśa-varṣe labhe yāhā*  
*navadvīpe tina rātre sādhi’ pāya tāhā*

**Перевод:**

**That which** ***yogīs*** **attain after ten years in other holy places is attained in three nights in Navadvīpa.**

### Text 67

*anya tīrthe brahma-jñāne yei mukti haya*  
*navadvīpe bhāgīrathī snāne tā ghaṭaya*

**Перевод:**

**The liberation attained through knowledge of Brahmā in other holy places is attained in Navadvīpa simply by bathing in the Gaṅgā.**

### Text 68

*sālokya-sārūpya-sārṣṭi-sāmīpya-nirvāṇa*  
*navadvīpe mumukṣu labhaya vinā jñāna*

**Перевод:**

**In Navadvīpa, even without knowledge of Brahma, seekers of liberation can attain residence in the Lord’s abode, a form like the Lord’s, opulence like the Lord’s, association with the Lord, and absorption into the Lord.**

### Text 69

*navadvīpe śuddha-bhakta caraṇe paḍiyā*  
*bhukti mukti sadā rahe dāsī rūpa haiyā*

**Перевод:**

**Enjoyment and liberation personified bow at the feet of the pure devotees in Navadvīpa and always wait upon them as maidservants.**

### Text 70

*bhakta-gaṇa lāthi māri’ se duye tāḍāya*  
*bhakta-pada chāḍi’ dāsī tabu nā palāya*

**Перевод:**

**Although devotees kick them and drive them away, these maidservants do not leave the feet of the devotees or run away.**

### Text 71

*śata-varṣa sapta-tīrthe mile yāhā bhāi*  
*navadvīpe eka-rātra vāse tāhā pāi*

**Перевод:**

**Brother, by spending one night in Navadvīpa, souls attain that which is gained by residing in the seven principal holy places for one hundred years.**

### Text 72

*hena navadvīpa-dhāma sarva-dhāma-sāra*  
*kalite āśraya kari’ jīva haya pāra*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, Navadvīpa Dhāma is the best of all abodes. Souls who take shelter here in Kali-yuga are delivered.**

### Text 73

*tāraka pāraka vidyā-dvaya avirata*  
*navadvīpa-vāsi-gaṇe seve rīti-mata*

**Перевод:**

**The two forms of knowledge—that which liberates and that which nourishes—constantly and perfectly serve the residents of Navadvīpa.**

### Text 74

*nitāi-jāhnavā-pada-chāyā yāra āśa*  
*se bhaktivinoda gāya pāiyā ullāsa*

**Перевод:**

**Bhaktivinoda, whose aspiration is the shade of Nitāi and Jāhnavā’s feet, sings the glories of Nadīya and rejoices.**

## Chapter Two – The Nature and Dimensions of the Dhāma

### Text 1

*jaya jaya navadvīpa-candra śacī-suta*  
*jaya jaya nityānanda-rāya avadhūta*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to the son of Śacī, the moon of Navadvīpa! All glory to the** ***avadhūta*** **Śrī Nityānanda Rāya!**

### Text 2

*jaya jaya navadvīpa sarva-dhāma-sāra*  
*se dhāmera tattva varṇe sādhya āchhe kāra*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to Navadvīpa, the best of all abodes! Who can describe its glory?**

### Text 3

*navadvīpa-dhāma gauḍa-maṇḍala bhitare*  
*jāhnavī-sevita haye sadā śobhā kare*

**Перевод:**

**Navadvīpa Dhāma shines eternally within Gauḍa Maṇḍala, and is served by Gaṅgā Devī.**

### Text 4

*e gauḍa-maṇḍala eka viṁśati yojana*  
*madhya-bhāge gaṅgā-devī rahe anukṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Gauḍa Maṇḍala is one hundred and sixty-eight miles in circumference. Gaṅgā Devī continuously flows throughout it.**

### Text 5

*śata-dala padmamaya maṇḍala ākāra*  
*madhya-bhāge navadvīpa ati śobhā tāra*

**Перевод:**

**Gauḍa Maṇḍala is a one-hundred-petalled lotus, and at its centre is the extremely beautiful Navadvīpa.**

### Text 6

*pañca-krośa haya tāra keśara ādhāra*  
*parimala-pūrṇa puṣpa yojana catvāra*

**Перевод:**

**In circumference, its whorl is ten miles in circumference, and its fragrant bloom is thirty-two miles.**

### Text 7

*bāhira pāpaḍi tāra śata-dala haya*  
*ekādhika yojana viṁśati vistāraya*

**Перевод:**

**Its outer petals form a one-hundred-petalled lotus that extends for one hundred and sixty-eight miles.**

### Text 8

*maṇḍala paridhi haya sei parimāṇa*  
*yojana saptaka vyāsa śāstrera vidhāna*

**Перевод:**

**The circumference of Gauḍa Maṇḍala is that size. The scriptures state that its diameter is fifty-six miles.**

### Text 9

*vyāsārddha pramāṇa sārddha tṛtīya yojana*  
*madhya-bindu haite tāra ha-ibe gaṇana*

**Перевод:**

**Its radius, measured from its centre point, is twenty-eight miles.**

### Text 10

*madhya-bindu navadvīpa-dhāma madhya-sthala*  
*yoga-pīṭha haya tāhā cinmaya vimala*

**Перевод:**

**The centre of Navadvīpa Dhāma is the pure, spiritual Yoga Pīṭha.**

### Text 11

*cintāmaṇi-rūpa haya ei gauḍa-maṇḍala*  
*cid-ānandamaya-dhāma cinmaya sakala*

**Перевод:**

**Śrī Gauḍa Maṇḍala is made of wish-fulfilling gemstone. It is spiritual and joyful: everything within it is made of spiritual energy.**

### Text 12

*jala bhūmi vṛkṣa ādi sakali cinmaya*  
*sadā vidyamāna tathā kṛṣṇa-śakti-traya*

**Перевод:**

**The water, land, trees, and so on, are all spiritual. Kṛṣṇa’s three energies exist there eternally.**

### Text 13

*svarūpa-śaktira sei sandhinī-prabhāva*  
*tāra pariṇati ei dhāmera svabhāva*

**Перевод:**

**The Dhāma is a manifestation of the** ***sandhinī*** **aspect of the Lord’s personal energy.**

### Text 14

*prabhu-līlā-pīṭha-rūpe dhāma nitya haya*  
*acintya śaktira kārya prāpañcika naya*

**Перевод:**

**The Dhāma is the eternal site of the Lord’s Pastimes. It is a manifestation of the Lord’s inconceivable energy; it is not material.**

### Text 15

*tabe ye e dhāme dekhe prapañcera sama*  
*baddha-jīve tāhe haya avidyā-vibhrama*

**Перевод:**

**Still, the conditioned souls are bewildered by illusion and see the Dhāma to be material.**

### Text 16

*meghācchanna cakṣu dekhe sūrya ācchādita*  
*divākara nāhi kabhu haya meghāvṛta*

**Перевод:**

**Eyes covered by clouds see that the sun is covered, but the sun is never actually covered by clouds.**

### Text 17

*sei rūpa e gauḍa-maṇḍala cid-ākāra*  
*prāpañcika jana dekhe jaḍera vikāra*

**Перевод:**

**Similarly, worldly people see the spiritual form of Śrī Gauḍa Maṇḍala to be a formation of matter.**

### Text 18

*nityānanda-kṛpā yāṅra prati kabhu haya*  
*se dekhe ānanda dhāma sarvatra cinmaya*

**Перевод:**

**But once Nityānanda blesses someone, they see the joyful, spiritual Dhāma everywhere.**

### Text 19

*gaṅgā yamunādi tathā sadā vidyamāna*  
*sapta-purī prayāgādi āchhe sthāne sthāna*

**Перевод:**

**The Gaṅgā, Yamunā, and other holy rivers exist there eternally. The seven holy cities, Prayāga, and other holy places are present throughout the Dhāma.**

### Text 20

*sākṣāt vaikuṇṭha-tattva e gauḍa-maṇḍala*  
*bhāgyavān jīva tāhā dekhe niramala*

**Перевод:**

**Śrī Gauḍa Maṇḍala is Vaikuṇṭha itself; fortunate souls see this clearly.**

### Text 21-22

*svarūpa-śaktira chāyā māyā bali’ yāre*  
*prabhura ājñāya nija prabhāva vistāre*

*bahirmukha jīva-cakṣu kare āvaraṇa*  
*cid-dhāma-prabhāva sabe nā pāya darśana*

**Перевод:**

**On the order of the Lord, Māyā, the shadow of His personal energy, extends her influence and covers the eyes of the averse souls. Thus, they do not see the glory of the Lord’s spiritual abode.**

### Text 23

*e gauḍa-maṇḍale yāṅra vāsa nirantara*  
*baḍa bhāgyavān sei saṁsāra bhitara*

**Перевод:**

**Souls who always reside in Śrī Gauḍa Maṇḍala are the most fortunate souls in this world.**

### Text 24

*deva-gaṇa svarge thāki’ dekhe sei jane*  
*catur-bhuja śyāma-kānti apūrva gaṭhane*

**Перевод:**

**The gods living in heaven see that they have beautiful four-armed forms with a cloud-like lustre.**

### Text 25

*ṣola-krośa navadvīpa-dhāma-vāsī yata*  
*gaura-kānti sadā nāma-saṅkīrtane rata*

**Перевод:**

**The residents of the thirty-two miles of Śrī Navadvīpa Dhāma have a golden lustre and are always engaged in chanting the Name.**

### Text 26

*brahmā ādi deva-gaṇe antarīkṣa haite*  
*navadvīpa-vāsī-gaṇe pūje nānā-mate*

**Перевод:**

**From the heavens, Brahmā and the other gods worship the residents of Navadvīpa in various ways.**

### Text 27-28

*brahmā bale, “kabe mora hena bhāgya habe*  
*navadvīpe tṛṇa kalevara pāba yabe*

*śrī-gaura-caraṇa-sevā kare yata jana*  
*tā sabāra pada-reṇu labhiba takhana*

**Перевод:**

**Brahmā says, “When will I attain the fortune of becoming a blade of grass in Navadvīpa and receiving the foot dust of the servants of Śrī Gaura’s feet?**

### Text 29

*hāya more gaura-candra vañcanā kariyā*  
*brahmāṇḍera adhipati rākhila kariyā*

**Перевод:**

**“Alas! Gaura-candra deceived me and made me the emperor of the universe.**

### Text 30

*kabe mora karma-granthi ha-ibe chedana*  
*abhimāna tyaji’ mora śuddha habe mana*

**Перевод:**

**“When will my entanglement in karma be cut away? When will my heart give up its pride and become pure?**

### Text 31

*adhikāra buddhi mora kabe habe kṣaya*  
*śuddha-dāsa haye pāba gaura padāśraya”*

**Перевод:**

**“When will my mentality of being a master be destroyed? When will I become a pure servant and attain the shelter of Śrī Gaura’s feet?”**

### Text 32

*deva-gaṇa ṛṣi-gaṇa rudra-gaṇa yata*  
*sthāne sthāne navadvīpe vaise avirata*

**Перевод:**

**The gods, sages, and Rudras live throughout Navadvīpa eternally.**

### Text 33

*cira-kāla tapa kari’ jīvana kāṭāya*  
*tabu nityānanda-kṛpā se sabe nā pāya*

**Перевод:**

**They spend their lives performing austerities for long periods, but they do not receive the mercy of Nityānanda.**

### Text 34-35

*deha-buddhi yata dina nāhi yāya dūre*  
*yata dina dainya-bhāva mane nāhi sphūre*

*tata dina śrī-gaura-nitāi-kṛpā-dhana*  
*brahmā-śiva nāhi pāya kariyā yatana*

**Перевод:**

**As long as their identification with the body does not go away and humility does not appear within their hearts, even Brahmā and Śiva do not attain the wealth of Gaura and Nitāi’s mercy, although they strive for it.**

### Text 36

*ei saba kathā āge ha-ibe prakāśa*  
*yatna kari’ śuna bhāi kariyā viśvāsa*

**Перевод:**

**All these points will be described ahead. Brother! Faithfully and carefully listen.**

### Text 37

*ei saba viṣaye bhāi tarka parihara*  
*tarka se apārtha ati amaṅgala-kara*

**Перевод:**

**Brother! Give up argument in these matters—inappropriate argument is very inauspicious.**

### Text 38

*śrī-caitanya-līlā haya gabhīra sāgara*  
*mocā-kholā-rūpa tarka tathāya phāṅpara*

**Перевод:**

**The Pastimes of Śrī Caitanya are a deep ocean. Argument over them, like a banana flower’s husk, is simply troublesome.**

### Text 39

*tarka kari’ e saṁsāra tarite ye cāya*  
*viphala tāhāra ceṣṭā kichhui nā pāya*

**Перевод:**

**The endeavour of those who want to cross over this world by argument is fruitless. Such persons do not attain anything.**

### Text 40

*tarke jalāñjali diyā sādhu śāstra dhare*  
*acire caitanya-lābha sei jana kare*

**Перевод:**

**Those who forgo argument and follow the sādhus and scriptures quickly attain Śrī Caitanya.**

### Text 41

*śruti smṛti tantra śāstra avirata gāya*  
*nadīyā-māhātmya nityānandera ājñāya*

**Перевод:**

**The** ***śrutis, smṛtis*****, and** ***tantras*** **always sing the glories of Nadīyā on the order of Nityānanda Prabhu.**

### Text 42

*sei saba śāstra paḍa sādhu-vākya māna*  
*tabe ta’ ha-ibe tava navadvīpa-jñāna*

**Перевод:**

**Read all these scriptures and honour the words of the** ***sādhus*****; then you will understand Navadvīpa.**

### Text 43

*kali-kāle tīrtha saba atyanta durbala*  
*navadvīpa tīrtha mātra parama prabala*

**Перевод:**

**During the Age of Kali, all the holy places are very weak. Only Navadvīpa is greatly powerful.**

### Text 44

*prabhura icchāya sei tīrtha bahu dina*  
*aprakaṭa mahimā āchhila sphūrti-hīna*

**Перевод:**

**By the will of the Lord, this holy place was unmanifest for a long time, and its greatness was not revealed.**

### Text 45

*kalira prabhāva yabe atyanta bāḍila*  
*anya tīrtha svabhāvataḥ nisteja ha-ila*

**Перевод:**

**When the influence of Kali strongly increased, the other holy places naturally became powerless.**

### Text 46

*jīvera maṅgala lāgi’ puruṣa-pradhāna*  
*mane mane cintā kari’ karila vidhāna*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord reflected on this and made an arrangement for the welfare of all souls.**

### Text 47

*“pīḍā bujhi’ vaidyarāja auṣadha khāoyāya*  
*kaṭhina auṣadha deya kaṭhina pīḍāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Understanding a patient’s disease, a good doctor prescribes medicine. For a strong disease, he gives strong medicine.**

### Text 48

*ebe kali ghora haila roga ha-ila bhārī*  
*kaṭhina auṣadha vinā nivārite nāri*

**Перевод:**

**“The Age of Kali has become severe, and the disease of the souls has become serious. Without strong medicine, I cannot check it.**

### Text 49-50

*atiśaya gopane rākhinu yei dhāma*  
*atiśaya gopane rākhinu yei nāma*

*atiśaya gopane rākhinu yei rūpa*  
*prakāśa nā kaile jīva taribe ki-rūpa*

**Перевод:**

**“If I do not reveal the Abode, Name, and Form that I have kept completely hidden, then how will the souls be delivered?**

### Text 51

*jīva ta āmāra dāsa āmi tāra prabhu*  
*āmi na tārile sei nā taribe kabhu”*

**Перевод:**

**“The souls are My servants, and I am their master. If I do not deliver them, then they will never be delivered.”**

### Text 52

*ei bali’ śrī-caitanya ha-ila prakāśa*  
*nija nāma nija dhāma laye nija dāsa*

**Перевод:**

**Thinking thus, Śrī Caitanya appeared with His Name, His abode, and His servants.**

### Text 53

*prabhura pratijñā ei haya sarva-kāla*  
*“tāriba sakala jīva ghucāba jañjāla*

**Перевод:**

**This promise of the Lord is eternal: “I will deliver all souls and end their suffering.**

### Text 54

*brahmāra durlabha dhana bilāba saṁsāre*  
*pātrāpātra nā vāchiba ei avatāre*

**Перевод:**

**“In this appearance, I will distribute throughout the world wealth that is difficult for even Brahmā to attain, and I will not consider who is fit or unfit.**

### Text 55

*dekhiba ki-rūpe kali jīve kare nāśa*  
*navadvīpa-dhāma āmi kariba prakāśa*

**Перевод:**

**“I will see how Kali devastates the souls, and I will reveal Navadvīpa Dhāma.**

### Text 56

*sei dhāme kalira bhāṅgiba viṣa-dāta*  
*kīrtana kariyā jīve kari’ ātma-sātha*

**Перевод:**

**“In the Dhāma, I will break Kali’s poisonous fangs, perform** ***kīrtana*****, and collect the souls.**

### Text 57

*yata-dūra mama nāma ha-ibe kīrtana*  
*tata-dūra ha-ibe ta kalira damana”*

**Перевод:**

**“Kali will be subdued to the extent that My Name is chanted.”**

### Text 58

*ei bali’ gaurahari kalira sandhyāya*  
*prakāśila navadvīpa svakīya māyāya*

**Перевод:**

**Deciding this, Gaurahari revealed Navadvīpa by His own power at the beginning of Kali-yuga.**

### Text 59

*chāyā samvariyā nitya svarūpa-vilāsa*  
*gaura-candra gauḍa-bhūme karila prakāśa*

**Перевод:**

**Gaura-candra held back Māyā’s darkness and revealed His eternal Pastimes in the land of Gauḍa.**

### Text 60-61

*emana dayālu prabhu ye jana nā bhaje*  
*emana acintya-dhāma yei jana tyaje*

*ei kali-kāle tāra samabhāgya-hīna*  
*nā dekhi jagate āra śocanīya dīna*

**Перевод:**

**I do not see anyone in the world during the Age of Kali as unfortunate, pitiable, and fallen as someone who does not serve such a merciful Lord and rejects such an inconceivable abode.**

### Text 62

*ataeva chāḍi’ bhāi anya vāñchā rati*  
*navadvīpa-dhāme mātra hao ekamati*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, brother! Give up all other desires and attachments, and devote yourself exclusively to Navadvīpa Dhāma.**

### Text 63

*jāhnavī-nitāi-pada-chāyā yāra āśa*  
*se bhaktivinoda kare e tattva prakāśa*

**Перевод:**

**Bhaktivinoda, whose aspiration is the shade of Jāhnavā and Nitāi’s feet, reveals the glories of Nadīyā.**

## Chapter Three – Instructions for Circumambulating the Dhāma

### Text 1

*jaya jaya navadvīpa-candra śacī-suta*  
*jaya jaya nityānanda-rāya avadhūta*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to the son of Śacī, the moon of Navadvīpa! All glory to the** ***avadhūta*** **Nityānanda Rāya!**

### Text 2

*jaya jaya śrī-advaita prabhu mahāśaya*  
*gadādhara śrīvāsa paṇḍita jaya jaya*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to the worshippable Śrī Advaita Prabhu! All glory to Gadādhara Paṇḍita and Śrīvāsa Paṇḍita!**

### Text 3

*jaya jaya navadvīpa-dhāma sarva-dhāma-sāra*  
*yei dhāma-saha gauracandra avatāra*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to Navadvīpa Dhāma, the best of all abodes, the abode with which Gauracandra appeared!**

### Text 4

*ṣola-krośa navadvīpa madhye yāhā yāhā*  
*varṇiba ekhana bhakta-gaṇa śuna tāhā*

**Перевод:**

**Devotees, listen! I will now describe everything within the thirty-two miles of Navadvīpa.**

### Text 5

*ṣola-krośa madhye navadvīpera pramāṇa*  
*ṣoḍaśa pravāha tathā sadā vidyamāna*

**Перевод:**

**Sixteen rivers exist eternally within the thirty-two miles of Navadvīpa.**

### Text 6

*mūla-gaṅgā pūrva-tīre dvīpa-catuṣṭaya*  
*tāṅhāra paścime sadā pañca-dvīpa raya*

**Перевод:**

**Four islands are on the east bank of the original Gaṅgā, and five are on the west bank.**

### Text 7

*svardhunī pravāha saba beḍi’ dvīpa-gaṇe*  
*navadvīpa-dhāme śobhā deya anukṣaṇe*

**Перевод:**

**These heavenly rivers flow throughout the islands and always beautify Navadvīpa Dhāma.**

### Text 8

*madhye mūla gaṅgā-devī rahe anukṣaṇa*  
*apara pravāhe anya puṇya-nadī-gaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Gaṅgā Devī’s original stream always flows through the centre of the Dhāma; the other holy rivers flow in separate streams.**

### Text 9

*gaṅgāra nikaṭa vahe yamunā sundarī*  
*anya dhārā madhye sarasvatī vīdyādharī*

**Перевод:**

**The beautiful Yamunā flows near the Gaṅgā. The Sarasvatī, the carrier of knowledge, flows in another channel.**

### Text 10

*tāmraparṇī kṛtamālā brahmaputra-traya*  
*yamunāra pūrva-bhāge dīrgha-dhārāmaya*

**Перевод:**

**East of the Yamunā are the three long rivers: the Tāmraparṇī, Kṛtamālā, and Brahmaputra.**

### Text 11

*sarayū narmadā sindhu kāverī gomatī*  
*prasthe vahe godāvarī saha drutagati*

**Перевод:**

**The Sarayū, Narmadā, Sindhu, Kāverī, Gomatī, and Godāvarī flow swiftly on the west side.**

### Text 12

*ei saba dhārā paraspara kari’ chheda*  
*eka navadvīpe nava-vidha kare bheda*

**Перевод:**

**All these rivers cross each other and divide Navadvīpa into nine parts.**

### Text 13

*prabhura icchāya kabhu dhārā śuṣka haya*  
*punaḥ icchā haile dhārā haya jalamaya*

**Перевод:**

**By the will of the Lord, these rivers sometimes go dry, and when the Lord wills it, they again fill with water.**

### Text 14

*prabhura icchāya kabhu ḍube kona sthāna*  
*prabhura icchāya punaḥ deya ta darśana*

**Перевод:**

**By the will of the Lord, they sometimes submerge portions of land and then again reveal them.**

### Text 15

*niravadhi ei rūpa dhāma līlā kare*  
*bhāgyavān jana prati sarva-kāla sphure*

**Перевод:**

**The Dhāma performs Pastimes eternally in this way and always appears before fortunate souls.**

### Text 16

*utkaṭa vāsanā yadi bhakta-hṛde haya*  
*sarva-dvīpa sarva-dhārā darśana milaya*

**Перевод:**

**When intense desire arises in the heart of a devotee, all the islands and rivers in the Dhāma reveal themselves.**

### Text 17

*kabhu svapne kabhu dhyāne kabhu dṛṣṭi-yoge*  
*dhāmera darśana pāya bhaktira saṁyoge*

**Перевод:**

**Sometimes in dreams, sometimes in meditation, and sometimes in their vision, devotees see the Dhāma through their devotion.**

### Text 18

*gaṅgā-yamunāra yoge yei dvīpa raya*  
*antardvīpa tāra nāma sarva-śāstre kaya*

**Перевод:**

**All the scriptures call the island at the confluence of the Gaṅgā and Yamunā Antardvīpa.**

### Text 19

*antardvīpa madhye āchhe pīṭha māyāpura*  
*yathāya janmila prabhu caitanya-ṭhākura*

**Перевод:**

**Within Antardvīpa is Māyāpura, where Lord Caitanya appeared.**

### Text 20

*golokera antavartī yei mahāvana*  
*māyāpura navadvīpe jāna bhakta-gaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Devotees know Mahāvana in Goloka to be Māyāpura in Navadvīpa.**

### Text 21

*śvetadvīpa vaikuṇṭha goloka vṛndāvana*  
*navadvīpe saba tattva āchhe sarva-kṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Śvetadvīpa, Vaikuṇṭha, and Goloka Vṛndāvana are always present in Navadvīpa.**

### Text 22-23

*ayodhyā mathurā māyā kāśī kāñcī āra*  
*avantī dvārakā sei purī sapta sāra*

*navadvīpe se samasta nija nija sthāne*  
*nitya vidyamāna gauracandrera vidhāne*

**Перевод:**

**The seven great cities—Ayodhyā, Mathurā, Māyā, Kāśī, Kāñcī, Avantī, and Dvārakā—exist eternally in Navadvīpa in their respective places, by the arrangement of Gauracandra.**

### Text 24

*gaṅgā-dvāra māyāra svarūpa māyāpuraa*  
*yāhāra māhātmya śāstre āchhaye pracura*

**Перевод:**

**The true form of the city Māyā, the gateway of the Gaṅgā, is Māyāpura, whose glories are stated repeatedly throughout the scriptures.**

### Text 25

*sei māyāpure ye yāya eka-bāra*  
*anāyāse haya sei jaḍa-māyā pāra*

**Перевод:**

**Those who once go to Māyāpura easily cross over material illusion.**

### Text 26

*māyāpurae bhramile māyāra adhikāra*  
*dūre yāya, janma kabhu nahe āra-bāra*

**Перевод:**

**If a soul wanders in Māyāpura, then the influence of Māyā goes away, and they do not take birth again.**

### Text 27

*māyāpura uttara sīmantadvīpa haya*  
*parikramā vidhi sādhu-śāstra sadā kaya*

**Перевод:**

**North of Māyāpura is Sīmantadvīpa. The** ***sādhus*** **and scriptures always explain the procedure for circumambulating the Dhāma.**

### Text 28

*antardvīpe śrī-māyāpura kariyā darśana*  
*śrī-sīmantadvīpe cala vijña bhakta-jana*

**Перевод:**

**O wise devotees, visit Śrī Māyāpura in Antardvīpa and then go to Sīmantadvīpa.**

### Text 29

*godrumākhya-dvīpa haya māyāra dakṣiṇe*  
*tāhā bhrami’ chala madhyadvīpa hṛṣṭa-mane*

**Перевод:**

**Godrumadvīpa is south of Māyāpura. Walk there and then joyfully go to Madhyadvīpa.**

### Text 30

*ei cāri-dvīpa jāhnavīra pūrva-tīre*  
*dekhiyā jāhnavī pāra hao dhīre dhīre*

**Перевод:**

**Visit these four islands on the east bank of the Gaṅgā and then slowly cross the river.**

### Text 31

*koladvīpa anāyāse kariyā bhramaṇa*  
*ṛtudvīpa śobhā tabe kara daraśana*

**Перевод:**

**Peacefully walk through Koladvīpa and then see the beauty of Ṛtudvīpa.**

### Text 32

*tārapara jahnudvīpa parama sundara*  
*dekhi’ modadrumadvīpe chala vijña-vara*

**Перевод:**

**O best of the wise, thereafter, see the wonderful beauty of Jahnudvīpa and then go to Modadrumadvīpa.**

### Text 33

*rudradvīpa dekha punaḥ gaṅgā haye pāra*  
*bhrami’ māyāpura bhakta cala āra bāra*

**Перевод:**

**O devotees, see Rudradvīpa, cross the Gaṅgā again, and then visit Māyāpura once more.**

### Text 34

*tathāya śrī-jagannātha-śacīra mandire*  
*prabhura darśane praveśaha dhīre dhīre*

**Перевод:**

**There, slowly enter the abode of Śrī Jagannātha and Śacī to see the Lord.**

### Text 35

*sarva-kāle ei rūpa parikramā haya*  
*jīvera ananta sukha-prāptira ālaya*

**Перевод:**

**Circumambulation is always done in this way and is a source of unlimited joy for the soul.**

### Text 36

*viśeṣataḥ mākarī-saptamī-tithi gate*  
*phālgunī pūrṇimāvadhi śreṣṭha sarva-mate*

**Перевод:**

**Everyone concurs that it is best to start on Mākarī Saptamī and end on Phālgunī Pūrṇimā.**

### Text 37-38

*parikramā samādhiyā yei mahājana*  
*janma-dine māyāpura karena darśana*

*nitāi gaurāṅga tāre kṛpā vitariyā*  
*bhakti adhikārī kare pada-chhāyā diyā*

**Перевод:**

**Nitāi and Gaurāṅga bless the great souls who complete their circumambulation and visit Māyāpura on the birthday of the Lord. They make them qualified for devotion and give them the shade of Their feet.**

### Text 39

*saṁkṣepe kahinu parikramā vivaraṇa*  
*vistāriyā bali ebe karaha śravaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**I have briefly described circumambulating the Dhāma. Now I will elaborate. Please listen.**

### Text 40

*yei jana bhrame ekaviṁśati yojana*  
*achire labhaye sei gaura-prema-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**Those who traverse the one hundred and sixty-eight miles of Śrī Gauḍa Maṇḍala quickly attain the wealth of divine love for Gaura.**

### Text 41

*jāhnavā-nitāi-pada-chāyā yāra āśa*  
*e bhaktivinoda kare e tattva prakāśa*

**Перевод:**

**Bhaktivinoda, whose aspiration is the shade of Jāhnavā and Nitāi’s feet, reveals the glories of Nadīyā.**

## Chapter Four – Śrī Jīva Hears the Glories of the Dhāma

### Text 1

*jaya jaya navadvīpa-candra śacī-suta*  
*jaya jaya nityānanda-rāya avadhūta*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to the son of Śacī, the moon of Navadvīpa! All glory to the** ***avadhūta*** **Nityānanda Rāy!**

### Text 2

*jaya jaya navadvīpa sarva-dhāma-sāra*  
*yathāya ha-ila śrī-caitanya-avatāra*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to the best of all abodes, Navadvīpa, where Śrī Caitanya appeared!**

### Text 3

*sarva-tīrthe vāsa kari’ yei phala pāi*  
*navadvīpe labhi tāhā eka-dine bhāi*

**Перевод:**

**Brother, souls attain in Navadvīpa in one day the result that others attain by residing in all the holy places.**

### Text 4

*sei navadvīpa parikramā vivaraṇa*  
*śāstra ālociyā gāi śuna sādhu-jana*

**Перевод:**

**O** ***sādhus*****, listen as I discuss the scriptures and sing about circumambulating Navadvīpa.**

### Text 5

*śāstrera likhana āra vaiṣṇava-vacana*  
*prabhu-ājñā ei tina mama prāṇa-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**The writing of the scriptures, the words of the devotees, and the order of the Lord are my life and wealth.**

### Text 6

*e tine āśraya kari’ kariba varṇana*  
*nadīyā-bhramaṇa-vidhi śuna sarva-jana*

**Перевод:**

**Having taken shelter of them, I will describe the procedure for circumambulating Nadia. Everyone listen.**

### Text 7

*śrī-jīva-gosvāmī yabe chāḍilena ghara*  
*“nadīyā nadīyā” bali’ vyākula antara*

**Перевод:**

**When Śrī Jīva Gosvāmī left home, he was eager at heart, calling out, “Nadīyā! Nadīyā!”**

### Text 8

*candradvīpa chāḍi’ teṅha yata patha cale*  
*bhāse dui cakṣu tāṅra nayanera jale*

**Перевод:**

**As he left Candradvīpa and walked down the road, his eyes filled with tears.**

### Text 9-10

*“hā gaurāṅga nityānanda jīvera jīvana*  
*kabe more kṛpā kari’ dibe daraśana*

*hā hā navadvīpa-dhāma sarva-dhāma-sāra*  
*kabe vā dekhiba āmi” bale bāra-bāra*

**Перевод:**

**Again and again, he called out, “O Gaurāṅga! O Nityānanda! O Life of the soul! When will You mercifully appear before me? O Navadvīpa Dhāma, best of all abodes! When will I see you?”**

### Text 11

*kaiśora vayasa jīva sundara gaṭhana*  
*vairāgyera parākāṣṭhā apūrva darśana*

**Перевод:**

**Jīva was young and had a beautiful figure. His extreme detachment was astonishing to see.**

### Text 12

*caliyā caliyā kata-dine mahāśaya*  
*navadvīpe uttarila sadā premamaya*

**Перевод:**

**After walking for many days, always filled with divine love, Jīva arrived in Navadvīpa.**

### Text 13

*dūra haite navadvīpa kari’ daraśana*  
*daṇḍavat haye paḍe prāya acetana*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing Navadvīpa from a distance, he bowed and fell almost unconscious.**

### Text 14

*kata-kṣaṇa pare nija citta kari’ sthira*  
*praveśila navadvīpe pulaka śarīra*

**Перевод:**

**Eventually he steadied his heart and entered Navadvīpa, the hair on his body standing on end.**

### Text 15

*bārakoṇā ghāṭe āsi’ jijñāse sabāre*  
*“kothā prabhu nityānanda dekhāo āmāre” \[15\]*

**Перевод:**

**He came to Bārakoṇā Ghāṭa and asked everyone, “Where is Nityānanda Prabhu? Please show me.”**

### Text 16

*śrī-jīvera bhāva dekhi’ kona mahājana*  
*prabhu nityānanda yathā laya tata-kṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing Śrī Jīva’s ecstasy, a great soul immediately brought him to Nityānanda Prabhu.**

### Text 17

*hethā prabhu nityānanda aṭṭa aṭṭa hāsi’*  
*śrī-jīva āsibe bali’ antare ullāsī*

**Перевод:**

**Knowing that Śrī Jīva would come, Nityānanda Prabhu was delighted at heart and roaring with laughter.**

### Text 18

*ājñā dila dāsa-gaṇe śrī-jīve ānite*  
*aneka vaiṣṇava yāya jīve sambodhite*

**Перевод:**

**He ordered His servants to bring Śrī Jīva, and many devotees went to call him.**

### Text 19

*sāttvika-vikāra-pūrṇa jīvera śarīra*  
*dekhi’ jīva bali’ sabe karilena sthira*

**Перевод:**

**Upon seeing the body of Jīva filled with divine ecstasy, Nityānanda Prabhu’s servants identified him.**

### Text 20

*keha keha āge giyā mahāprema-bhare*  
*nityānanda-prabhu-ājñā vijñāpana kare*

**Перевод:**

**Some of them, filled with intense divine love, went forward and conveyed the order of Nityānanda Prabhu to Jīva.**

### Text 21

*prabhu-nityānanda-nāma kariyā śravaṇa*  
*dharaṇīte paḍe jīva haye acetana*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the Name of Nityānanda Prabhu, Jīva fell to the ground unconscious.**

### Text 22

*kṣaṇeka uṭhiyā bale, “baḍa bhāgya mama*  
*prabhu-nityānanda-kṛpā pāila adhama”*

**Перевод:**

**Immediately, he arose and said, “It is my great fortune that I, a fallen soul, have received the mercy of Nityānanda Prabhu.”**

### Text 23

*se saba vaiṣṇava-gaṇe daṇḍavat haye*  
*praṇāma karaye jīva praphulla hṛdaye*

**Перевод:**

**Jīva prostrated himself before all the devotees with a jubilant heart.**

### Text 24

*bale, “tumi sabe more ha-ile sadaya*  
*nityānanda-pada pāi sarva-śāstre kaya”*

**Перевод:**

**He said, “All the scriptures say that if you all are merciful to me, then I will attain the feet of Nityānanda Prabhu.”**

### Text 25

*jīvera saubhāgya heri’ kateka vaiṣṇava*  
*caraṇera dhūli laya kariyā utsava*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing Jīva’s fortune, some of the devotees joyously took dust from his feet.**

### Text 26

*sabe meli’ jīve laya nityānanda yathā*  
*vaiṣṇava-veṣṭita prabhu kahe kṛṣṇa-kathā*

**Перевод:**

**They all joined together and took Jīva to Nityānanda Prabhu, where He was speaking about Kṛṣṇa, surrounded by devotees.**

### Text 27

*prabhu nityānandera dekhiyā divya-rūpa*  
*jīvera śarīra haya bhāva aparūpa*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the divine form of Nityānanda Prabhu, extraordinary ecstasy arose in Jīva’s body.**

### Text 28

*“ki apūrva-rūpa āja herinu” baliyā*  
*paḍila dharaṇī-tale acetana haiyā*

**Перевод:**

**Thinking, “What a wonderful form I have seen today!”, Jīva fell to the ground unconscious.**

### Text 29

*mahākṛpā-vaśe prabhu nityānanda-rāya*  
*jīve uṭhāiyā laya āpanāra pāya*

**Перевод:**

**Moved by intense compassion, Nityānanda Prabhu raised Jīva to His feet.**

### Text 30

*vyasta haye śrī-jīva-gosvāmī dāṅḍāila*  
*kara yuḍi’ nityānande kahite lāgila*

**Перевод:**

**Śrī Jīva Gosvāmī quickly stood up, joined his palms, and began to speak to Nityānanda.**

### Text 31

*“viśva-rūpa viśva-dhāma tumi balarāma*  
*āmi jīva kibā jāni tava guṇa-grāma*

**Перевод:**

**“You are Balarāma, the original form and shelter of the world. I am just a soul. How can I understand Your glories?**

### Text 32

*tumi mora prabhu nitya āmi tava dāsa*  
*tomāra caraṇa-chāyā eka-mātra āśa*

**Перевод:**

**“You are my Master, and I am Your eternal servant. The shade of Your feet is my one and only aspiration.**

### Text 33

*tumi yāre kara dayā sei anāyāse*  
*śrī-caitanya-pada pāya prema-jale bhāse*

**Перевод:**

**“Those whom you bless easily attain the feet of Śrī Caitanya and float in the waters of divine love.**

### Text 34

*tomāra karuṇā vinā gaura nāhi pāya*  
*śata janma bhaje yadi gaurāṅga hiyāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Without Your grace, souls do not attain Śrī Gaura, even if they serve Him within their heart for one hundred births.**

### Text 35

*gaura daṇḍa kare yadi tumi rakṣā kara*  
*tumi yāre daṇḍa kara gaura tāra para*

**Перевод:**

**“If Gaura punishes someone, You protect them, and Gaura rejects whomever You punish.**

### Text 36

*ataeva prabhu tava caraṇa-kamale*  
*la-inu śaraṇa āmi sukṛtira bale*

**Перевод:**

**“Therefore, O Lord, by my good fortune, I have taken shelter at Your lotus feet.**

### Text 37

*tumi kṛpā kari’ more deha anumati*  
*śrī-gaura-darśane pāi gaure ha-u rati*

**Перевод:**

**“O Lord, mercifully give Your consent that I may see Śrī Gaura and develop devotion to Him.**

### Text 38

*yabe rāmakeli-grāme śrī-gaurāṅga-rāya*  
*āmāra pitṛvya-dvaye la-ilena pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“When Śrī Gaurāṅga Rāya was in the village of Rāmakeli, my uncles took shelter at His feet.**

### Text 39

*sei kāle śiśu āmi sajala nayane*  
*herilāma gaura-rūpa sadā jāge mane*

**Перевод:**

**“At that time, I was a child. With tearful eyes, I saw Śrī Gaura’s form, and still now that form constantly arises in my heart.**

### Text 40

*śrī-gaurāṅga-pade paḍi’ karinu praṇati*  
*śrī-aṅga sparśiyā sukha pāilāma ati*

**Перевод:**

**“Bowing at Śrī Gaurāṅga’s feet, I offered my obeisance. Touching His divine form, I felt great joy.**

### Text 41

*sei kāle gaura more kahilā vacana*  
*‘ohe jīva kara tumi śāstra adhyayana*

**Перевод:**

**“At that time, Gaura said to me, ‘O Jīva, study the scriptures.**

### Text 42

*adhyayana samāpiyā navadvīpe chala*  
*nityānanda śrī-caraṇe pāibe sakala’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Complete your study and then go to Navadvīpa. There, at the holy feet of Nityānanda, you will attain everything.’**

### Text 43

*sei ājñā śire dhari’ āmi akiñcana*  
*yathā sādhya vidyā kariyāchhi upārjana*

**Перевод:**

**“Holding this order on my head, I, a poor soul, have learned as much as possible.**

### Text 44

*candradvīpe paḍilāma sāhityādi yata*  
*vedānta ācārya nāhi pāi manomata*

**Перевод:**

**“I studied literature and other subjects in Candradvīpa, but I did not find an expert teacher of** ***Vedānta*****.**

### Text 45

*prabhu ājñā dila more vedānta paḍite*  
*vedānta-sammata kṛṣṇa-bhakti prakāśite*

**Перевод:**

**“The Lord ordered me to study** ***Vedānta*** **and prove that devotion to Kṛṣṇa is its purport.**

### Text 46

*āilāma navadvīpe tomāra caraṇe*  
*yei rūpa ājñā haya kari ācaraṇe*

**Перевод:**

**“So, I have come to Your feet in Navadvīpa, and I will do whatever You order me.**

### Text 47

*ājñā haya yāi kṣetre prabhura caraṇe*  
*vedānta paḍiba sārva-bhaumera sadane”*

**Перевод:**

**“If You order me, I will go to the feet of the Lord in Puruṣottama Kṣetra and study** ***Vedānta*** **at the school of Sārvabhauma Bhattācārya.”**

### Text 48

*jīvera madhura vākye nityānanda-rāya*  
*jīve kole kari’ kāṅde dhairya nāhi pāya*

**Перевод:**

**Moved by Jīva’s sweet words, Nityānanda Rāya embraced Jīva and cried, unable to restrain Himself.**

### Text 49

*bale, “śuna ohe jīva nigūḍha vacana*  
*sarva-tattva avagata rūpa sanātana*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda said, “O Jīva, listen to My confidential words. Rūpa and Sanātana know everything.**

### Text 50

*prabhu more ājñā dila balite tomāya*  
*‘kṣetre nāhi yāo tumi nā raha hethāya’*

**Перевод:**

**“The Lord ordered Me to tell you, ‘Do not go to Puruṣottama Kṣetra or stay here.’**

### Text 51

*tumi āra rūpa sanātana dui bhāi*  
*prabhura ekānta dāsa jānena sabai*

**Перевод:**

**“Everyone knows that you and the brothers Rūpa and Sanātana are exclusive servants of the Lord.**

### Text 52-53

*tomā prati ājñā ei vārāṇasī giyā*  
*vācaspati nikaṭete vedānta paḍiyā*

*ekebāre yāha tathā haite vṛndāvana*  
*tathā kṛpā karibena rūpa sanātana*

**Перевод:**

**“The Lord’s order to you is that you go to Vārāṇasī, study** ***Vedānta*** **with Madhusūdana Vācaspati, and then go straight to Vṛndāvana from there. Rūpa and Sanātana will bless you in Vṛndāvana.**

### Text 54

*rūpera anuga haye yugala-bhajana*  
*kara tathā vedāntādi śāstra-ālāpana*

**Перевод:**

**“Serve the Divine Couple as a follower of Śrī Rūpa, and discuss** ***Vedānta*** **and other scriptures there.**

### Text 55

*bhāgavata śāstra haya sarva-śāstra-sāra*  
*vedānta-sūtrera bhāṣya karaha pracāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Preach that** ***Śrīmad Bhāgavatam*** **is the best of all the scriptures and the natural commentary on the** ***Vedānta-sūtra*****.**

### Text 56

*sārvabhaume kṛpā kari’ gaurāṅga śrī-hari*  
*brahma-sūtra vyākhyā kaila bhāgavata dhari’*

**Перевод:**

**“Śrī Gaurāṅga Hari mercifully explained the** ***Brahma-sūtra*** **to Sārvabhauma Bhaṭṭācārya on the basis of** ***Śrīmad Bhāgavatam*****.**

### Text 57

*sei vidyā sārvabhauma śrī-madhusūdane*  
*śikhāila kṣetra-dhāme parama yatane*

**Перевод:**

**“Sārvabhauma very carefully taught this knowledge to Śrī Madhusūdana in Śrī Kṣetra Dhāma.**

### Text 58

*sei madhu-vācaspati prabhu-ājñā peye*  
*āchhe vārāṇasī dhāme dekha tumi yeye*

**Перевод:**

**“Go and see Madhusūdana Vāchaspati, who is in Vārāṇasī Dhāma on the order of the Lord.**

### Text 59

*bāhye teṅha sampradāyī vaidāntika haya*  
*śāṅkarī sannyāsī yāra nikaṭe paḍaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Externally, he is a traditional Vedāntic scholar, and Śaṅkarite** ***sannyāsīs*** **study with him.**

### Text 60

*krame krame sannyāsi-gaṇere kṛpā kari’*  
*gaurāṅgera vyākhyā śikṣā deya sūtra dhari’*

**Перевод:**

**“In a gradual way, he mercifully teaches the** ***sannyāsīs*** **Gaurāṅga’s explanations of the** ***sūtras*****.**

### Text 61

*pṛthak bhāṣyera ebe nāhi prayojana*  
*bhāgavate kaya sūtra bhāṣyete gaṇana*

**Перевод:**

**“There is no need for a separate commentary on the** ***Brahma-sūtra*** **now. The** ***Bhāgavatam*** **explains the** ***sūtras*** **and is considered the** ***sūtras’*** **natural commentary.**

### Text 62

*kāle yabe bhāṣyera ha-ibe prayojana*  
*śrī-govinda-bhāṣya tabe habe prakaṭana*

**Перевод:**

**“In time, when there is a need for another commentary, the** ***Śrī Govinda-bhāṣya*** **will be revealed.**

### Text 63

*sārvabhauma samparke sei gopīnātha*  
*śunila prabhura bhāṣya sārvabhauma sātha*

**Перевод:**

**“Sārvabhauma’s relative, Gopīnātha Ācārya, heard the Lord’s commentary with Sārvabhauma.**

### Text 64

*kāle teṅha prabhura icchāya janma laye*  
*baladevāveśe yābe jayapura jaye*

**Перевод:**

**“In time, by the will of the Lord, he will take birth as Baladeva Vidyābhūṣaṇa and conquer Jaipur.**

### Text 65

*tathā śrī-govinda bale bhāṣya prakāśiyā*  
*sevibe gaurāṅga-pada jīve nistāriyā*

**Перевод:**

**“There, he will compose a commentary spoken by Śrī Govinda, serve the feet of Gaurāṅga, and deliver the souls.**

### Text 66

*ei saba gūḍha kathā rūpa sanātana*  
*sakala kahibe tomā prati dui-jana”*

**Перевод:**

**“Rūpa and Sanātana will explain all these confidential matters to you.”**

### Text 67

*nityānanda-vākya śuni’ śrī-jīva goṅsāi*  
*kāṅdiyā loṭāya bhūme saṁjña āra nāi*

**Перевод:**

**Upon hearing the words of Nityānanda, Śrī Jīva Gosvāmī rolled on the ground crying and lost consciousness.**

### Text 68

*kṛpā kari’ prabhu nija caraṇa-yugala*  
*śrī-jīvera śire dhari’ arpilena bala*

**Перевод:**

**Mercifully, the Lord placed His feet on Śrī Jīva’s head and empowered him.**

### Text 69

*“jaya śrī-gaurāṅga jaya nityānanda-rāya”*  
*baliyā nācena jīva vaiṣṇava-sabhāya*

**Перевод:**

**Chanting, “All glory to Śrī Gaurāṅga! All glory to Nityānanda Rāya!”, Jīva then danced amidst the assembly of Vaiṣṇavas.**

### Text 70-71

*śrīvāsādi chila tathā yata mahājana*  
*jīve nityānanda-kṛpā kari’ daraśana*

*sabe nāce “śrī-gaurāṅga nityānanda” bali’*  
*mahākala-rave tathā haya hulu-sthulī*

**Перевод:**

**Śrīvāsa and the other great souls there, seeing the mercy of Nityānanda upon Jīva, danced, chanting, “Śrī Gaurāṅga! Nityānanda!”, and the loud sound of women cheering arose.**

### Text 72

*kata-kṣaṇa pare nṛtya kari’ samvaraṇa*  
*jīve laye nityānanda basila takhana*

**Перевод:**

**Eventually everyone stopped dancing, and Nityānanda sat down with Jīva.**

### Text 73

*jīvera ha-ila vāsā śrīvāsa-aṅgane*  
*sandhyā-kāle āila punaḥ prabhu daraśane*

**Перевод:**

**Jīva stayed in the courtyard of Śrīvāsa, and in the evening he came to see Nityānanda Prabhu again.**

### Text 74

*nirjane basiyā prabhu gaura-guṇa gāya*  
*śrī-jīva āsiyā paḍe nityānanda-pāya*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu was sitting alone chanting the glories of Śrī Gaura. Jīva came and bowed at His feet.**

### Text 75

*yatna kari prabhu tāre nikaṭe basāya*  
*kara-yoḍa kari’ jīva svadainya jānāya*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu carefully sat Jīva nearby. Jīva joined his palms and humbly prayed to the Lord.**

### Text 76

*jīva bale, “prabhu more karuṇā kariyā*  
*navadvīpa-dhāma-tattva bala vivariya”*

**Перевод:**

**Jīva said, “O Lord, mercifully describe the glories of Navadvīpa Dhāma for me.”**

### Text 77

*prabhu bale, “ohe jīva baliba tomāya*  
*atyanta nigūḍha tattva rākhibe hiyāya*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said, “O Jīva, I will describe the most confidential truths to you. Hold them in your heart.**

### Text 78

*yathā tathā ebe ihā nā kara prakāśa*  
*prakaṭa-līlāra ante ha-ibe vikāśa*

**Перевод:**

**“Do not reveal them anywhere now. They will be revealed after the manifest Pastimes of the Lord.**

### Text 79-80

*ei navadvīpa haya sarva-dhāma-sāra*  
*śrī-virajā brahma-dhāma ādi haye pāra*

*vaikuṇṭhera para śvetadvīpa śrī-goloka*  
*tadante gokula vṛndāvana kṛṣṇa-loka*

**Перевод:**

**“Navadvīpa is the best of all the Lord’s abodes. Beyond Śrī Virajā, Brahmaloka, and Vaikuṇṭha is Śvetadvīpa, Śrī Goloka, and lastly Gokula Vṛndāvana, Kṛṣṇa’s abode.**

### Text 81

*sei loka dui bhāve haya ta prakāśa*  
*mādhurya audārya bhede rasera vikāśa \[81\]*

**Перевод:**

**“That abode manifests in two ways: its rasa appears as either mādhurya or audārya.**

### Text 82

*mādhurye audārya pūrṇa-rūpe avasthita*  
*audārye mādhurya pūrṇa-rūpete vihita*

**Перевод:**

**“*****Audārya*** **is fully present in** ***mādhurya*****, and** ***mādhurya*** **is fully present in** ***audārya*****.**

### Text 83

*tathāpio ye prakāśe mādhurya pradhāna*  
*vṛndāvana bali tāhā jāne bhāgyavān*

**Перевод:**

**“Still, fortunate souls know that Vṛndāvana is where** ***mādhurya*** **is predominant.**

### Text 84

*ye prakāśe audārya pradhāna nitya haya*  
*sei navadvīpa-dhāma sarva-vede kaya*

**Перевод:**

**“All the** ***Vedas*** **say that Navadvīpa Dhāma is where** ***audārya*** **is always predominant.**

### Text 85

*vṛndāvana navadvīpe nāhi kichhu bheda*  
*rasera prakāśa-bhede karaya prabheda*

**Перевод:**

**“There is no difference between Vṛndāvana and Navadvīpa other than the difference made by their respective manifestations of** ***rasa*****.**

### Text 86

*ei dhāma nitya-siddha cinmaya ananta*  
*jaḍa-buddhi-jane tāra nāhi pāya anta*

**Перевод:**

**“This abode is eternally perfect, spiritual, and infinite. Materialists cannot even reach its edge.**

### Text 87

*hlādinī-prabhāve jīva chāḍi’ jaḍa-dharma*  
*nitya-siddha jñāna-bale pāya tāra dharma*

**Перевод:**

**“Only by the influence of the pleasure potency of the Lord do souls give up material nature and realise the true nature of the Dhāma through eternal, perfect knowledge.**

### Text 88

*sarva-navadvīpa haya cinmaya prakāśa*  
*sei pīṭhe śrī-gaurāṅga karena vilāsa*

**Перевод:**

**“The whole of Navadvīpa is a spiritual manifestation, and Śrī Gaurāṅga performs His Pastimes there.**

### Text 89

*carma-cakṣe loke dekhe prapañca gaṭhana*  
*māyā ācchādiyā rākhe nitya-niketana*

**Перевод:**

**“With fleshy eyes, people see a material form of the Dhāma—Māyā keeps the eternal abode covered.**

### Text 90

*navadvīpe māyā nāi jaḍa deśa kāla*  
*kichu nāhi āchhe tathā jīvera jañjāla*

**Перевод:**

**“In Navadvīpa, there is no illusory matter, space, or time. Souls undergo no suffering there.**

### Text 91

*kintu karma-bandha-krame jīva māyā-vaśe*  
*navadvīpa-dhāme prāpañcika bhāve paśe*

**Перевод:**

**“Souls bound by karma and bewildered by Māyā, however, enter Navadvīpa Dhāma in a material way.**

### Text 92

*bhāgya-krame sādhu-saṅge premera udaya*  
*haya yabe tabe dekhe vaikuṇṭha cinmaya*

**Перевод:**

**“When souls develop divine love by good fortune and the association of the** ***sādhus*****, they then see the spiritual world.**

### Text 93

*aprākṛta deśa kāla dhāma dravya yata*  
*anāyāse dekhe svīya cakṣe avirata*

**Перевод:**

**“Souls easily and uninterruptedly see with their own eyes the supramundane space, time, light, and objects in the Dhāma.**

### Text 94

*ei ta kahinu āmi navadvīpa-tattva*  
*vicāriyā dekha jīva haye śuddha sattva”*

**Перевод:**

**“Thus, I have described the glory of Navadvīpa. O Jīva, reflect on it with a pure heart.”**

### Text 95

*nitāi-jāhnavā-pade nitya yāra āśa*  
*gūḍha-tattva kare bhaktivinoda prakāśa*

**Перевод:**

**Bhaktivinoda, whose eternal aspiration is the feet of Nitāi and Jāhnavā, reveals the hidden glories of Nadīyā.**

## Chapter Five – Śrī Māyāpura and Antardvīpa

### Text 1

*jaya jaya śrī-caitanya śacīra nandana*  
*jaya jaya nityānanda jāhnavī-jīvana*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to the son of Śacī, Śrī Caitanya! All glory to the life of Jāhnavā, Śrī Nityānanda!**

### Text 2

*jaya jaya navadvīpa-dhāma sarva-dhāma-sāra*  
*yathā kali-yuge haila gaura avatāra*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to Navadvīpa Dhāma, the best of all abodes, where Gaura descended during the Age of Kali!**

### Text 3

*nityānanda prabhu bale, “śunaha vacana*  
*ṣola-krośa navadvīpa yathā vṛndāvana*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu said, “Listen to My words! The thirty-two miles of Navadvīpa are non-different from Vṛndāvana.**

### Text 4

*ei ṣola-krośa madhye dvīpa haya naya*  
*aṣṭa-dala padma yena jalete bhāsaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Within these thirty-two miles, there are nine islands, which form an eight-petalled lotus that floats on water.**

**\|**

### Text 5

*aṣṭa-dala aṣṭa-dvīpa madhye antardvīpa*  
*tāra mājhe māyāpura madhya-bindu-ṭīpa*

**Перевод:**

**“In the middle of the eight petals—the eight islands—is Antardvīpa. At its centre is Māyāpura.**

### Text 6

*māyāpura yoga-pīṭha sadā golākāra*  
*tathā nitya caitanyera vividha bihāra*

**Перевод:**

**“The circular Yoga Pīṭha in Māyāpura is eternal. There, Śrī Caitanya performs various eternal Pastimes.**

### Text 7

*trisahasra-dhanu tāra paridhi pramāṇa*  
*sahasreka-dhanu tāra vyāsera vidhāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Its circumference is six miles, and its diameter two miles.**

### Text 8

*ei yoga-pīṭha-mājhe vaise pañca-tattva*  
*anya-sthāna haite yoga-pīṭhera mahattva*

**Перевод:**

**“The Pañca Tattva reside at the Yoga Pīṭha, whose greatness exceeds that of all other places.**

### Text 9

*ati śīghra gupta habe prabhura ichchhāya*  
*bhāgīrathī-jale habe saṅgopita prāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Very soon it will be hidden by the will of the Lord. The waters of the Gaṅgā will almost entirely conceal it.**

### Text 10

*kabhu punaḥ prabhu-icchā habe balavān*  
*prakāśa ha-ibe dhāma habe dīptimān*

**Перевод:**

**“Someday, when the desire of the Lord becomes strong, the Yoga Pīṭha will be revealed and the Dhāma will shine again.**

### Text 11

*nitya-dhāma kabhu kāle lopa nāhi haya*  
*gupta haye punar-bāra haya ta udaya*

**Перевод:**

**“The eternal Dhāma is never lost at any time—it only becomes hidden and then emerges again.**

### Text 12

*bhāgīrathī pūrva-tīre haya māyāpura*  
*māyāpure nitya āchhena āmāra ṭhākura*

**Перевод:**

**“My Lord is eternally present in Māyāpura on the eastern bank of the Gaṅgā.**

### Text 13

*loka-dṛṣṭye sannyāsī ha-iyā viśvambhara*  
*chāḍi’ navadvīpa phire deśa-deśāntara*

**Перевод:**

**“In the vision of common people, Viśvambhara became a** ***sannyāsī*****, left Navadvīpa, and toured other places.**

### Text 14

*vastutaḥ gaurāṅga mora navadvīpa-dhāma*  
*chāḍiyā nā yāya kabhu māyāpura-grāma*

**Перевод:**

**“In truth, however, My Gaurāṅga never leaves Navadvīpa Dhāma or the village of Māyāpura.**

### Text 15

*dainandina līlā tāṅra dekhe bhakta-gaṇa*  
*tumio dekhaha jīva gaurāṅga-nartana*

**Перевод:**

**“The devotees see His Pastimes here daily, and you too, Jīva, will see His dancing.**

### Text 16

*māyāpura ante antardvīpa śobhā pāya*  
*gaurāṅga darśana brahmā pāila yathāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Māyāpura shines within Antardvīpa, where Brahmā saw Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 17

*ohe jīva cāha yadi dekhite sakala*  
*parikramā kara tumi ha-ibe saphala”*

**Перевод:**

**“O Jīva, if you want to see everything, then circumambulate the Dhāma, and you will be successful.”**

### Text 18

*prabhu-vākya śuni’ jīva sajala-nayane*  
*daṇḍavat haye paḍe prabhura caraṇe*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the words of Nityānanda Prabhu, with tears in his eyes Jīva bowed at His feet.**

### Text 19

*“kṛpā yadi kara prabhu ei akiñcane*  
*saṅge laye parikramā karāo āpane”*

**Перевод:**

**“O Lord, if You are merciful to this poor soul, then please personally take me with You on a circumambulation of the Dhāma.”**

### Text 20

*jīvera prārthanā śuni’ nityānanda-rāya*  
*“tathāstu” baliyā nija mānasa jānāya*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing Jīva’s prayer, Nityānanda Rāya answered, “So be it.”**

### Text 21

*prabhu bale, “ohe jīva adya māyāpura*  
*karaha darśana kalya bhramiba pracura”*

**Перевод:**

**He said, “O Jīva, today see Māyāpura. Tomorrow we will tour extensively.”**

### Text 22

*eta bali’ nityānanda uṭhila takhana*  
*pāchhe pāchhe uṭhe jīva praphullita mana*

**Перевод:**

**Having said this, Nityānanda arose, and with a joyous heart Jīva rose after Him.**

### Text 23

*cale nityānanda-rāya manda manda gati*  
*gaurāṅga-premete deha suvihvala ati*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Rāya moved slowly, His body completely overwhelmed by divine love for Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 24

*mohana murati prabhu bhāve ḍhala-ḍhala*  
*alaṅkāra sarva-dehe kare jhalamala*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu’s enchanting form swayed to and fro in ecstasy, and the ornaments all over His body shone.**

### Text 25

*ye caraṇa brahmā śiva dhyāne nāhi pāya*  
*śrī-jīve kariyā kṛpā se pada bāḍāya*

**Перевод:**

**The feet that Brahmā and Śiva cannot attain by meditation mercifully walked in front of Śrī Jīva.**

### Text 26

*pāchhe thāki’ jīva laya padāṅkera dhūli*  
*sarva-aṅge mākhe cale baḍa kutūhalī*

**Перевод:**

**Remaining behind, Jīva collected dust from their footprints, smeared it all over his body, and proceeded with great delight.**

### Text 27

*jagannātha-miśra-gṛhe karila praveśa*  
*śacī-mātā śrī-caraṇe jānāya viśeṣa*

**Перевод:**

**They entered the home of Jagannātha Miśra and spoke carefully at the holy feet of Mother Śacī.**

### Text 28

*“śunago jananī ei jīva mahāmatī*  
*śrī-gaurāṅga-priya-dāsa bhāgyavān ati”*

**Перевод:**

**“O Mother, listen! This is Jīva, a dear servant of Śrī Gaurāṅga. He is very intelligent and very fortunate.”**

### Text 29

*balite balite jīva ācāḍiyā paḍe*  
*chinna-mūla taru yena baḍa baḍa jhaḍe*

**Перевод:**

**As Nityānanda Prabhu spoke, Jīva threw himself down like a tree uprooted by an intense storm.**

### Text 30

*śacīra caraṇe paḍi’ yāya gaḍāgaḍi*  
*sāttvika vikāra dehe kare huḍāhuḍi*

**Перевод:**

**He bowed at the feet of Śacī and rolled on the ground. Divine ecstasy surged through his body.**

### Text 31

*kṛpā kari’ śacī-devī kaila āśīrvāda*  
*sei dina sei gṛhe pāila prasāda*

**Перевод:**

**Śacī Devī mercifully blessed him, and that day he received** ***prasāda*** **in her home.**

### Text 32

*viṣṇu-priyā śacī-devī-ājñā yabe pāila*  
*nānā anna vyañjanādi randhana karila*

**Перевод:**

**Receiving the order of Śacī Devī, Viṣṇu Priyā cooked rice, various vegetables, and other things.**

### Text 33

*śrī-vaṁśī-vadanānanda prabhu kata–kṣaṇe*  
*śrī-gaurāṅge bhoga nivedila sayatane*

**Перевод:**

**Śrī Vaṁśī Vadanānanda Prabhu then carefully offered the preparations to Śrī Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 34

*īśāna ṭhākura sthāna kari’ ataḥpara*  
*nityānande bhuñjāila hariṣa antara*

**Перевод:**

**Īśāna Ṭhākura then prepared a place and fed Nityānanda, with joy in his heart.**

### Text 35

*putra-snehe śacī-devī nityānande bale*  
*“khāo vāchhā nityānanda jananīra sthale*

**Перевод:**

**With motherly affection, Śacī Devī said to Nityānanda, “Eat, my child. Nityānanda, You are in the home of Your mother.**

### Text 36

*ei āmi gaura-candre bhuñjānu gopane*  
*tumi khāile baḍa sukhī ha-i āmi mane”*

**Перевод:**

**“Privately, I fed this to Gaura-candra. If You eat it, I will be very happy at heart.”**

### Text 37

*jananīra vākye prabhu nityānanda-rāya*  
*bhuñjila ānande, jīva avaśiṣṭa pāya*

**Перевод:**

**At the request of Mother Śacī, Nityānanda Rāya joyfully ate, and Jīva received His remnants.**

### Text 38

*jīva bale, “dhanya āmi mahāprabhu-ghare*  
*pāinu prasāda anna ei māyāpure”*

**Перевод:**

**Jīva said, “I am so fortunate to have received** ***prasāda*** **at the home of Mahāprabhu in Māyāpura.”**

### Text 39

*bhojana kariyā tabe nityānanda-rāya*  
*śacī-devī śrī-caraṇe ha-ila vidāya*

**Перевод:**

**After eating, Nityānanda Rāya took leave of Śacī Devī’s holy feet.**

### Text 40

*yāibāra kāle saṅge vaṁśīke la-ila*  
*śrī-jīva vaṁśīra pade praṇati karila*

**Перевод:**

**As He was leaving, He took Vaṁśī Vadanānanda with Him. Śrī Jīva bowed at the feet of Vaṁśī.**

### Text 41

*jīva prati bale prabhu, “e vaṁśī-vadana*  
*śrī-kṛṣṇera priya-vaṁśī jāne bhakta-jana*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda said to Jīva, “This is Vaṁśī Vadanānanda. The devotees know him to be Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s dear flute.**

### Text 42

*ihāra kṛpāya jīva haya kṛṣṇākṛṣṭa*  
*mahārāsa labhe sabe ha-iyā satṛṣṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“By his mercy, souls become attracted to Kṛṣṇa and desire to join the Rāsa Dance.**

### Text 43

*dekha jīva ei gṛhe caitanya-ṭhākura*  
*āmā sabā laye līlā karila prachura*

**Перевод:**

**“Jīva, look! Lord Caitanya performed many Pastimes with us in this house.**

### Text 44

*ei dekha jagannātha miśrera mandira*  
*viṣṇu-pūjā nitya yathā karitena dhīra*

**Перевод:**

**“See here. This is the Temple of Jagannātha Miśra, where Gaura would earnestly worship Viṣṇu each day.**

### Text 45

*ei gṛhe karitena atithi-sevana*  
*tulasī-maṇḍapa ei karaha darśana*

**Перевод:**

**“In this house, the Lord would serve guests. See here His** ***tulasī*** **courtyard.**

### Text 46

*śrī-gaurāṅga-candra gṛhe chila yata kāla*  
*pitāra ācāra pālitena bhakta-pāla*

**Перевод:**

**“When Śrī Gauracandra, the maintainer of the devotees, was present at home, He would perform His father’s duties.**

### Text 47

*ebe saba vaṁśī-ṭhākurera tattvādhīne*  
*īśāna nirvāha kare prati dine dine*

**Перевод:**

**“Īśāna Ṭhākura now performs these duties each day under the direction of Vaṁśī Ṭhākura.**

### Text 48

*ei sthāne chila eka nimba vṛkṣa-vara*  
*prabhura paraśe vṛkṣa ha-ila agocara”*

**Перевод:**

**“In this place, there was a great neem tree that disappeared upon being touched by the Lord.”**

### Text 49

*yata kāṅde nityānanda kariyā varṇana*  
*jīva vaṁśī duṅhe tata karaye krandana*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda wept as He described all of this, as did Jīva and Vaṁśī.**

### Text 50

*dekhite dekhite tathā āila śrīvāsa*  
*cāri-jane cale chāḍi’ jagannātha-vāsa*

**Перевод:**

**Just then, Śrīvāsa arrived, and the four of them then left Jagannātha Miśra’s home.**

### Text 51

*śata-dhanu uttarete śrīvāsa-aṅgana*  
*jīve dekhāila prabhu ānandita mana*

**Перевод:**

**Three hundred and twenty metres to the north, Nityānanda Prabhu joyfully showed Śrīvāsa Aṅgana to Jīva.**

### Text 52

*śrīvāsa-aṅgane jīva yāya gaḍāgaḍi*  
*smariyā prabhura līlā preme huḍāhuḍi*

**Перевод:**

**Jīva rolled on the ground in Śrīvāsa Aṅgana. Remembering the Pastimes of the Lord there, he was overwhelmed with divine love.**

### Text 53

*śrī-jīva uṭhibāmātra dekhe eka raṅga*  
*nāciche gaurāṅga laye bhakta antaraṅga*

**Перевод:**

**Jīva immediately had a vision: he saw Gaurāṅga dancing with His intimate devotees.**

### Text 54

*mahā-saṅkīrtana dekhe vallabha-nandana*  
*sarva-bhakta mājhe prabhura apūrva nartana*

**Перевод:**

**Jīva saw the Lord’s** ***mahā-saṅkīrtana*** **and wonderful dancing amidst all the devotees.**

### Text 55

*nāciche advaita prabhu nityānanda-rāya*
