*gadādhara haridāsa nāce āra gāya*

**Перевод:**

**Advaita Prabhu, Nityānanda Rāya, Gadādhara, and Haridāsa were dancing and singing.**

### Text 56

*śuklāmbara nāce āra śata śata jana*  
*dekhiyā premete jīva haila acetana*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing Śuklāmbara and hundreds of other devotees dancing, Jīva fainted in divine love.**

### Text 57

*cetana pāile āra se raṅga nā bhāya*  
*kāṅdi’ jīva gosvāmī karena hāya hāya*

### Text 58

*“kena mora kichu pūrve janama nahila*  
*emana kīrtanānanda bhāgye nā ghaṭila*

**Перевод:**

**When he regained consciousness and this vision disappeared, Jīva Gosvāmī wept and lamented, “Why was I not born a little earlier? I was not fortunate enough to be part of such ecstatic** ***kīrtana*****.**

### Text 59

*prabhu nityānanda-kṛpā asīma ananta*  
*sei bale kṣaṇa-kāla hainu bhāgyavanta*

**Перевод:**

**“The mercy of Nityānanda Prabhu is unlimited and infinite, and by its influence I have attained a moment of good fortune.**

### Text 60

*icchā haya māyāpure thāki’ cira-kāla*  
*ghucibe sampūrṇa-rūpe māyāra jañjāla*

**Перевод:**

**“I want to stay in Māyāpura forever. Here, the miseries of material existence will be completely dispelled.**

### Text 61

*dāsera vāsanā haite prabhu-ājñā baḍa*  
*māyāpura chāḍite antara dhaḍa-phaḍa”*

**Перевод:**

**“The order of the Lord, however, is greater than the desire of His servant, and my heart is palpitating at the thought of leaving Māyāpura.”**

### Text 62

*tathā haite nityānanda jīve laye yāya*  
*daśa-dhanu uttare advaita-gṛha pāya*

**Перевод:**

**From there, Nityānanda took Jīva thirty-two metres north to the house of Advaita.**

### Text 63

*prabhu bale, “dekha jīva sītānāthālaya*  
*hethā vaiṣṇavera goṣṭhi sadāi milaya*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said, “O Jīva, see the house of Advaita. The devotees met here every day.**

### Text 64

*hethā sītānātha kaila kṛṣṇera pūjana*  
*huṅkāre ānila mora śrī-gaurāṅga dhana”*

**Перевод:**

**“Here, Advaita worshipped Kṛṣṇa and brought down our wealth, Śrī Gaurāṅga, with His cries.”**

### Text 65

*tathā gaḍāgaḍi diyā cale cāri-jana*  
*pañca-dhanu pūrve gadādharera bhavana*

**Перевод:**

**There, the four of them rolled on the ground. Then they walked sixteen metres east to the house of Gadādhara.**

### Text 66

*tathā haite dekhāila nityānanda-rāya*  
*sarva-pāriṣada gṛha yathāya tathāya*

**Перевод:**

**Thereafter, Nityānanda showed Jīva the houses of all the Lord’s associates throughout Māyāpura.**

### Text 67

*brāhmaṇa-maṇḍalī gṛha kariyā darśana*  
*tabe cale gaṅgā-tīre harṣe cāri-jana*

**Перевод:**

**The four of them visited the homes of some** ***brāhmaṇas*** **and then joyfully walked to the bank of the Gaṅgā.**

### Text 68

*māyāpura sīmā-śeṣa vṛddha-śivālaya*  
*jāhnavīra taṭe dekhe jīva mahāśaya*

**Перевод:**

**Along the outer boundary of Māyāpura on the bank of the Gaṅgā, Jīva saw the Temple of Vṛddha Śiva.**

### Text 69

*prabhu bale, “māyāpure ini kṣetra-pāla*  
*prauḍhā-māyā śakti adhiṣṭhāna nitya-kāla*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu said, “In Māyāpura, Vṛddha Śiva is the protector of the Dhāma. His consort, Prauḍhā Māyā, resides with him eternally.**

### Text 70

*prabhu yabe aprakaṭa ha-ibe takhana*  
*tāṅhāra ichchhāya gaṅgā ha-ibe vardhana*

**Перевод:**

**“When the Lord disappears, then, by His will, the Gaṅgā will swell.**

### Text 71

*māyāpura prāya gaṅgā ācchādibe jale*  
*śata-varṣa rākhi’ punaḥ chāḍibena bale*

**Перевод:**

**“She will almost entirely cover Māyāpura with water for one hundred years, and then she will withdraw.**

### Text 72

*sthāna mātra jāgibeka gṛha nā rahibe*  
*vāsa-hīna haye kata-kāla sthita habe*

**Перевод:**

**“At that time, only the land will emerge; no houses will remain. Māyāpura will stay uninhabited for a long time.**

*punaḥ kabhu prabhu-icchā haye balavān*  
*habe māyāpure ei rūpa vāsa-sthāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Again, when the Lord strongly desires it, Māyāpura will be inhabited like this again.**

### Text 73

*ei saba ghāṭa gaṅgā-tīre punaḥ habe*  
*prabhura mandira karibena bhakta sabe*

**Перевод:**

**“All these** ***ghāṭas*** **will appear on the banks of the Gaṅgā again, and the devotees will make Temples for the Lord.**

### Text 74

*adbhuta mandira eka ha-ibe prakāśa*  
*gaurāṅgera nitya-sevā ha-ibe vikāśa*

**Перевод:**

**“One wonderful Temple will manifest, and there daily worship of Gaurāṅga will flourish.**

### Text 75

*prauḍhā-māyā vṛddha-śiva āsi’ punarāya*  
*nija kārya sādhibeka prabhura icchāya*

**Перевод:**

**“By the will of the Lord, Prauḍhā Māyā and Vṛddha Śiva will return and perform their duties (of revealing and protecting the Dhāma).”**

### Text 76

*eta śuni’ jīva tabe kara-yoḍa kari’*  
*prabhure jijñāse vārtā pada-yūga dhari’*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Jīva joined his palms, questioned Nityānanda Prabhu, and then grasped the Lord’s feet.**

### Text 77

*“ohe prabhu tumi śeṣa tattvera nidāna*  
*dhāma-rūpa nāma-tattva tomāri vidhāna*

**Перевод:**

**“O Lord, You are the basis of the ultimate truth. You manifest Yourself as the Dhāma and the Holy Name.**

### Text 78

*yadio prabhura icchā-mate karma kara*  
*tabu jīva-guru tumi sarva-śakti-dhara*

**Перевод:**

**“Although You act according to the will of Mahāprabhu, You are the Guru of all souls and the controller of all energies.**

### Text 79

*gaurāṅge tomāte bheda yei jana kare*  
*pāṣaṇḍī madhyete tāṅre vijña-jane dhare*

**Перевод:**

**“The wise consider anyone who distinguishes between You and Gaurāṅga to be a sinner.**

### Text 80

*sarvajña puruṣa tumi līlā avatāra*  
*saṁśaya jāgila eka hṛdaye āmāra*

**Перевод:**

**“You are the all-knowing Lord, who descends to perform Pastimes. A doubt has arisen in my heart.**

### Text 81

*ye samaya gaṅgā lukāibe māyāpura*  
*kothā yābe śiva śakti balaha ṭhākura”*

**Перевод:**

**“O Lord, please tell me, where will Vṛddha Śiva and Prauḍhā Māyā go when the Gaṅgā hides Māyāpura?”**

### Text 82

*nityānanda bale, “jīva śunaha vacana*  
*gaṅgāra paścima bhūmi karaha darśana*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu said, “Jīva, listen to My words and look at the land to the west of the Gaṅgā.**

### Text 83

*ei uccha caḍā dekha pāraḍāṅgā nāma*  
*tathā āchhe vipra-maṇḍalīra eka grāma*

**Перевод:**

**“See there the sand dune known as Pāraḍāṅgā. In that place, there is a village of** ***brāhmaṇas*****.**

### Text 84

*tāhāra uttare āchhe jāhnavī-pulina*  
*chinnaḍeṅgā bali tāre jānena pravīṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“The wise know the bank of the Gaṅgā to its north as Chinnaḍeṅgā.**

### Text 85

*ei ta puline eka nagara vasibe*  
*tathā śiva śakti kichhu divasa rahibe*

**Перевод:**

**“A village will be established on this bank. Vṛddha Śiva and Prauḍhā Māyā will remain there for some time.**

### Text 86

*o pulina māhātmya ke kahibāre pāre*  
*rāsa-sthalī āchhe yathā jāhnavīra dhāre*

**Перевод:**

**“Who can describe the glories of that bank? The Rāsa Sthalī is there beside the Gaṅgā.**

### Text 87

*bālumaya bhūmi vaṭe carma-cakṣe bhāya*  
*ratnamaya nitya-dhāma divya līlā tāya*

**Перевод:**

**“It appears like a beach to fleshy eyes, but the Lord’s divine Pastimes take place in that eternal abode of gemstone.**

### Text 88

*māyāpura haya śrī-gokula mahāvana*  
*pāraḍāṅgā saṭṭīkāra svarūpa gaṇana*

**Перевод:**

**“Māyāpura is Śrī Gokula Mahāvana, and Pāraḍāṅgā is considered Saṭṭīkāra.**

### Text 89

*tathā āchhe vṛndāvana śrī-rāsa-maṇḍala*  
*kāle ei sthāne habe gāna kolāhala*

**Перевод:**

**“Śrī Rāsa Maṇḍala of Vṛndāvana is present there, and in the future there will be joyous singing at that place.**

### Text 90

*māyāpura śrī-pulina madhye bhāgīrathī*  
*saba laye gaura-dhāma jāna mahāmati*

**Перевод:**

**“O wise soul, know that Māyāpura and the Gaṅgā at Śrī Pulina are both part of Śrī Gaura’s Dhāma.**

### Text 91

*pañca-krośa dhāma yebā karibe bhramaṇa*  
*māyāpura śrī-pulina karibe darśana*

**Перевод:**

**“One who visits this abode of ten miles will see Māyāpura and Śrī Pulina.**

### Text 92

*phālguna-pūrṇimā-dine ye kare bhramaṇa*  
*pañca-krośa bhakta-saha pāya nitya-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**“One who visits these ten miles with devotees on the full moon day in the month of Phālguna attains the eternal wealth.**

### Text 93

*ohe jīva gūḍha kathā śunaha āmāra*  
*śrī-gaurāṅga-mūrti śobhe śrī-viṣṇu-priyāra*

**Перевод:**

**“O Jīva, listen to My confidential words. Śrī Viṣṇu Priyā’s Deity of Śrī Gaurāṅga shines here.**

### Text 94

*ai kāle miśra-vaṁśodbhava vipra–gaṇa*  
*saṭṭīkāra dhāme labe śrī-mūrti-ratana*

**Перевод:**

**“In the future,** ***brāhmaṇas*** **in the lineage of Jagannātha Miśra will bring this jewel-like Deity to Saṭṭīkāra Dhāma.**

### Text 95

*cāri-śata-varṣa gaura-janma-dina dhari’*  
*ha-ile śrī-mūrti-sevā habe sarvopari*

**Перевод:**

**“Four hundred years after the appearance day of Śrī Gaura, the worship of the Deity will be set to the highest standard.**

### Text 96

*ei saba kathā ebe rākha aprakāśa*  
*parikramā kara haye antare ullāsa*

**Перевод:**

**“For now, keep all these matters secret and circumambulate the Dhāma with a joyful heart.**

### Text 97

*vṛddha-śiva ghāṭa haite tridhanu uttara*  
*gaurāṅgera nija-ghāṭa dekha vijña-vara*

**Перевод:**

**“O best of the wise, see Śrī Gaurāṅga’s** ***ghāṭa*** **ten metres north of Vṛddha Śiva’s** ***ghāṭa*****.**

### Text 98

*ei sthāne bālya-līlā chale gaurahari*  
*bhāgīrathī-krīḍā karilena citta bhari’*

**Перевод:**

**“On the pretext of childhood Pastimes, Gaurahari played here with the Gaṅgā and filled her heart with joy.**

### Text 99

*yamunāra bhāgya dekhi’ himādri-nandinī*  
*bahu tapa kaila haite līlāra saṅginī*

**Перевод:**

**“Seeing the fortune of the Yamunā, Gaṅgā Devī had performed many austerities to become an associate of the Lord in His Pastimes.**

### Text 100

*kṛṣṇa kṛpā kari’ bale diyā daraśana*  
*‘gaura-rūpe tava jale kariba krīḍana’*

**Перевод:**

**“Kṛṣṇa mercifully appeared before her and said, ‘As Gaura, I will play in your waters.’**

### Text 101

*sei līlā kaila hethā tribhuvana rāya*  
*bhāgyavān jīva dekhi’ baḍa sukha pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“The Lord of the three worlds performed this Pastime here. Upon seeing this place, fortunate souls feel great joy.**

### Text 102

*pañca-daśa-dhanu yei ghāṭa tad uttare*  
*mādhāiyera ghāṭa bale vyakta carācare*

**Перевод:**

**“The** ***ghāṭa*** **one hundred and sixty metres to the north is known by everyone as Mādhāi’s** ***ghāṭa*****.**

### Text 103

*tāra pāñca-dhanura uttare ghāṭa śobhā*  
*nāgarīyā janera sarvadā mano-lobhā*

**Перевод:**

**“Sixteen metres to its north is a beautiful** ***ghāṭa*** **that always attracts the minds of all the villagers.**

### Text 104

*bārakoṇā ghāṭa ei atīva sundara*  
*viśvakarmā nirmilena prabhu-ājñā-dhara*

**Перевод:**

**“This extremely beautiful** ***ghāṭa*** **is known as Bārakoṇā Ghāṭa. Viśvakarmā constructed it on the order of the Lord.**

### Text 105

*ei ghāṭe dekha jīva pañca śivālaya*  
*pañca-tīrtha liṅga pañca sadā jyotirmaya*

**Перевод:**

**“O Jīva, see the five Śiva Temples at this** ***ghāṭa*****. Five effulgent** ***liṅgams*** **always reside here in these five holy places.**

### Text 106

*ei cāri ghāṭa māyāpura śobhā kare*  
*yathāya karile snāna sarva-duḥkha hare*

**Перевод:**

**“These four** ***ghāṭas*** **beautify Māyāpura. Bathing at them removes all sorrow.**

### Text 107

*māyāpura pūrva-dike āchhe yei sthāna*  
*antardvīpa bali’ tāya nāma vidyamāna*

**Перевод:**

**“To the east of Māyāpura is a place known as Antardvīpa.**

### Text 108

*ebe prabhu-icchā-mate loka-vāsa-hīna*  
*ei rūpa sthiti rahe āro kata dina*

**Перевод:**

**“Now, by the will of the Lord, it is uninhabited. It will remain this way for some time.**

### Text 109

*kata-kāle punaḥ hethā loka vāsa habe*  
*prakāśa ha-ibe sthāna nadīyā gaurave*

**Перевод:**

**“Eventually, people will live here again, and the place will manifest as the glory of Nadīyā.**

### Text 110

*ohe jīva adya tumi raha māyāpure*  
*kalya laye yāba āmi sīmanta-nagare”*

**Перевод:**

**“O Jīva, today stay in Māyāpura. Tomorrow I will take you to Sīmantadvīpa.”**

### Text 111

*eta śuni’ jīva tabe balena vacana*  
*“saṁśaya uṭhila eka karaha śravaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Jīva said, “A doubt has arisen within me. Please hear it.**

### Text 112-113

*yabe gaṅgā-devī māyāpura ācchādana*  
*uṭhāiyā la-ibena nā rabe gopana*

*sei kāle bhakta-gaṇa kon cihna dhari’*  
*prakāśibe gupta-sthāna bala vyakta kari’”*

**Перевод:**

**“When Gaṅgā Devī uncovers Māyāpura and it no longer remains hidden, by what signs will the devotees discover the hidden places? Please explain this.”**

### Text 114

*jīvera vacana śuni’ nityānanda-rāya*  
*balilā uttara tabe amṛtera prāya*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing Jīva’s words, Nityānanda Rāya gave a nectarean answer.**

### Text 115

*“śuna jīva gaṅgā yabe ācchādibe sthāna*  
*māyāpura eka koṇa rabe vidyamāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Listen, Jīva, when Gaṅgā Devī covers Māyāpura, one edge of it will remain manifest.**

### Text 116

*tathāya yavana vāsa ha-ibe pracura*  
*tathāpi rahibe nāma tāra māyāpura*

**Перевод:**

**“Many uncivilised people will reside there, but the name of the place will remain Māyāpura.**

### Text 117-118

*avaśiṣṭa sthāne paścima dakṣiṇete*  
*pañca-śata-dhanu pāre pāibe dekhite*

*kichu uccha sthāna sadā tṛṇa āvaraṇa*  
*sei sthāna jagannātha-miśrera bhavana*

**Перевод:**

**“One thousand six hundred metres southwest, past this remaining place, a raised area always covered with grass will be visible. That place will be the home of Jagannātha Miśra.**

### Text 119

*tathā haite pañca-dhanu vṛddha-śivālaya*  
*ei parimāṇa dhari’ karibe nirṇaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Sixteen metres from there is the Vṛddha Śiva Temple. According to these measurements, devotees will ascertain the lost places.**

### Text 120

*śiva-ḍobā bali khāta dekhite pāibe*  
*sei khāta gaṅgā-tīra baliyā jānibe*

**Перевод:**

**“A pond known as Śiva Ḍobā will be visible. Devotees will recognise that pond as the previous bank of the Gaṅgā.**

### Text 121

*bhakta-gaṇa ei rūpe prabhura icchāya*  
*prakāśibe lupta-sthāna jānaha niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Know for certain that the devotees will reveal the lost places in this way by the will of the Lord.**

### Text 122

*prabhura śatābdī-catuṣṭaya anta yabe*  
*lupta-tīrtha uddhārera yatna habe tabe”*

**Перевод:**

**“Four hundred years after the appearance of the Lord, the endeavour to reveal the lost places will be completed.”**

### Text 123

*śrī-jīva balena, “prabhu balaha ekhana*  
*antardvīpa nāmera ye yathārtha kāraṇa”*

**Перевод:**

**Śrī Jīva said, “O Lord, now please tell me the true reason for Antardvīpa’s name.”**

### Text 124

*prabhu bale, “ei sthāne dvāparera śeṣe*  
*tapasyā karila brahmā gaura-kṛpā āśe*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu said, “At the end of Dvāpara-yuga, Brahmā performed austerities in this place, aspiring for the mercy of Gaura.**

### Text 125

*govatsa gopāla sabe kariyā haraṇa*  
*chalila kariyā māyā govindera mana*

**Перевод:**

**“Previously, using his illusion, he tried to trick Govinda by stealing the calves and cowherd boys.**

### Text 126

*nija māyā parājaya dekhi’ catur-mukha*  
*nija kārya-doṣe baḍa pāila asukha*

**Перевод:**

**“When he saw his illusion overpowered by Govinda, Brahmā became very sad, understanding his offence.**

### Text 127

*bahu stava kari’ kṛṣṇe karila minati*  
*kṣamila tāhāra doṣa vṛndāvana-pati*

**Перевод:**

**“He profusely praised Kṛṣṇa and prayed to Him. The Lord of Vṛndāvana then forgave Brahmā’s offence.**

### Text 128

*tabu brahmā mane mane karila vicāra*  
*‘brahma-buddhi mora haya atiśaya chāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Brahmā considered within his mind, ‘My mentality of being Brahmā is completely worthless.**

### Text 129

*ei buddhi doṣe kṛṣṇa-premete rahita*  
*vraja-līlā rasa bhoge ha-inu vañcita*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Because of my wrong mentality, I am devoid of divine love for Kṛṣṇa and deprived of relishing the rasa of Pastimes in Vraja.**

### Text 130

*gopāla ha-iyā janma pāitāma āmi*  
*sevitāma anāyāse gopikāra svāmī*

**Перевод:**

**“‘If I had been born as a cowherd boy, I would have easily served the Lord of the** ***gopīs*****.**

### Text 131

*se līlā-rasete mora nā ha-ila gati*  
*ebe śrī-gaurāṅge mora nā haya kumati’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I did not attain entrance into the** ***rasa*** **of those Pastimes, but now, with Śrī Gaurāṅga, I will not have any misconceptions.’**

### Text 132

*ei bali’ bahu-kāla antardvīpa sthāne*  
*tapasyā kariyā brahmā rahila dheyāne*

**Перевод:**

**“Thinking in this way, Brahmā performed austerities and meditated for a long time in Antardvīpa.**

### Text 133

*kata-dine gauracandra karuṇā kariyā*  
*catur-mukha sannidhāne kahena āsiyā*

**Перевод:**

**“Eventually, Gauracandra mercifully came before Brahmā and spoke to him.**

### Text 134

*‘ohe brahmā tava tape tuṣṭa haye āmi*  
*āsilāma dite yāhā āśā kara tumi’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Brahmā, I am pleased by your austerities, and I have come to give you whatever you desire.’**

### Text 135

*nayana meliyā brahmā dekhi’ gaura-rāya*  
*ajñāna ha-iyā bhūme paḍila tathāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Opening his eyes and seeing Gaura Rāya, Brahmā fell to the ground unconscious.**

### Text 136

*brahmāra mastake prabhu dharilā caraṇa*  
*divya-jñāna peye brahmā karaya stavana*

**Перевод:**

**“The Lord placed His foot on Brahmā’s head. Receiving divine knowledge, Brahmā praised the Lord.**

### Text 137

*‘āmi dīna-hīna ati abhimāna vaśe*  
*pāsariyā tava pada phiri jaḍa rase*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I am very lowly and fallen. Controlled by pride, I forgot Your feet and turned to material pleasures.**

### Text 138

*āmi pañcānana indra ādi deva-gaṇa*  
*adhikṛta dāsa tava śāstrera likhana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘The scriptures say that myself, Śiva, Indra, and the other gods are all servants under Your control.**

### Text 139

*śuddha dāsa haite āmādera bhāgya naya*  
*ataeva māyā moha-jāla vistāraya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘But we do not have the fortune of being Your pure servants, and thus Māyā covers us with her net of illusion.**

### Text 140

*prathama parārdha mora kāṭila jīvana*  
*ebe ta carama cintā karaye poṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“‘The first one hundred and fifty-five trillion years of my lifespan have passed. Now I am contemplating my final days.**

### Text 141

*dvitīya parārdha mora kāṭibe kemane*  
*bahir-mukha ha-ile yātanā baḍa mane*

**Перевод:**

**“‘How will I pass the second one hundred and fifty-five trillion years of my lifespan? I know that if I remain averse to You, I will suffer greatly.**

### Text 142

*ei mātra tava pade prārthanā āmāra*  
*prakaṭa līlāya yena ha-i paribāra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘My only prayer at Your feet is that I may become one of Your associates in Your manifest Pastimes.**

### Text 143

*brahma-buddhi dūre yāya hena janma pāi*  
*tomāra saṅgete thāki’ tava guṇa gāi’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘May I attain a birth in which my mentality of being Brahmā leaves me, and I live in Your company, chanting Your glories.’**

### Text 144

*brahmāra prārthanā śuni’ gaura bhagavān*  
*‘tathāstu’ baliyā vara karilena dāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing Brahmā’s prayer, the Supreme Lord Gaura said, ‘So be it’, and gave him the boon he desired.**

### Text 145

*‘ye samaya mama līlā prakaṭa ha-ibe*  
*yavanera gṛhe tumi janama labhibe*

**Перевод:**

**“‘When My Pastimes manifest, you will be born in a home of uncivilised people.**

### Text 146

*āpanāke hīna bali’ ha-ibe geyāna*  
*haridāsa habe tumi śūnya abhimāna*

**Перевод:**

**“‘You will consider yourself lowly. You will be known as Haridāsa, and you will be free from pride.**

### Text 147

*tina-lakṣa hari-nāma jihvāgre nāchibe*  
*niryāṇa-samaye tumi āmāke dekhibe*

**Перевод:**

**“‘The Name will dance on the tip of your tongue three hundred thousand times a day, and you will see Me at the time of your passing away.**

### Text 148

*ei ta sādhana-bale dviparārdha-śeṣe*  
*pābe navadvīpa-dhāma maji’ nitya-rase*

**Перевод:**

**“‘As a result of your practice, at the end of the second one hundred trillion years of your lifespan, you will attain Navadvīpa Dhāma and relish eternal rasa.**

### Text 149

*ohe brahmā śuna mora antarera kathā*  
*vyakta kabhu nā karibe śāstre yathā tathā*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Brahmā, listen to My heartfelt words, but do not express them in the scriptures anywhere at any time.**

### Text 150

*bhakta-bhāva laye bhakti-rasa āsvādiba*  
*parama durlabha saṅkīrtana prakāśiba*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Taking the mood of a devotee, I will relish the** ***rasa*** **of devotion and reveal the most rare form of** ***saṅkīrtana*****.**

### Text 151

*anya anya avatāra-kāle bhakta yata*  
*vraja-rase sabe mātāiba kari’ rata*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I will completely madden all the devotees of My previous** ***avatāras*** **with the** ***rasa*** **of Vraja.**

### Text 152

*śrī-rādhikā prema-baddha āmāra hṛdaya*  
*tāṅra bhāva kānti laye ha-iba udaya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘My heart is bound by the love of Śrī Rādhikā. I will take Her heart and halo, and appear.**

### Text 153

*kibā sukha rādhā pāya āmāre seviyā*  
*sei sukha āsvādiba rādhā-bhāva laiyā*

**Перевод:**

**“‘What sort of happiness does Rādhā feel by serving Me? Taking Her heart, I will relish that happiness.**

### Text 154

*āji haite tumi mora śiṣyatā labhibe*  
*haridāsa rūpe more satata sevibe’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘From today, you are My disciple, and as Haridāsa, you will serve Me eternally.’**

### Text 155

*eta bali’ mahāprabhu haila antardhāna*  
*āchāḍiyā paḍe brahmā ha-iyā ajñāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Saying this, Mahāprabhu disappeared, and Brahmā fell heavily to the ground unconscious.**

### Text 156

*‘hā gaurāṅga dīna-bandhu bhakata-vatsala*  
*kabe vā pāiba tava caraṇa-kamala’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Gaurāṅga! O Friend of the fallen! O affectionate Lord of the devotees! When will I reach Your lotus feet?’**

### Text 157

*ei mata kata dina kāṅdite kāṅdite*  
*brahma-loke gela brahmā kārya sampādite”*

**Перевод:**

**“After crying and crying in this way for some time, Brahmā returned to his abode to perform his duties.”**

### Text 158

*nitāi-jāhnavā-pade āśā mātra yāra*  
*nadīyā-māhātmya gāya dīna hīna chāra*

**Перевод:**

**This lowly, fallen, insignificant soul, who aspires only for the feet of Nitāi and Jāhnavā, chants the glories of Nadīyā.**

## Chapter Six – Visiting Śrī Gaṅgā Nagara, Śrī Pṛthu Kuṇḍa, Śrī Sīmantadvīpa, Śrī Viśrāma Sthāna, and Other Place

### Text 1

*jaya jaya śrī-caitanya śacīra nandana*  
*jaya nityānanda prabhu jāhnavā-jīvana*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to the son of Śacī, Śrī Caitanya! All glory to the life of Jāhnavā, Nityānanda Prabhu!**

### Text 2

*jaya jaya sītānātha jaya gadādhara*  
*jaya jaya śrīvāsādi gaura-parikara*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to the Lord of Sītā Devī, Advaita Ācārya! All glory to Gadādhara, Śrīvāsa, and all the Lord’s associates!**

### Text 3

*para-dina prāte prabhu nityānanda-rāya*  
*śrīvāsa śrī-jīva laye gṛha bāhirāya*

**Перевод:**

**The next day in the morning, Nityānanda Prabhu left the Temple with Śrīvāsa and Śrī Jīva.**

### Text 4

*saṅge cale rāma-dāsa ādi bhakta-gaṇa*  
*yāite yāite kare gaura-saṅkīrtana*

**Перевод:**

**Rāmadāsa and other devotees accompanied them, and they chanted the Name of Gaura as they walked.**

### Text 5

*antardvīpa prānte prabhu āilā yakhana*  
*śrī-gaṅgā-nagara jīve dekhāya takhana*

**Перевод:**

**When the Lord came to the boundary of Antardvīpa, He showed Śrī Gaṅgā Nagara to Jīva.**

### Text 6

*prabhu bale, “śuna jīva e gaṅgā-nagara*  
*sthāpilena bhagīratha raghu-vaṁśa-dhara*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu said, “Listen Jīva, this is Gaṅgā Nagara. It was established by Mahārāja Bhagīratha, a forefather of Lord Rāmacandra.**

### Text 7

*yabe gaṅgā bhāgīrathī āila caliyā*  
*bhagīratha yāya āge śaṅkha bājāiyā*

**Перевод:**

**“When the Gaṅgā, who is also known as Bhāgīrathī, came here, Mahārāja Bhagīratha went ahead of her blowing a conch.**

### Text 8

*navadvīpa-dhāme āsi’ gaṅgā haya sthira*  
*bhagīratha dekhe gaṅgā nā haya bāhira*

**Перевод:**

**“Arriving in Navadvīpa Dhāma, the Gaṅgā stopped, and Mahārāja Bhagīratha saw that she would not proceed.**

### Text 9

*bhayete vihvala haye rājā bhagīratha*  
*gaṅgāra nikaṭe āila phiri’ kata patha*

**Перевод:**

**“Overwhelmed with fear, Mahārāja Bhagīratha retraced his course and came before the Gaṅgā.**

### Text 10

*gaṅgā-nagarete vasi’ tapa ārambhila*  
*tape tuṣṭa haye gaṅgā sākṣāt ha-ila*

**Перевод:**

**“He stayed in Gaṅgā Nagara and began to perform austerities. Pleased by them, Gaṅgā personally appeared before him.**

### Text 11

*bhagīratha bale, ’mātā tumi nāhi gele*  
*pitṛloka uddhāra nā habe kona kāle’*

**Перевод:**

**“Mahārāja Bhagīratha said, ‘O Mother, if you do not continue, my ancestors will never be delivered.’**

### Text 12

*gaṅgā bale, ‘śuna vāchā bhagīratha vīra*  
*kichu dina tumi hethā haye thāka sthira*

**Перевод:**

**“Gaṅgā said, ‘Listen, my child, O heroic Bhagīratha! Peacefully stay here for some time.**

### Text 13

*māgha-māse āsiyāchhi navadvīpa-dhāme*  
*phālgunera śeṣe yāba tava pitṛ-kāme*

**Перевод:**

**“‘We have come to Navadvīpa Dhāma in the month of Māgha. At the end of Phālguna, I will deliver your ancestors.**

### Text 14

*yaṅhāra caraṇa-jala āmi bhagīratha*  
*taṅra nija-dhāme mora pure manoratha*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Bhagīratha, I am the water that washes the feet of the Lord. Here in His abode, I will first fulfil my own desires.**

### Text 15

*phālguna-pūrṇimā-tithi prabhu-janma-dina*  
*sei dina mama vrata āchhe samīcīna*

**Перевод:**

**“‘The full moon day in Phālguna is the birthday of the Lord, and I should perform a rite on that day.**

### Text 16

*sei vrata udyāpana kariyā niścaya*  
*caliba tomāra saṅge nā kariha bhaya’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I will complete this rite, and then certainly I will go with you. Do not be afraid.’**

### Text 17

*e gaṅgā-nagare rājā raghu-kula-pati*  
*phālguna-pūrṇimā-dine karila vasati*

**Перевод:**

**“Mahārāja Bhagīratha resided in Gaṅgā Nagara until the full moon day in Phālguna.**

### Text 18-19

*yei jana śrī-phālguna-pūrṇimā-divase*  
*gaṅgā-snāna kari’ gaṅgā-nagarete vase*

*śrī-gaurāṅga pūjā kare upavāsa kari’*  
*pūrva-puruṣera saha sei yāya tari’*

**Перевод:**

**“Anyone who stays in Gaṅgā Nagara on the full moon day in Phālguna, bathes in the Gaṅgā, fasts, and worships Śrī Gaurāṅga crosses over material existence, as do their ancestors.**

### Text 20

*sahasra puruṣa pūrva-gaṇa saṅge kari’*  
*śrī-goloka prāpta haya yathā tathā mari’*

**Перевод:**

**“Along with one thousand of their ancestors, they attain Śrī Goloka, regardless of how or where they died.**

### Text 21

*ohe jīva e sthānera māhātmya apāra*  
*śrī-caitanya nṛtya yathā kaila kata-bāra*

**Перевод:**

**“O Jīva, the glories of this place are unlimited. Śrī Caitanya danced here many times.**

### Text 22

*gaṅgā-dāsa-gṛhe āra sañjaya-ālaya*  
*ai dekha dṛṣṭa haya sadā sukhamaya*

**Перевод:**

**“See there the homes of Gaṅgā Dāsa Paṇḍita and Sañjaya Dāsa, which are always filled with joy.**

### Text 23

*ihāra pūrvete yei dīrghikā sundara*  
*tāhāra māhātmya śuna ohe vijña-vara*

**Перевод:**

**“O best of the wise, hear now about the glories of the beautiful lake to the east.**

### Text 24

*ballāla-dīrghikā nāma hayechhe ekhana*  
*satya-yuge chila era kata vivaraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“It has now become known as Ballāl Dīrghikā, but it was described in many other ways during Satya-yuga.**

### Text 25-26

*pṛthu nāme mahārājā uccha nīca sthāna*  
*kāṭiyā pṛthivī yabe karila samāna*

*sei kāle ei sthāna samāna karite*  
*mahājyotirmaya prabhā uṭhe caturbhite*

**Перевод:**

**“When Mahārāja Pṛthu was cutting and levelling all the high and low places on the earth, he came to level this place, and an extremely bright light appeared in all directions.**

### Text 27

*karmacāri-gaṇa mahārājāre jānāya*  
*rājā āsi’ jyotiḥ-puñja dekhibare pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“The workmen informed the king, and Mahārāja Pṛthu came and saw the light.**

### Text 28

*śaktyāveśa-avatāra pṛthu-mahāśaya*  
*dhyānete jānila sthāna navadvīpa haya*

**Перевод:**

**“Mahārāja Pṛthu, a** ***śaktyāveśa-avatāra*****, through meditation understood this place to be Navadvīpa.**

### Text 29

*sthānera māhātmya gupta rākhibara tare*  
*ājñā dila, ‘kara kuṇḍa sthāna manohare’*

**Перевод:**

**“To keep the glories of this place hidden, he ordered, ‘Make a beautiful lake here.’**

### Text 30

*ye kuṇḍa karila tāhā pṛthu-kuṇḍa nāme*  
*vikhyāta ha-ila sarva-navadvīpa-dhāme*

**Перевод:**

**“The lake he made, known as Pṛthu Kuṇḍa, became famous all over Navadvīpa Dhāma.**

### Text 31

*svaccha jala pāna kari’ grāma-vāsi-gaṇe*  
*kata sukha pāila tāhā kahiba kemane*

**Перевод:**

**“How can I describe how much joy the villagers felt when they drank the pure water of this lake?**

### Text 32

*pare sei sthāne śrī-lakṣmaṇa sena dhīra*  
*dīrghikā khanana kaila baḍa-i gabhīra*

**Перевод:**

**\|“Later, King Lakṣmaṇa Sen excavated, deepened, and enlarged the lake.**

### Text 33

*nija pitṛlokera uddhāra kari’ āśa*  
*ballāla-dīrghikā nāma karila prakāśa*

**Перевод:**

**“Desiring to deliver his ancestors, he named the lake Ballāl Dīrghikā (after his father, Ballāl Sen).**

### Text 34

*ai dekha uccha-ṭilā dekhite sundara*  
*lakṣmaṇa-senera gṛha bhagna ataḥpara*

**Перевод:**

**“See there that large beautiful hill. There stood the house of Lakṣmaṇa Sen. It has since fallen into ruin.**

### Text 35

*e sakala alaṅkāra mahātīrtha sthāne*  
*rāja-gaṇa kare sadā puṇya upārjane*

**Перевод:**

**“Various kings made all these ornaments at this holy place so that they would continuously accrue good fortune.**

### Text 36

*parete yavana-rāja duṣila e-sthāna*  
*ataeva bhakta-gaṇa nā kare sammāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Later, an uncivilised king desecrated this place, and thus devotees do not worship it.**

### Text 37

*bhūmi mātra supavitra ei sthāne haya*  
*yavana-saṁsarga-bhaye vāsa nā karaya*

**Перевод:**

**“The land in this place is pure, but fearing interaction with the uncivilised people, devotees do not reside here.**

### Text 38

*e sthāne ha-ila śrī-mūrtira apamāna*  
*ataeva bhakta-vṛnda chāḍe ei sthāna”*

**Перевод:**

**“Offences were made against Deities in this place, and thus devotees left.”**

### Text 39

*eta bali’ nityānanda garjite garjite*  
*āilena simuliyā grāma sannihite*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this, Nityānanda roared. He then came to Simuliyā Grāma.**

### Text 40

*simuliyā dekhi’ prabhu jīva prati kaya*  
*“ei ta sīmantadvīpa jāniha niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing Simuliyā, Nityānanda Prabhu said to Jīva, “Know for certain that this is Sīmantadvīpa.**

### Text 41

*gaṅgāra dakṣiṇa tīre navadvīpa prānte*  
*sīmanta nāmete dvīpa bale saba śānte*

**Перевод:**

**“The** ***sādhus*** **call the island on the border of Navadvīpa and the southern bank of the Gaṅgā ‘Sīmanta’.**

### Text 42

*kāle ei dvīpa gaṅgā grāsibe sakala*  
*rahibe kevala eka sthāna sunirmala*

**Перевод:**

**“In time, the Gaṅgā will completely cover this island, and only one pure place will remain.**

### Text 43

*yathāya simulī nāme pārvatī pūjana*  
*karibe viṣayī loka karaha śravaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“Hear now about how materialistic people will worship Pārvatī in that place, which is known as Simulī.**

### Text 44

*kona kāle satya-yuge deva maheśvara*  
*‘śrī-gaurāṅga’ bali’ nṛtya karila vistara*

**Перевод:**

**“Once, during Satya-yuga, the god of gods, Lord Śiva, was chanting the Name of Śrī Gaurāṅga and dancing.**

### Text 45

*pārvatī jijñāse tabe deva maheśvare*  
*‘kebā se gaurāṅga-deva balaha āmāre*

**Перевод:**

**“Pārvatī asked him, ‘Who is this golden Lord? Please tell me.**

### Text 46

*tomāra adbhūta nṛtya kari’ daraśana*  
*śuniyā gaurāṅga-nāma gale mora mana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Seeing your wonderful dancing and hearing the Name ‘Gaurāṅga’, my heart melts.**

### Text 47

*eta ye śunechi mantra-tantra eta-kāla*  
*se saba jāninu mātra jīvera jañjāla*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I have understood that all the** ***mantras*** **and** ***tantra*** **I have heard about for so long are simply misery for the soul.**

### Text 48

*ataeva bala prabhu gaurāṅga sandhāna*  
*bhajiyā tāhāre āmi pāiba parāṇa’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘So, please tell me about Lord Gaurāṅga. Serving Him, I will attain new life.’**

### Text 49

*pārvatīra kathā śuni’ deva paśupati*  
*śrī-gaurāṅga smari’ kahe pārvatīra prati*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing Pārvatī’s words and remembering Śrī Gaurāṅga, Lord Śiva spoke to Pārvatī.**

### Text 50

*‘ādyā-śakti tumi hao śrī-rādhāra aṁśa*  
*tomāre baliba tattva-gaṇa avataṁsa*

**Перевод:**

**“‘You are the primordial energy and an expansion of Śrī Rādhā. I will tell you the crown jewel of truths.**

### Text 51

*rādhā-bhāva laye kṛṣṇa kalite e-bāra*  
*māyāpure śacī-garbhe habe avatāra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘This time, during Kali-yuga, Kṛṣṇa will take the heart of Rādhā and appear in Māyāpura in the womb of Śacī Devī.**

### Text 52

*kīrtana-raṅgete māti’ prabhu gorāmaṇi*  
*vitaribe prema-ratna pātra nāhi gaṇi’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Maddened by His Pastimes of** ***kīrtana*****, Lord Gorāmaṇi will distribute the jewel of divine love without judging its recipients.**

### Text 53

*ei prema-vanyā-jale ye jīva nā bhāse*  
*dhik tāra bhāgye devī jīvana vilāse*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O goddess, shame on the life, enjoyment, and fortune of the souls who do not float in the waters of this flood of divine love!**

### Text 54

*prabhura pratijñā smari’ preme yāi bhāsi’*  
*dhairaya nā dhare mana chāḍilāma kāśī*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Remembering the promise of the Lord, I float in divine love. My heart has lost its composure, and thus I have left Kāśī.**

### Text 55

*māyāpura antarbhāge jāhnavīra tīre*  
*gaurāṅga bhajiba āmi rahiyā kuṭīre’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I will stay in a hut on the bank of the Gaṅgā in Māyāpura and worship Gaurāṅga.’**

### Text 56

*dhūrjaṭira vākya śuni’ pārvatī sundarī*  
*āilena sīmantadvīpete tvarā kari’*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing Śiva’s words, beautiful Pārvatī quickly came to Sīmantadvīpa.**

### Text 57

*śrī-gaurāṅga-rūpa sadā karena cintana*  
*‘gaura’ bali’ preme bhāse sthira nahe mana*

**Перевод:**

**“She constantly meditated on the form of Śrī Gaurāṅga. Chanting Gaura’s Name she floated in divine love, and her heart became unsteady.**

### Text 58

*kata-dine gauracandra kṛpā vitariyā*  
*pārvatīre dekhā dilā sagaṇe āsiyā*

**Перевод:**

**“Eventually, Gauracandra bestowed His mercy; He came with His associates and appeared to Pārvatī.**

### Text 59

*sutapta kāñcana-varṇa dīrgha kalevara*  
*māthāya caṅcara keśa sarvāṅga sundara*

**Перевод:**

**“His long form was the colour of molten gold, and His limbs and the wavy hair on His head appeared very beautiful.**

### Text 60

*trikaccha kariyā vastra tāra paridhāna*  
*gale dole phula-mālā apūrva vidhāna*

**Перевод:**

**“He wore a** ***dhotī*** **tucked in three places, and a wonderful flower garland swung on His neck.**

### Text 61

*preme gadagada vākya kahe gaura-rāya*  
*‘balago pārvatī! kena āile hethāya’*

**Перевод:**

**“Stammering in divine love, Gaura Rāya said, ‘O Pārvatī! Why have you come here?’**

### Text 62

*jagatera prabhu-pade paḍiyā pārvatī*  
*jānāya āpana duḥkha sthira nahe mati*

**Перевод:**

**“Pārvatī bowed at the feet of the Lord of the universe, and with an unsteady mind, explained her sadness.**

### Text 63

*‘ohe prabhu jagannātha jagata-jīvana*  
*sakalera dayāmaya mora viḍambana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Master! O Lord and Life of the universe! You are merciful to everyone, but cruel to me.**

### Text 64

*tava bahirmukha jīve bandhana kāraṇa*  
*niyukta karila more patita-pāvana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Saviour of the fallen, You have engaged me in imprisoning the souls who are averse to You.**

### Text 65

*āmi thāki sei kāje saṁsāra pātiyā*  
*tomāra ananta preme vañcita ha-iyā*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I perform this duty and manage the material world, but I am deprived of Your infinite divine love.**

### Text 66

*loke bale yathā kṛṣṇa māyā nāhi tathā*  
*āmi tabe bahirmukha ha-inu sarvathā*

**Перевод:**

**“‘People say that Māyā is absent wherever Kṛṣṇa is present. Thus, I have become completely estranged from You.**

### Text 67

*kemane dekhiba prabhu tomāra vilāsa*  
*tumi nā karile patha ha-inu nirāśa’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Lord, how will I see Your Pastimes? If You do not make a way for me, I will be hopeless.’**

### Text 68

*eta bali’ śrī-pārvatī gaura-pada-dhūli*  
*sīmante la-ila satī kariyā ākuli*

**Перевод:**

**“Saying this, chaste Pārvatī Devī desperately rubbed Gaura’s foot-dust into the parting in her hair.**

### Text 69

*sei haite śrī-sīmantadvīpa nāma haila*  
*simuliyā bali’ ajña-janete kahila*

**Перевод:**

**“Since then, this place has been known as Sīmantadvīpa, though the ignorant call it Simuliyā.**

### Text 70

*śrī-gaurāṅga-candra tabe prasanna ha-iyā*  
*balila, “pārvatī śuna kathā mana diyā*

**Перевод:**

**“Moon-like Śrī Gaurāṅga was pleased and said, ‘O Pārvatī, listen to My words attentively.**

### Text 71

*tumi mora bhinna nao śakti sarveśvarī*  
*eka śakti dui rūpa mama sahacarī*

**Перевод:**

**“‘You are not separate from Me. You are My energy and the goddess of all. My consort, My singular energy, has two forms.**

### Text 72

*svarūpa-śaktite tumi rādhikā āmāra*  
*bahiraṅgā-rūpe rādhā tomāte vistāra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘As My personal energy, you are Rādhikā. As My external energy, Rādhā expands as you.**

### Text 73

*tumi naile mora līlā siddha nāhi haya*  
*tumi yogamāyā-rūpe līlāte niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Without you, My Pastimes are not complete. As Yogamāyā, you are certainly involved in My Pastimes.**

### Text 74

*vraje tumi paurṇamāsī-rūpe nitya kāla*  
*navadvīpe prauḍhā-māyā saha kṣetra-pāla’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘In Vraja, you are eternally present as Paurṇamāsī, and in Navadvīpa, you are present as Prauḍhā Māyā with Lord Śiva, the guardian of the Dhāma.’**

### Text 75

*eta bali’ śrī-gaurāṅga haila adarśana*  
*premāviṣṭa haye rahe pārvatīra mana*

**Перевод:**

**“Saying this, Śrī Gaurāṅga disappeared, and Pārvatī’s heart became overwhelmed with divine love.**

### Text 76

*simantinī-devī-rūpe rahe eka bhite*  
*prauḍhā-māyā māyāpure rahe gaura-prīte”*

**Перевод:**

**“Out of love for Śrī Gaurāṅga, Pārvatī remains here as the goddess of Sīmantadvīpa and in Māyāpura as Prauḍhā Māyā.”**

### Text 77

*eta bali’ nityānanda kājira nagare*  
*praveśila jīve laye takhana satvare*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this, Nityānanda quickly entered Kāzī Nagara with Jīva.**

### Text 78

*prabhu bale, “ohe jīva śunaha vacana*  
*kājira nagara ei mathurā bhuvana*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu said, “O Jīva, listen to My words. Kāzī Nagara is Mathurā.**

### Text 79

*hethā śrī-gaurāṅga-rāya kīrtana kariyā*  
*kāji nistārila prabhu prema-ratna diyā*

**Перевод:**

**“Here, Śrī Gaurāṅga performed** ***kīrtana*****, delivered the Kāzī, and gave him the jewel of divine love.**

### Text 80

*śrī-kṛṣṇa-līlāya yei kaṁsa mathurāya*  
*gaurāṅga-līlāya caṅda-kāji nāma pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“He who was Kaṁsa during Kṛṣṇa’s Pastimes in Mathurā became known as Caṅda Kāzī during Gaurāṅga’s Pastimes.**

### Text 81

*ei-janya prabhu tāre mātula balila*  
*bhaye kāji gaura-pade śaraṇa la-ila*

**Перевод:**

**“The Lord thus called the Kāzī His uncle. Out of fear, the Kāzī took shelter at Gaurāṅga’s feet.**

### Text 82

*kīrtana ārambhe kāji mṛdaṅga bhāṅgila*  
*hosena sāhāra bale utpāta karila*

**Перевод:**

**“When Gaurāṅga began performing** ***kīrtana*****, the Kāzī broke a** ***mṛdaṅga*** **and made a disturbance on the order of Nawab Hussein Shah.**

### Text 83

*hosenasā se jarāsandha gauḍa-rājyeśvara*  
*taṅhāra ātmīya kāji pratāpa vistara*

**Перевод:**

**“Hussein Shah, the emperor of Gauḍa at that time, was previously Jarāsandha. He influenced the Kāzī, who was his relative.**

### Text 84

*prabhu tāre nṛsiṁha-rūpete deya bhaya*  
*bhaye kaṁsa-sama kāji jaḍasaḍa haya*

**Перевод:**

**“In the form of Nṛsiṁha, the Lord frightened the Kāzī, and like Kaṁsa, the Kāzī cringed in fear.**

### Text 85

*tāre prema diyā kaila vaiṣṇava-pradhāna*  
*kājira nistāra kathā śune bhāgyavān*

**Перевод:**

**“The Lord then gave him divine love and made him a great devotee. Those who hear of the Kāzī’s deliverance are fortunate.**

### Text 86-87

*vraja-tattva navadvīpa-tattve dekha bheda*  
*kṛṣṇa-aparādhī labhe nirvāṇa abheda*

*hethā aparādhī pāya prema-ratna-dhana*  
*ataeva gaura-līlā sarvopari hana*

**Перевод:**

**“See the difference between Vraja and Navadvīpa: in Vraja, those who offend Kṛṣṇa attain static liberation, but in Navadvīpa, offenders attain the jewel of divine love. Thus, the Pastimes of Gaura are supreme.**

### Text 88

*gaura-dhāma gaura-nāma gaura-rūpa-guṇa*  
*aparādha nāhi māne tārite nipuṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“Gaura’s Abode, Name, Form, and Qualities do not consider offences and are adept at delivering souls.**

### Text 89

*yadi aparādha thāke sādhakera mane*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāme kṛṣṇa-dhāme tāre bahu-dine*

**Перевод:**

**“If offences are present in the mind of a practitioner, Kṛṣṇa’s Name and abode deliver them after a long time.**

### Text 90

*gaura-nāme gaura-dhāme sadya prema haya*  
*aparādha nāhi tāra bādhā upajaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Gaura’s Name and abode, however, immediately give souls divine love; offences do not obstruct them.**

### Text 91

*ai dekha ohe jīva kājira samādhi*  
*dekhile jīvera nāśa haya ādhi vyādhi”*

**Перевод:**

**“O Jīva, see there the** ***samādhi*** **of the Kāzī. Seeing it dispels sickness and sorrow.”**

### Text 92

*eta bali’ nityānanda preme garagara*  
*calilena druta śaṅkha-vaṇika-nagara*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this, Nityānanda, smouldering with divine love, quickly went to Śaṅkha Vaṇika Nagara.**

### Text 93

*tathā giyā śrī-jīvere balena vacana*  
*“oi dekha śaraḍāṅgā apūrva darśana*

**Перевод:**

**Arriving there, He said to Śrī Jīva, “See there the wonderful sight of Śaraḍāṅgā.**

### Text 94

*śrī-śaraḍāṅgā nāma ati manohara*  
*jagannātha vaise yathā la-iyā śabara*

**Перевод:**

**“The name Śaraḍāṅgā is also very beautiful. Jagannātha resides there with the** ***śabaras*****.**

### Text 95

*pūrve yabe raktabāhu daurātmya karila*  
*dayitā sahita prabhu hethāya āila*

**Перевод:**

**“Previously, when a demon named Raktabāhu was on a rampage, the Lord came here with the** ***śabaras*****.**

### Text 96

*śrī-puruṣottama sama ei dhāma haya*  
*nitya jagannātha-sthiti tathāya niścaya”*

**Перевод:**

**“This place is non-different from Śrī Puruṣottama Kṣetra; Jagannātha certainly resides here eternally.”**

### Text 97

*tabe tantuvāya-grāma ha-ilena pāra*  
*dekhilena kholā-vecā śrīdhara-āgāra*

**Перевод:**

**They then passed through Tantuvāya Grāma and saw the cottage of Kholāvecā Śrīdhara.**

### Text 98

*prabhu bale, “ei sthāne śrī-gaurāṅga hari*  
*kīrtana viśrāma kaila bhakte kṛpā kari’*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu said, “Here, Śrī Gaurāṅga chanted, rested, and blessed the devotees.**

### Text 99

*ei hetu viśrāma-sthāna era nāma*  
*hethā śrīdharera ghare karaha viśrāma*

**Перевод:**

**“This place thus became known as Viśrāma Sthāna. You may rest here in Śrīdhara’s cottage.”**

### Text 100

*śrīdhara śunila yabe prabhu-āgamana*  
*sāṣṭāṅge āsiyā kare prabhura pūjana*

**Перевод:**

**When Śrīdhara heard of Nityānanda Prabhu’s arrival, he came, prostrated himself, and worshipped the Lord.**

### Text 101

*bale, “prabhu baḍa dayā e dāsera prati*  
*viśrāma karaha hethā āmāra minati”*

**Перевод:**

**He said, “O Lord, be most merciful to this servant and rest here. This is my request.”**

### Text 102

*prabhu bale, “tumi hao ati bhāgyavān*  
*tomāre karila kṛpā gaura bhagavān*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu replied, “You are very fortunate: Lord Gaurāṅga has bestowed His mercy upon you.**

### Text 103

*adya morā e sthāne kariba viśrāma”*  
*śuniyā śrīdhara tabe haya āpta-kāma*

**Перевод:**

**“Today, we will rest here.” Hearing this, Śrīdhara was satisfied.**

### Text 104

*bahu-yatne sevā-yogya sāmagrī la-iyā*  
*randhana karāya bhakta brāhmaṇere diyā*

**Перевод:**

**With great care, Śrīdhara collected suitable ingredients and engaged a Vaiṣṇava** ***brāhmaṇa*** **to cook.**

### Text 105

*nitāi śrīvāsa sevā haile samāpana*  
*ānande prasāda pāya śrī-jīva takhana*

**Перевод:**

**Once Nitāi and Śrīvāsa had been served, Śrī Jīva joyfully took their** ***prasāda*****.**

### Text 106

*nityānande khaṭṭopari karāya śayana*  
*savaṁśe śrīdhara kare pāda-samvāhana*

**Перевод:**

**Śrīdhara laid Nityānanda on a bed, and then he and his family massaged the Lord’s feet.**

### Text 107

*aparāhne śrī-jīvere la-iyā śrīvāsa*  
*ṣaṣṭi-tīrtha dekhāila ha-iyā ullāsa*

**Перевод:**

**In the afternoon, Śrīvāsa took Śrī Jīva and joyfully showed him Ṣaṣṭi Tīrtha.**

### Text 108-109

*śrīvāsa kahila, “śuna jīva sad-āśaya*  
*pūrve deva-gaṇa yabe śunila niścaya*

*navadvīpe habe mahāprabhu avatāra*  
*viśvakarmā āilena nadīyā nagara*

**Перевод:**

**Śrīvāsa said, “Listen, O pure-hearted Jīva! Previously, when the gods heard that Mahāprabhu would descend in Navadvīpa, Viśvakarmā came to Nadia.**

### Text 110-111

*prabhu yei pathe karibena saṅkīrtana*  
*sei pathe jalakaṣṭa karite vāraṇa*

*eka rātre ṣāṭa kuṇḍa kāṭila viśāi*  
*śeṣa kuṇḍa kāji-grāme karila kāṭāi*

**Перевод:**

**“To prevent a scarcity of water along the paths where the Lord would perform** ***saṅkīrtana*****, Viśvakarmā dug sixty large ponds in one night, the last of which he made in the village of the Kāzī.**

### Text 112

*śrīdharera kalā-bāga dekhite sundara*  
*ihāra nikaṭa eka dekha sarovara*

**Перевод:**

**“Śrīdhara’s banana grove is beautiful to see. See also the lake nearby.**

### Text 113

*ei sarovare kabhu kari’ jala-khelā*  
*mahāprabhu la-ilena śrīdharera kholā*

**Перевод:**

**“Mahāprabhu sometimes played in the water there and took Śrīdhara’s bananas.**

### Text 114

*adyāvadhi mocā thoḍa la-iyā śrīdhara*  
*śrī-śacī-mātāke deya ullāsa antara*

**Перевод:**

**“Still today, Śrīdhara collects banana flowers and stems, and gives them to Mother Śacī with joy in his heart.**

### Text 115

*ihāra nikaṭe mayāmāri nāma sthāna*  
*dekhaha śrī-jīva ājo āchhe vidyamāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Near here is a place called Mayāmāri. O Jīva, see that it still exists there today.**

### Text 116-117

*paurāṇika kathā eka karaha śravaṇa*  
*tīrthā-yātrā baladeva karila yakhana*

*navadvīpe āsi’ yabe karila viśrāma*  
*vipra-gaṇa jānāila mayāsura nāma*

**Перевод:**

**“Please hear a story from the** ***Purāṇas*****: once when Baladeva was on a pilgrimage, He came to Navadvīpa and rested. At that time, some** ***brāhmaṇas*** **told Him about a demon named Maya.**

### Text 118

*mayāsura-upadrava śuni haladhara*  
*mahāvege dhare tāre māṭhera bhitara*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing of the havoc raised by Mayāsura, Baladeva furiously confronted him in a field.**

### Text 119

*mahāyuddha kaila daitya baladeva sātha*  
*avaśeṣe rāma tāre karila nipāta*

**Перевод:**

**“The demon fought with Baladeva ferociously, and finally Baladeva killed him.**

### Text 120

*se avadhi mayāmāri nāma khyāta haila*  
*bahu-kāla kathā āja tomāre kahila*

**Перевод:**

**“Since then, this place has become known as Mayāmāri. Today, I have told you a very old story.**

### Text 121

*tālavana nāma ei tīrtha vraja-pure*  
*sadā bhāgyavān jana nayanete sphure”*

**Перевод:**

**“This place is known as Tālavana in Vraja and always appears before the eyes of the fortunate souls.”**

### Text 122

*sei rātre sei sthāne thākilena sabe*  
*para-dina yātrā kare “hari hari” rabe*

**Перевод:**

**That night, they stayed there, and the next day they continued on, chanting, “Hari! Hari!”**

### Text 123

*nitāi-jāhnavā-pada-chāyā yāra āśa*  
*nadīyā-māhātmya kare e dāsa prakāśa*

**Перевод:**

**This servant, whose aspiration is the shade of Nitāi and Jāhnavā’s feet, reveals the glories of Nadīyā.**

## Chapter Seven – Śrī Suvarṇa Bihāra and Śrī Deva Pallī

### Text 1

*jaya śrī-caitanya-candra jaya prabhu nityānanda*  
*jayādvaita jaya gadādhara*  
*jaya śrīvāsādi bhakta gaura-pade anurakta*  
*jaya navadvīpa-dhāma-vara*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to the moon-like Śrī Caitanya, Nityānanda Prabhu, Advaita Ācārya, Gadādhara Paṇḍita, Śrīvāsa Paṇḍita, all the devotees attached to the feet of the Lord, and the best of abodes, Śrī Navadvīpa!**

### Text 2

*chāḍiyā viśrāma-sthāna śrī-jīve la-iyā yāna*  
*yathā grāma suvarṇa-bihāra*  
*“ohe jīva”, prabhu kaya, “apūrva e-sthāna haya*  
*navadvīpa prakṛtira pāra*

**Перевод:**

**Leaving Viśrāma Sthāna, with Śrī Jīva the party proceeded to the village known as Suvarṇa Bihāra. There, Nityānanda Prabhu said, “O Jīva, this is a wonderful place. Like all of Navadvīpa, it is beyond material nature.**

### Text 3

*satya-yuge ei-sthāne chila rājā sabe jāne*  
*śrī-suvarṇa sena tāra nāma*  
*bahu-kāla rājya kaila parete vārdhakya haila*  
*tabu nāhi kāryete viśrāma*

**Перевод:**

**“Here, in Satya-yuga, there was a king known by all as Śrī Suvarṇa Sen. He ruled for a long time. Eventually he grew old, but he did not retire from his duties.**

### Text 4

*viṣaye āviṣṭa-citta kise vṛddhi haya vitta*  
*ei cintā kare nara-vara*  
*ki jāni ki bhāgya-vaśe śrī-nārada tathā āise*  
*rājā taṅre pūjila vistara*

**Перевод:**

**“His mind absorbed in mundane affairs, the king thought only of how to accumulate more wealth. As a result of some unknown fortune, Śrī Nārada came here, and the king worshipped him elaborately.**

### Text 5

*nāradera dayā haila tattva upadeśa kaila*  
*rājāre ta la-iyā nirjane*  
*nārada kahena, ‘rāya vṛthā tava dina yāya*  
*artha-cintā kari’ mane mane*

**Перевод:**

**“Nārada blessed the King. He then met with him privately and instructed him in the Absolute Truth. Nārada said, ‘O King, your days pass in vain as you think only of wealth within your mind.**

### Text 6

*arthake anartha jāna paramārtha divya jñāna*  
*hṛdaye bhāvaha eka-bāra*  
*dārā-putra-bandhu-jana keha nahe nija-jana*  
*maraṇete keha nahe kāra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Understand that material wealth is meaningless and that divine knowledge is true wealth. Once consider within your heart that your wife, sons, and friends do not belong to you. After death, no one belongs to anyone.**

### Text 7

*tomāra maraṇa hale dehaṭi bhāsāye jale*  
*sabe yābe gṛhe āpanāra*  
*tabe kena mithyā āśā viṣaya-jala-pipāsā*  
*yadi keha nāhi haila kāra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘When you die, everyone will set your body afloat down the river and then go back to their houses. So, why this false hope? Why this thirst for the water of sense objects, when no one actually belongs to anyone?**

### Text 8

*yadi bala, “labhi sukha jīvane nā pāi duḥkha*  
*ataeva artha-ceṣṭā kari”*  
*seha mithyā kathā rāya jīvana anitya haya*  
*nāhi rahe śata-varṣopari*

**Перевод:**

**“‘You may say, “I enjoy life; I do not suffer. Therefore, I endeavour for wealth.” O King, such words are false. Life is temporary and does not last more than a hundred years.**

### Text 9

*ataeva jāna sāra yete habe māyāpāra*  
*yathā sukhe duḥkha nāhi haya*  
*kise vā sādhiba bala sei ta apūrva phala*  
*yāhe nāhi śoka duḥkha bhaya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘So, understand the essence: we must cross over** ***māyā*** **to the land where happiness does not produce sadness. Tell me, how will we reach that wonderful land where there is no lamentation, sadness, or fear?**

### Text 10

*kevala vairāgya kari’ tāhā nā pāite pāri*  
*kevala jñānete tāhā nāi*  
*vairāgya jñānera bale viṣaya-bandhana gale*  
*jīvera kaivalya haya bhāi*

**Перевод:**

**“‘We cannot attain it by mere renunciation or knowledge. Brother! Through renunciation and knowledge, material bondage falls away, and souls enter oneness.**

### Text 11

*kaivalye ānanda nāi sarva-nāśa bali tāi*  
*kaivalyera nitānta dhikkāra*  
*e-dike viṣaya gela śreṣṭha kichu nā milila*  
*kaivalyera karaha vicāra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘There is no joy in oneness; we consider oneness total ruination. Oneness is utterly condemned. Understand oneness: by it, material things disappear, but nothing superior is attained.**

### Text 12

*ataeva jñānī-jana bhukti mukti nāhi lana*  
*kṛṣṇa-bhakti karena sādhana*  
*viṣayete anāsakti kṛṣṇa-pade anurakti*  
*sambandhābhidheya prayojana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Thus, the wise do not accept enjoyment or liberation—they practise devotion to Kṛṣṇa. To be detached from mundanity and attached to Kṛṣṇa is the relationship, the process, and the goal.**

### Text 13

*jīva se kṛṣṇera dāsa bhakti vinā sarva-nāśa*  
*bhakti-vṛkṣe phale prema-phala*  
*sei phala prayojana kṛṣṇa-prema nitya-dhana*  
*bhukti mukti tuchha se sakala*

**Перевод:**

**“‘The soul is a servant of Kṛṣṇa, and without devotion, the soul is utterly ruined. The fruit of divine love for Kṛṣṇa grows on the tree of devotion; that fruit—the eternal wealth of the soul—is the goal. Enjoyment and liberation are both insignificant.**

### Text 14

*kṛṣṇa cid-ānanda ravi māyā taṅra chāyā chabi*  
*jīva taṅra kiraṇāṇu-gaṇa*  
*taṭastha dharmera vaśe jīva yadi māyā sparśe*  
*māyā tāre karaye bandhana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Kṛṣṇa is the joyful, spiritual sun, Māyā is His shadow, and the souls are His atomic rays. If souls, governed by their marginal nature, touch Māyā, then Māyā binds them.**

### Text 15

*kṛṣṇa bahirmukha yei māyā-sparśī jīva sei*  
*māyā-sparśe karma-saṅga pāya*  
*māyā-jāle bhrami’ mare karma jñāne nāhi tare*  
*kaṣṭa-nāśa mantraṇā karāya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘The souls that turn away from Kṛṣṇa touch Māyā, and thus become bound in karma. They wander and die in Māyā’s prison. Neither action nor knowledge help them, and Māyā causes them to invite in their own suffering and ruin.**

### Text 16

*kabhu karma ācaraya aṣṭāṅgādi yogamaya*  
*kabhu brahma-jñāna ālocana*  
*kabhu kabhu tarka kare avaśeṣe nāhi tare*  
*nāhi māne ātma-tattva-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Souls sometimes perform action, sometimes practise** ***aṣṭāṅga-yoga*****, sometimes discuss theory of Brahma, and sometimes argue. Ultimately, none of these things help them, however, and they fail to appreciate the wealth of the self.**

### Text 17

*bhramite bhramite yabe bhakta-jana-saṅga habe*  
*tabe śraddhā labhibe nirmala*  
*sādhu-saṅge kṛṣṇa bhaji’ hṛdaya-anartha tyaji’*  
*niṣṭhā lābha kare suvimala*

**Перевод:**

**“‘After wandering and wandering in this way, when souls attain the association of devotees, they develop pure faith. By serving Kṛṣṇa in the association of the** ***sādhus*****, souls free their hearts from impediments and attain pure constancy.**

### Text 18

*bhajite bhajite tabe sei niṣṭhā ruci habe*  
*krame ruci ha-ibe āsakti*  
*āsakti ha-ite bhāva tāhe habe prema-lābha*  
*ei krame haya śuddha bhakti*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Then, as they continue to serve, their constancy develops into attraction and, gradually, attachment to Kṛṣṇa. From attachment, feeling develops, and thereafter divine love. Pure devotion awakens in these stages.**

### Text 19

*śravaṇa kīrtana mati sevā kṛṣṇārcchana nati*  
*dāsya sakhya ātma-nivedana*  
*navadhā sādhana ei bhakta-saṅge kare yei*  
*sei labhe kṛṣṇa-prema-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Hearing, chanting, remembering, serving, worshipping Kṛṣṇa, bowing, following, befriending, and surrendering—souls who engage in these nine practices in the association of devotees attain the wealth of divine love for Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 20

*tumi rājā bhāgyavān navadvīpe tava sthāna*  
*dhāma-vāse tava bhāgyodaya*  
*sādhu-saṅge śraddhā peye kṛṣṇa-nāma-guṇa geye*  
*prema-sūrya karāo udaya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O King, you are fortunate. You reside in Navadvīpa, and your fortune has arisen by residing in the Dhāma. So, in the association of** ***sādhus*****, develop faith, chant Kṛṣṇa’s Name and glories, and thus cause the sun of divine love to rise.**

### Text 21

*dhanya kali āgamane hethā kṛṣṇa laye gaṇe*  
*śrī-gaurāṅga-līlā prakāśibe*  
*yei gaura-nāma labe tāte kṛṣṇa-kṛpā habe*  
*vraje vāsa sei ta karibe*

**Перевод:**

**“‘The coming of Kali-yuga is glorious! Kṛṣṇa will manifest His Pastimes as Śrī Gaurāṅga with His associates here, and those who chant the Name of Gaura will receive the mercy of Kṛṣṇa and reside in Vraja.**

### Text 22

*gaura-nāma nā la-iyā yei kṛṣṇa bhaje giyā*  
*sei kṛṣṇa bahu-kāle pāya*  
*gaura-nāma laya yei sadya kṛṣṇa pāya sei*  
*aparādha nāhi rahe tāya’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Those who serve Kṛṣṇa but do not chant the Name of Gaura attain Kṛṣṇa after a long time, but those who chant the Name of Gaura immediately attain Kṛṣṇa—offences do not remain in them.’**

### Text 23

*balite balite muni adhairya haya amani*  
*nācite lāgila ‘gaura’ bali’*  
*‘gaurahari’ bola dhari’ vīṇā bale gaurahari*  
*‘kabe se āsibe dhanya kali’*

**Перевод:**

**“As he spoke, Nārada Muni suddenly became overwhelmed. He began to dance, chanting, ‘Gaurahari!’, and as he did so, his** ***vīṇā*** **also chanted ‘Gaurahari!’ He mused, ‘When will the glorious Age of Kali arrive?’**

### Text 24

*ei saba bali’ tāya nārada caliyā yāya*  
*premodaya ha-ila rājāra*  
*‘gaurāṅga’ baliyā nāce sādhu haite prema yāce*  
*viṣaya-vāsanā ghuce taṅra*

**Перевод:**

**“Having spoken to the king, Nārada left, and the king’s divine love arose. Chanting ‘Gaurāṅga’, the king danced and begged for divine love from the** ***sādhus*****. His material desires disappeared.**

### Text 25

*nidrā-kāle nara-vara dekhe gaura gadādhara*  
*sapārṣade taṅhāra aṅgane*  
*nāce ‘hare kṛṣṇa’ bali’ kare sabe kolākuli*  
*suvarṇa-pratimā gaura sane*

**Перевод:**

**“While asleep, the king had a vision of Gaura and Gadādhara with Their associates in his courtyard. Everyone was dancing, chanting ‘Hare Kṛṣṇa’, and embracing in the company of the golden Lord Gaura.**

### Text 26

*nidrā bhāṅgi’ nara-pati kātara ha-ila ati*  
*gaura lāgi’ karaya krandana*  
*daiva-vāṇī haila tāya ‘prakaṭa samaye rāya*  
*habe tumi pārṣade gaṇana*

**Перевод:**

**“When he awoke, the king became deeply dismayed and cried for Gaura. Then he heard a divine voice: ‘O King, when the Lord appears, you will be one of His associates.**

### Text 27

*buddhimanta-khaṅa nāma pāibe he guṇa-dhāma*  
*sevibe gaurāṅga-śrī-caraṇa’*  
*daiva-vāṇī kāṇe śuni’ sthira haila nara-maṇi*  
*kare tabe gaurāṅga bhajana”*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O virtuous King, you will be named Buddhimanta Khaṅa, and you will serve the holy feet of Gaurāṅga.’ Hearing this divine voice, the king became steady and then worshipped Gaurāṅga.”**

### Text 28

*nityānanda-kathā śeṣe nāradera śaktyāveśe*  
*śrīvāsa haila acetana*  
*mahāpremāveśe tabe gaura-nāmāmṛtāsabe*  
*bhūme loṭe śrī-jīva takhana*

**Перевод:**

**When Nityānanda finished speaking, Śrīvāsa became immersed in the mood of Nārada and fainted. Śrī Jīva rolled on the ground, immersed in intense divine love and the intoxicating nectar of Gaura’s Name.**

### Text 29

*“āhā ki gaurāṅga-rāya dekhiba āmi hethāya*  
*suvarṇa putali gorāmaṇi”*  
*balite balite tabe śrī-gaura-kīrtana sabe*  
*nayanete dekhaye amani*

**Перевод:**

**“Oh! Gaurāṅga Rāya! Gorāmaṇi! Will I see Your golden form here?” As Jīva spoke, everyone suddenly saw Śrī Gaura’s** ***kīrtana*** **with their own eyes.**

### Text 30

*āhā se amiya jini’ gaurāṅgera rūpa-khāni*  
*nācite lāgila sei khāne*  
*tabe nityānanda-rāya gaurāṅgera guṇa gāya*  
*advaita sahita sarva-jane*

**Перевод:**

**Oh! The form of Gaurāṅga, which surpasses nectar, began to dance there, and Nityānanda Rāya chanted the glories of Gaurāṅga with Advaita and all the devotees.**

### Text 31

*mṛdaṅga mandirā bāje saṅkīrtana suvirāje*  
*pūrva-līlā ha-ila vistara*  
*kata ye ānanda haya varṇite śakati naya*  
*belā ha-ila dvitīya prahara*

**Перевод:**

***Mṛdaṅgas*** **and cymbals resonated, the** ***saṅkīrtana*** **resounded, and the previous Pastimes of the Lord manifested. I do not have the power to describe the joy that arose and remained there until midday.**

### Text 32

*tabe ta calila sabe gaura-gīta kala-rave*  
*devapallī grāmera bhitara*  
*tathāya viśrāma kaila devera atithi ha-ila*  
*madhyāhna-bhojana ataḥpara*

**Перевод:**

**Eventually, the party continued on, loudly singing songs about Gaurāṅga. They rested in the village of Deva Pallī as guests of the village Deity and then ate at midday.**

### Text 33

*divasera śeṣa yāme sakale bhramaya grāme*  
*prabhu nityānanda tabe kaya*  
*“devapallī ei haya śrī-nṛsiṁha-devālaya*  
*satya-yuga haite paricaya*

**Перевод:**

**In the evening, they toured the village, and then Nityānanda Prabhu said, “This is Deva Pallī. This Temple of Śrī Nṛsiṁhadeva was established in Satya-yuga.**

### Text 34

*prahlādere dayā kari’ hiraṇya badhiyā hari*  
*ei sthāne karila viśrāma*  
*brahmā ādi deva-gaṇa nija nija niketana*  
*kari’ eka vasāila grāma*

**Перевод:**

**“After blessing Prahlāda and killing Hiraṇyakaśipu, the Lord rested here. Brahmā and the other gods made their own homes here and established a village.**

### Text 35

*mandākinī-taṭa dhari’ ṭilāya vasati kari’*  
*nṛsiṁha-sevāya haila rata*  
*śrī-nṛsiṁha-kṣetra nāma navadvīpe ei dhāma*  
*parama-pāvana śāstra-mata*

**Перевод:**

**“They resided on these hills beside the bank of the Mandākinī and worshipped Nṛsiṁha. The scriptures state that this abode in Navadvīpa, known as Śrī Nṛsiṁha Kṣetra, is very purifying.**

### Text 36

*sūrya-ṭilā brahma-ṭilā nṛsiṁha pūrave chilā*  
*ebe sthāne haila viparyaya*  
*gaṇeśera ṭilā hera indra-ṭilā tāra para*  
*ei rūpa bahu ṭilāmaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Sūrya’s hill and Brahmā’s hill were to the east of Nṛsiṁha. Now this place has since changed. See Gaṇeśa’s hill and Indra’s hill behind. This place was filled with many hills like these.**

### Text 37

*viśvakarmā mahāśaya nirmilā prastaramaya*  
*kata śata devera vasati*  
*kāle saba lopa haila mandākinī śukāila*  
*ṭilā mātra āchaye samprati*

**Перевод:**

**“The renowned Viśvakarmā constructed hundreds of stone homes for the gods. In time, they decayed, and the Mandākinī dried up. Only the hills remain.**

### Text 38

*śilā-khaṇḍa agaṇana kara ebe daraśana*  
*sei saba mandirera śeṣa*  
*punaḥ kichu-dina pare eka bhakta nara-vare*  
*pābe nṛsiṁhera kṛpā-leśa*

**Перевод:**

**“See here the countless broken stones. These are the ruins of the homes of the gods. Again in the future, a devotee-king will receive a drop of mercy from Nṛsiṁha.**

### Text 39

*bṛhat mandira kari’ vasāibe narahari*  
*punaḥ sevā karibe prakāśa*  
*navadvīpa parikramā taṅra ei eka sīmā*  
*ṣola-krośa madhye ei vāsa*

**Перевод:**

**“He will build a grand Temple, establish Nṛsiṁha there, and resume His worship. This place is within the thirty-two miles of Navadvīpa and is one of the boundaries of the route to circumambulate Navadvīpa Dhāma.”**

### Text 40

*nitāi-jāhnavā-pada ye janāra sampada*  
*sei bhaktivinoda kāṅgāla*  
*navadvīpa sumahimā nāhi tāra kabhu sīmā*  
*tāhā gāya chāḍi’ māyā-jāla*

**Перевод:**

**The humble Bhaktivinoda, whose wealth is the feet of Nitāi and Jāhnavā, chants the unending glories of Navadvīpa and thus leaves behind the net of māyā.**

## Chapter Eight – Śrī Harihara Kṣetra, Śrī Vārāṇasī, and Śrī Godruma

### Text 1

*jaya jaya jaya śrī-śacī-sūta*  
*jaya jaya jaya śrī-avadhūta*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to the son of Śacī! All glory to the Avadhūta!**

### Text 2

*sītāpati jaya bhakata-rāja*  
*gadādhara jaya bhakta-samāja*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to Advaita Ācārya, the leader of the devotees! All glory to Gadādhara and all the devotees!**

### Text 3

*jaya navadvīpa sundara-dhāma*  
*jaya jaya jaya gaura ki nāma*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to beautiful Navadvīpa Dhāma! All glory to the Name of Gaura!**

### Text 4

*nitāi sahita bhakta-gaṇa*  
*“hari hari” bali’ cale takhana*

**Перевод:**

**Nitāi and the devotees then departed, chanting “Hari! Hari!”**

### Text 5

*bhāve ḍhala ḍhala nitāi cale*  
*preme ādha ādha vacana bale*

**Перевод:**

**Swaying to and fro in ecstasy, Nitāi walked and stammered with divine love.**

### Text 6

*jhara jhara jhare aṅkhira jala*  
*“gorā gorā” bali’ haya vikala*

**Перевод:**

**Tears streamed from His eyes. Chanting, “Gorā! Gorā!”, He became overwhelmed.**

### Text 7

*jhakmak kare bhūṣaṇa māla*  
*rūpe daśa-dika ha-ila āla*

**Перевод:**

**His ornaments and garlands sparkled, and His form illumined the ten directions.**

### Text 8

*śrīvāsa nāciche jīvera sane*  
*kabhu kaṅde kabhu nāce saghane*

**Перевод:**

**Śrīvāsa danced with Jīva. Sometimes they cried, and sometimes they danced continuously.**

### Text 9

*āra yata saba bhakta-gaṇa*  
*nācite nācite cale takhana*

**Перевод:**

**All the other devotees danced as they walked.**

### Text 10

*alakānandāra nikaṭe āsi’*  
*balena nitāi ānande bhāsi’*

**Перевод:**

**Coming to the Alakānandā River, Nitāi spoke, floating in joy.**

### Text 11

*“bilvapakṣa-grama paścime dhari’*  
*mandākinī āse nadīyā gheri*

**Перевод:**

**“Flowing west of Bilvapakṣa Grāma, the Gaṅgā comes and surrounds Nadīyā.**

### Text 12

*suvarṇa-bihāra dekhile yathā*  
*mandākinī chāḍe alakā tathā*

**Перевод:**

**“Then she separates from the Alakānandā where we saw Suvarṇa Bihāra.**

### Text 13

*alakānandāra pūrava pāre*  
*harihara-kṣetra gaṇḍaka dhāre*

**Перевод:**

**“On the eastern shore of the Alakānandā, beside the Gaṇḍaka River, is Harihara Kṣetra.**

### Text 14

*śrī-mūrti prakāśa ha-ibe kāle*  
*sundara kānana śobhibe bhāle*

**Перевод:**

**“In time, a Deity will manifest there, and a beautiful forest will shine gloriously.**

### Text 15

*alakā paścime dekhaha kāśī*  
*śaiva śākta seve mukati-dāsī*

**Перевод:**

**“See Kāśī to the west of the Alakānandā. There the followers of Śiva and Śakti worship the maidservant Liberation.**

### Text 16-17

*vārāṇasī hate e dhāma para*  
*hethāya dhūrjaṭī pināka-dhara*

*‘gaura gaura’ bali’ sadāi nāhe*  
*nija-jane gaura-bhakati yāce*

**Перевод:**

**“This place is superior to Vārāṇasī because here Lord Śiva constantly chants, “Gaura! Gaura!”, dances holding his trident, and offers his followers devotion to Gaura.**

### Text 18-19

*sahasra varaṣa kāśīte vasi’*  
*labhe ye mukati jñānete nyāsī*

*tāhā ta hethāya caraṇe ṭheli’*  
*nācena bhakata ‘gaurāṅga’ bali’*

**Перевод:**

**“Here, devotees chant Gaurāṅga’s Name, dance, and kick away the liberation that** ***sannyāsīs*** **attain by residing in Kāśī and practising** ***jñāna-yoga*** **for a thousand years.**

### Text 20

*niryāṇa samaye ekhāne jīva*  
*kāṇe ‘gaura’ bali’ tārena śiva*

**Перевод:**

**“Here, when souls pass away, Śiva chants the Name of Gaura into their ears and delivers them.**

### Text 21

*mahā-vārāṇasī e dhāma haya*  
*jīvera maraṇe nāhika bhaya”*

**Перевод:**

**“This place is thus Mahā-Vārāṇasī. Souls have no fear of death here.”**

### Text 22

*eta bali’ tathā nitāi nāce*  
*gaurahari-prema jīvere yāce*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this, Nitāi danced and offered Jīva divine love for Gaurahari.**

### Text 23

*alakṣye takhana kailāsa-pati*  
*nitāi caraṇe karila nati*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Śiva then invisibly bowed at the feet of Nitāi.**

### Text 24

*gaurī-saha śiva gaurāṅga-nāma*  
*gāiyā gāiyā pūraya kāma*

**Перевод:**

**Chanting the Name of Gaurāṅga, Śiva and Pārvatī fulfilled their desires.**

### Text 25

*svatantra īśvara nitāi tabe*  
*bhakata-saṅgete calila yabe*

**Перевод:**

**Then the independent Lord, Nitāi, left with the devotees.**

### Text 26

*gādigāchā-grāme pauṅchila āsi’*  
*tathāya āsiyā kahila hāsi’*

**Перевод:**

**Arriving in Gādigāchā Grāma, Nityānanda came over and spoke, smiling.**

### Text 27

*“godruma nāmete e dvīpa haya*  
*surabhi satata ekhāne raya*

**Перевод:**

**“This island is called Godruma. Surabhi resides here eternally.**

### Text 28-29

*kṛṣṇa-māyā-vaśe devendra yabe*  
*bhāsāya gokula nija gaurave*

*govardhana-giri dhariyā hari*  
*rakṣila gokula yatana kari’*

**Перевод:**

**“When Indra, bewildered by Kṛṣṇa’s illusion, flooded Gokula with his powerful rains, Kṛṣṇa lifted Govardhana Hill and carefully protected Gokula.**

### Text 30

*indra-darpa-cūrṇa ha-ile para*  
*śacī-pati cine śāraṅga-dhara*

**Перевод:**

**“After Indra’s pride was crushed, Indra recognised Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 31

*nija aparādha mārjana tare*  
*paḍila kṛṣṇera caraṇa dhare*

**Перевод:**

**“To clear his offence, he bowed and grasped the feet of Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 32

*dayāra samudra nanda-tanaya*  
*kṣamila indrere dila abhaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Kṛṣṇa, the ocean of mercy, forgave Indra and gave him the boon of fearlessness.**

### Text 33

*tathāpi indrera rahila bhaya*  
*surabhi nikaṭe takhana kaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Still, Indra remained fearful. He then spoke to Surabhi.**

### Text 34

*‘kṛṣṇa-līlā mui bujhite nāri*  
*aparādha mama ha-ila bhāri*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I could not understand the Pastimes of Kṛṣṇa, and I committed a very heavy offence.**

### Text 35

*śuneci kalite vrajendra-suta*  
*karibe nadīyā-līlā adbhūta*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I have heard that during Kali-yuga, Kṛṣṇa will perform wonderful Pastimes in Nadīyā.**

### Text 36

*pāche se samaya mohita haba*  
*aparādhī punaḥ haye rahiba*

**Перевод:**

**“‘At that time, I will become bewildered and be an offender again.**

### Text 37

*tumi ta surabhi sakala jāna*  
*karaha ekhana tāhāra vidhāna’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Surabhi, you know everything. Please advise me about this.’**

### Text 38

*surabhi balila, ‘calaha yāi*  
*navadvīpa-dhāme bhaji’ nimāi’*

**Перевод:**

**“Surabhi said, ‘Let us go and serve Nimāi in Navadvīpa Dhāma.’**

### Text 39

*devendra surabhi hethāya āsi’*  
*gaurāṅga bhajana karila vasi’*

**Перевод:**

**“Indra and Surabhi moved here and worshipped Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 40

*gaurāṅga bhajana sahaja ati*  
*sahaja tāhāra phala vitati*

**Перевод:**

**“Worshipping Gaurāṅga is very easy, and the results of doing so are easily distributed.**

### Text 41

*‘gaurāṅga’ baliyā krandana kare*  
*gaurāṅga darśana haya satvare*

**Перевод:**

**“They cried, chanting the Name of Gaurāṅga, and immediately saw Him.**

### Text 42-43

*kibā aparūpa rūpa-lāvaṇi*  
*dekhila gaurāṅga pratimā-khāni*

*ādha ādha hāsi’ varada rūpa*  
*preme gada-gada rasera kūpa*

**Перевод:**

**“How extraordinary is His beauty and form! They saw the figure of Gaurāṅga—the embodiment of benevolence, the well of** ***rasa*****—smiling and stammering, choked with divine love.**

### Text 44

*hāsiyā balena ṭhākura mora*  
*‘jāninu vāsanā āmi ta tora*

**Перевод:**

**“Smiling, My Lord said, ‘I know your desire.**

### Text 45

*alpa-dina āchhe prakaṭa-kāla*  
*nadīyā-nagare dekhibe bhāla*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I will appear in Nadīyā soon, and you will see Me fully then.**

### Text 46

*se līlā samaye sevibe more*  
*māyā-jāla āra nā dhare tore’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘During those Pastimes, you will serve Me, and the net of māyā will no longer bind you.’**

### Text 47

*eta bali’ prabhu adṛśya haya*  
*surabhi sundarī tathāya raya*

**Перевод:**

**“Saying this, the Lord disappeared. Beautiful Surabhi remained there.**

### Text 48

*aśvattha nikaṭe rahilā devī*  
*nirantara gaura-caraṇa sevi’*

**Перевод:**

**“She stayed near a banyan tree and constantly worshipped the feet of Gaura.**

### Text 49

*godrumadvīpa ta ha-ila nāma*  
*hethāya pūraya bhakata-kāma*

**Перевод:**

**“This place thus became known as Godrumadvīpa. The Lord fulfils the desires of the devotees here.**

### Text 50

*hethāya kuṭīra baṅdhiyā bhaje*  
*anāyāse gaura-caraṇe maje*

**Перевод:**

**“Anyone who builds a hut and serves here easily becomes immersed in the feet of Gaura.**

### Text 51

*ei dvīpe kabhu mṛkaṇḍa-suta*  
*pralaye āchila kathā adbhuta*

**Перевод:**

**“There is a wonderful story about Mārkaṇḍeya Muni, who was present on this island during the destruction of the universe.**

### Text 52

*sāta-kalpa āyu pāila muni*  
*pralaye baḍa-i vipad gaṇi’*

**Перевод:**

**“Mārkaṇḍeya Muni attained a lifetime of seven** ***kalpas*****, and during the destruction of the universe, he underwent great difficulty.**

### Text 53

*jalamaya haila samasta sthāna*  
*kothā vā rahibe kare sandhāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Everything became submerged in water, and he searched for a place to stay.**

### Text 54

*bhāsiyā bhāsiyā caliyā yāya*  
*‘kena hena vara la-inu hāya’*

**Перевод:**

**“Floating and floating about, he thought, ‘Alas! Why have I taken this boon?’**

### Text 55

*ṣola-krośa mātra nadīyā-dhāma*  
*jāgiyā bhakate deya viśrāma*

**Перевод:**

**“During the destruction, only the thirty-two miles of Navadvīpa Dhāma arise and give shelter to the devotees.**

### Text 56

*jalera taraṅge bhāsiyā muni*  
*ajñāna ha-iyā paḍe amani*

**Перевод:**

**“Floating in the waves of the water, the sage suddenly lost consciousness.**

### Text 57

*mahākṛpā kari’ surabhi tāya*  
*yatane munire hethā uṭhāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Being very merciful to him, Surabhi carefully rescued the sage.**

### Text 58

*samvit labhiyā mṛkaṇḍa-suta*  
*dekhila godrumadvīpa adbhuta*

**Перевод:**

**“Regaining consciousness, Mārkaṇḍeya Muni saw the wonders of Godrumadvīpa.**

### Text 59

*śata-koṭī-krośa vistāra sthāna*  
*nada-nadī śobhā prakāśamāna*

**Перевод:**

**“He saw an abode spanning two billion miles around, resplendent with rivers and streams.**

### Text 60

*taru-latā kata śobhaya tathā*  
*pakṣi-gaṇa gāya śrī-gaura-gāthā*

**Перевод:**

**“Numerous trees and vines were shining, and birds were chanting the glories of Śrī Gaura.**

### Text 61

*yojana-vistāra aśvattha hera*  
*surabhike tathā darśana kara*

**Перевод:**

**“He saw a banyan tree that was eight miles wide, and he saw Surabhi beneath it.**

### Text 62

*kṣudhāya ākula muni takhana*  
*surabhira prati bale vacana*

**Перевод:**

**“Stricken with hunger, the sage spoke to Surabhi.**

### Text 63

*‘tumi bhagavati rākhaha prāṇa*  
*dugdha diyā mora karaha trāṇa’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O goddess! Please save my life. Rescue me by giving me some milk.’**

### Text 64

*surabhi takhana sadaya haye*  
*piyāila dugdha munire laye*

**Перевод:**

**“Surabhi mercifully fed the sage with her milk.**

### Text 65

*sabala ha-iyā mṛkaṇḍa-sūnu*  
*surabhira prati kahaya punaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**“Revitalised, Mārkaṇḍeya Muni spoke to Surabhi again.**

### Text 66

*‘tumi bhagavati jananī mora*  
*tomāra māyāya jagat bhora*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O goddess, you are my mother, and the world is absorbed in your illusion.**

### Text 67

*nā bujhiyā āmi layechi vara*  
*sapta-kalpa jīva haye amara*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Not understanding the consequences, I accepted a boon of living for seven** ***kalpas*** **without dying.**

### Text 68

*pralaya samaye baḍa-i duḥkha*  
*nānā-vidha kleśa nāhika sukha*

**Перевод:**

**“‘During the destruction of the universe, there are numerous miseries and extreme distress. There is no happiness.**

### Text 69

*ki kari janani balago more*  
*kise vā yāiba e duḥkha tare’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Mother, tell me, what should I do? How can I overcome this distress?’**

### Text 70

*surabhi takhana balila vāṇī*  
*‘bhajaha śrī-gaura-pada dukhāni*

**Перевод:**

**“Surabhi said, ‘Serve the feet of Śrī Gaura.**

### Text 71

*ei navadvīpa prakṛti-pāra*  
*kabhu nāśa nāhi haya ihāra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘This abode of Navadvīpa is beyond material nature—it is never destroyed.**

### Text 72

*carma-cakṣe ihā ṣoḍaśa-krośa*  
*parama vaikuṇṭha sadā nirdoṣa*

**Перевод:**

**“‘To fleshy eyes, it is only thirty-two miles, but it is the supreme abode Vaikuṇṭha and eternally faultless.**

### Text 73

*aprākṛta deśa kāla ekhāne*  
*jaḍa māyā kebā keha nā jāne*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Here, time and space are supramundane, and no one knows of material illusion.**

### Text 74

*navadvīpe dekha apūrva ati*  
*cāri-dike beḍe virajā satī*

**Перевод:**

**“‘See the great wonder of Navadvīpa! The chaste Virajā flows all around it.**

### Text 75

*śata-koṭi-krośa pratyeka khaṇḍa*  
*madhye māyāpura nagara gaṇḍa*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Every part of Navadvīpa measures two billion miles, and at its centre is the grand city of Māyāpura.**

### Text 76

*aṣṭa-dala aṣṭa-dvīpera māna*  
*antardvīpa tāra keśara sthāna*

**Перевод:**

**“‘The eight islands of Navadvīpa are petals, and Antardvīpa is their whorl.**

### Text 77

*sarva-tīrtha sarva-devatā ṛṣi*  
*gaurāṅga bhajichhe hethāya vasi’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘All the holy places, gods, and sages reside here and worship Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 78

*tumi mārkaṇḍeya gaurāṅga-pada*  
*āśraya karaha jāni’ sampada*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Mārkaṇḍeya, understand your fortune and take shelter at the feet of Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 79

*akaitava dharma āśraya kara*  
*bhukti-mukti-vāñchā sudūre dhara*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Take shelter of the true** ***dharma*****, and keep desires for enjoyment and liberation far away.**

### Text 80

*gaurāṅga-bhajana-āśraya-bale*  
*madhura prema ta labhibe phale*

**Перевод:**

**“‘By taking shelter of Gaurāṅga and serving Him, you will attain divine love in** ***madhura-rasa*****.**

### Text 81

*sei prema yabe hṛdaye vase*  
*bhāsāya vilāsa-kalāra rase*

**Перевод:**

**“When that divine love appears in your heart, it will set you afloat in the rasa of the arts within the Pastimes of the Lord.**

### Text 82

*vraje rādhā-pade āśraya haya*  
*yugala-sevāya mānasa raya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘You will take shelter at the feet of Rādhā in Vraja, and your heart will be engaged in the service of the Divine Couple.**

### Text 83

*sevāra sukha ta atula jāna*  
*abheda nirvāṇe apārtha jñāna’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Know that the joy of service is unparalleled, and knowledge of static liberation is useless.’**

### Text 84

*surabhi-vacana śuniyā muni*  
*kara-yoḍa kari’ bale amani*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing the words of Surabhi, the sage immediately joined his palms and spoke.**

### Text 85

*‘śrī-gaura-caraṇa bhajiba yabe*  
*āmāra adṛṣṭa kothāya rabe’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘When I serve the feet of Śrī Gaura, what will become of my destiny?’**

### Text 86

*surabhi kahila siddhānta-sāra*  
*‘śrī-gaura-bhajane nāhi vicāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Surabhi then spoke the ultimate conclusion: ‘There is no judgement in the service of Śrī Gaura.**

### Text 87

*‘śrī-gaura’ baliyā ḍākibe yabe*  
*samasta karama vināśa habe*

**Перевод:**

**“‘When you call the Name of Śrī Gaura, all your** ***karma*** **will be destroyed.**

### Text 88

*kichu nāhi rabe vipāka āra*  
*ghucibe tomāra bhava-saṁsāra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘No reactions will remain any longer, and your attachment to material existence will disappear.**

### Text 89

*karma kene ekā jñānera phala*  
*ghucibe samūle haye vikala*

**Перевод:**

**“‘And not only your** ***karma*****, but the consequences of your pursuing liberation will also be eradicated.**

### Text 90

*tumi ta majibe gaurāṅga-rase*  
*bhajibe taṅhāre e dvīpe vase’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘You will become immersed in the ecstasy of Gaurāṅga, living on this island and serving Him.’**

### Text 91

*mārkaṇḍeya śuni’ ānande bhāse*  
*‘gaura’ bali’ kaṅde kakhana hāse*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing this, Mārkaṇḍeya floated in joy. He chanted the Name of Gaura, cried, and sometimes laughed.**

### Text 92

*ei dekha jīva apūrva sthāna*  
*mārkaṇḍeya yathā pāila prāṇa”*

**Перевод:**

**“Jīva, see here the wonderful place where Mārkaṇḍeya received life.”**

### Text 93

*gaurāṅga-mahimā nitāi-mukhe*  
*śuni’ jīva bhāse parama sukhe*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the glories of Gaurāṅga from the mouth of Nitāi, Jīva floated in the greatest joy.**

### Text 94

*se sthāne se dina yāpana kari’*  
*madhyadvīpe cale baliyā “hari”*

**Перевод:**

**They spent the day in that place and then went to Madhyadvīpa, chanting “Hari!”**

### Text 95-96

*nitāi-jāhnavā-caraṇa sāra*  
*jāniyā bhaktivinoda cāra*
