*nitāi-ādeśa mastake dhare*  
*nadīyā-mahimā varṇana kare*

**Перевод:**

**Knowing Nitāi and Jāhnavā’s feet to be all-in-all, the fallen Bhaktivinoda holds the order of Nitāi on his head and describes the glories of Nadīyā.**

## Chapter Nine – Śrī Madhyadvīpa and Śrī Naimiṣa

### Text 1

*jaya gaura-candra jaya nityānanda*  
*jaya jaya gadādhara*  
*śrīvāsādi jaya jaya bhaktālaya*  
*navadvīpa-dhāma-vara*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to Gaura-candra, Nityānanda, Gadādhara, Śrīvāsa, all the devotees of the Lord! All glory to their home, the best of abodes, Śrī Navadvīpa!**

### Text 2

*niśi avasāne matta gaura-gāne*  
*calilena nityānanda*  
*saṅge bhakta-gaṇa premete magana*  
*vistāriyā parānanda*

**Перевод:**

**Immersed in divine love and madly chanting about Gaurāṅga, Nityānanda departed with the devotees at the end of the night, distributing divine joy.**

### Text 3

*madhyadvīpe āsi’ bale hāsi’ hāsi’*  
*“ei ta mājidā grāma*  
*hethā sapta-ṛṣi bhaji’ gaura-śaśī*  
*karilena suviśrāma*

**Перевод:**

**Coming into Madhyadvīpa and laughing, Nityānanda Prabhu said, “This is Mājidā Grāma. The seven sages resided here and worshipped the moon-like Śrī Gaura.**

### Text 4

*pitṛ-sannidhāne gaura-guṇa-gāne*  
*satya-yuge ṛṣi-gaṇa*  
*ha-iyā magana yācila takhana*  
*gaura-prema nitya-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**“During Satya-yuga, the sages became immersed in chanting Gaura’s glories and prayed to their father, Brahmā, for the eternal wealth of divine love for Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 5

*brahmā catur-mukha peye baḍa sukha*  
*sapta-putre bale tabe*  
*‘navadvīpe yāo gaura-guṇa gāo*  
*anāyāse prema habe*

**Перевод:**

**“Four-headed Brahmā felt great joy and said to his seven sons, ‘Go to Navadvīpa and chant the glories of Gaura. You will easily attain divine love.**

### Text 6

*dhāma-kṛpā sāra lābha haya yāra*  
*tāra haya sādhu-saṅga*  
*sādhu-saṅge bhaje kṛṣṇa-preme maje*  
*ei ta parama raṅga*

**Перевод:**

**“‘The mercy of the Dhāma is supreme. Those who attain it, attain the association of the** ***sādhus*****. Serving in the association of the** ***sādhus*** **and becoming immersed in divine love for Kṛṣṇa is the ultimate joy.**

### Text 7

*navadvīpe rati labhe yāra mati*  
*sei pāya vraja-vāsa*  
*aprākṛta dhāma gaurahari nāma*  
*kevala sādhura āśa’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Those whose hearts develop attachment to Navadvīpa attain residency in Vraja. The supramundane Dhāma and the Name of Gaurahari are the only desires of the** ***sādhus*****.’**

### Text 8

*pitṛ-upadeśa bujhiyā viśeṣa*  
*sapta-ṛṣi āsi’ tabe*  
*‘hari’ bali’ nāce gaura-prema yāce*  
*gāya guṇa uccha-rave*

**Перевод:**

**“Properly understanding the instructions of their father, the seven sages came here, chanted the Name of the Lord, and danced. They prayed for divine love for Gaurāṅga and loudly chanted His glories.**

### Text 9

*bale, ‘gaurahari anugraha kari’*  
*dekhā dāo eka-bāra*  
*nānā dharma sādhi‘ hainu aparādhī*  
*bhakti ebe kainu sāra’*

**Перевод:**

**“They prayed, ‘O Gaurahari! Just once mercifully reveal Yourself to us. Practising various** ***dharmas*****, we became offenders, but we have now accepted devotion to be all-in-all.’**

### Text 10

*bhakti-niṣṭhā kari’ bhaji’ gaurahari*  
*ṛṣi-gaṇa kare tapa*  
*kichu nāhi khāya nidrā nāhi yāya*  
*gaura-nāma kare japa*

**Перевод:**

**“The sages performed austerities and served Gaurahari with firm devotion. They ate nothing, never went to sleep, and constantly chanted the Name of Gaura.**

### Text 11

*madhyāhna samaya gaura dayāmaya*  
*dekhā dila ṛṣi-gaṇe*  
*śata-sūrya-prabhā yogi-mano-lobhā*  
*śuddha pañca-tattva sane*

**Перевод:**

**“Once, at midday, merciful Gaurāṅga, in the company of the Pañca Tattva, revealed Himself to the sages. As bright as a hundred suns, His effulgence was alluring to the minds of** ***yogīs*****.**

### Text 12

*kibā sei rūpa ati aparūpa*  
*suvarṇa sundara mūrti*  
*gale vana-mālā dik kare ālā*  
*tāhe ābharaṇa sphūrti*

**Перевод:**

**“How extraordinary was His form! How beautiful and golden was His figure! He had a forest garland around His neck, and the glittering of His ornaments illumined the directions.**

### Text 13

*cāhani sundara cikura caṅcara*  
*candanera bindu bhāle*  
*trikaccha vasana sūtra suśobhana*  
*śobhita mallikā-māle*

**Перевод:**

**“His charming glance, His wavy hair, the spot of sandalwood paste on His forehead, His** ***dhotī*** **tucked in three places, His sacred thread—all were extremely beautiful and further beautified by His garland of jasmine flowers.**

### Text 14

*se rūpa dekhiyā mohita ha-iyā*  
*sabe kare nivedana*  
*‘tomāra caraṇa la-inu śaraṇa*  
*deha pade bhakti-dhana’*

**Перевод:**

**“Seeing His form and becoming enchanted, the sages prayed, ‘May we take shelter at Your feet. Please give us the wealth of devotion to Your feet.’**

### Text 15

*śuni’ gaurahari bale dayā kari’*  
*‘śuna ohe ṛṣi-gaṇa*  
*chāḍi’ abhilāṣa jñāna-karma-pāśa*  
*kara kṛṣṇa ālocana*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing their prayer, Gaurahari mercifully said, ‘O sages, listen! Leave behind all other desires and the chains of exploitation and renunciation, and discuss only Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 16

*svalpa dināntare nadīyā nagare*  
*ha-ibe prakaṭa līlā*  
*tumi sabe tabe darśana karibe*  
*nāma-saṅkīrtana-khelā*

**Перевод:**

**“‘My Pastimes will manifest in Nadia soon, and you all will then see My Pastime of chanting My Name.**

### Text 17

*e kathā ekhana rākhaha gopana*  
*āmāra vacana dhara*  
*śrī-kumāra-haṭṭe nija-kṛta ghaṭṭe*  
*kṛṣṇera bhajana kara’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘For now, keep this secret. Remember My words and worship Kṛṣṇa at the** ***ghāṭa*** **you made in Śrī Kumāra Haṭṭa.’**

### Text 18

*gaura-adarśane saptarṣi takhane*  
*kumāra-haṭṭete yāya*  
*e-sthāne ekhana kara daraśana*  
*sapta-ṭīlā śobhā pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“When Gaura disappeared, the seven sages went to Kumāra Haṭṭa. See now the seven hills shining here.**

### Text 19

*saptarṣi ākāśe yemate prakāśe*  
*sapta-ṭīlā taṅra sama*  
*hethā vāsa kari’ pāya gaurahari*  
*nā sādhi’ niyama yama*

**Перевод:**

**“They are arranged just as the seven sages are in the sky. Residing here, the sages attained Gaurahari without following the rules and regulations of yoga practice.**

### Text 20

*ihāra dakṣiṇe dekhaha nayane*  
*āchhe eka jaladhāra*  
*ei ta gomatī supavitra ati*  
*naimiṣa-kānana āra*

**Перевод:**

**“See the river to the south of here; this is the Gomatī. She is extremely pure. See also the Naimiṣa forest.**

### Text 21

*purā kalpe kali haile mahābalī*  
*śaunakādi ṛṣi-gaṇa*  
*sūtera śrī-mukhe śune sabe sukhe*  
*gaura-bhāgavata-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**“In the previous manifestation of the universe, as Kali became very strong, Śaunaka and other sages joyfully heard about Lord Gaura from the holy mouth of Sūta Goswāmī.**

### Text 22

*hethā yei jana purāṇa paṭhana*  
*karaya kārtika māse*  
*sarva-kleśa tyaje gaura-raṅge maje*  
*vraja labhe anāyāse*

**Перевод:**

**“Anyone who recites the Purāṇas here during the month of Kārttika leaves behind all miseries, becomes immersed in the Pastimes of Gaura, and easily attains Vraja.**

### Text 23

*kabhu pañcānana chāḍi’ vṛṣāsana*  
*śrī-haṁsa-vāhana haye*  
*śunila purāṇa gaura-guṇa-gāna*  
*āpana bhakata laye*

**Перевод:**

**“Once, Śiva left Nandī, rode Brahmā’s swan here with his devotees, and heard the Purāṇas and songs about the glories of Gaura.**

### Text 24

*gāiyā gāiyā nāciyā nāciyā*  
*śaiva yata kāśī-vāsī*  
*pañcānane gheri’ bali’ ‘gaurahari’*  
*puṣpa phele rāśi rāśi”*

**Перевод:**

**“Chanting and dancing, Śiva’s followers from Kāśī surrounded him, chanted the Name of Gaurahari, and showered flowers.”**

### Text 25

*nitāi-vacana śuniyā takhana*  
*jīvera uthale bhāva*  
*gaḍāgaḍi yāya dhairaya nā pāya*  
*āsvāde dhāma-prabhāva*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the words of Nitāi, Jīva became ecstatic. He lost his composure and rolled on the ground, relishing the glory of the Dhāma.**

### Text 26

*se dina yāpana kare bhakta-gaṇa*  
*nitāi-caṅdera sane*  
*para-dina sabe calilena tabe*  
*śrī-puṣkara daraśane*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees spent the day there with Nitāi-caṅda. The next day, they went to see Śrī Puṣkara.**

### Text 27

*jāhnavā-nitāi bhajana sadāi*  
*yāhāra antare jāge*  
*nadīyā-mahimā bhakta-madhurimā*  
*gāichhe se jana rāge*

**Перевод:**

**As constant service to Jāhnavā and Nitāi arises in his heart, this soul lovingly sings of the sweetness of the devotees and the glories of Nadīyā.**

## Chapter Ten – Visiting Śrī Brāhmaṇa Puṣkara, Śrī Uccha Haṭṭa, and Other Places

### Text 1

*jaya gaura nityānanda advaita sahita*  
*jaya gadādhara jaya śrīvāsa paṇḍita*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to Gaurāṅga, Nityānanda, and Advaita! All glory to Gadādhara and Śrīvās Paṇḍita!**

### Text 2

*jaya navadvīpa śuddha prema-bhakti-dhāma*  
*jaya jaya jaya gaura-nityānanda-nāma*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to Navadvīpa, the abode of pure love and devotion! All glory to the Names of Gaura and Nityānanda!**

### Text 3

*śunahe kalira jīva chāḍi’ jñāna karma*  
*nitāi caitanya bhaja tyaji’ dharmādharma*

**Перевод:**

**O souls of Kali-yuga, listen! Abandon renunciation and exploitation, reject religion and irreligion, and serve Nitāi and Caitanya!**

### Text 4

*dayāra samudra sei gaura-nityānanda*  
*akātare dibe bhāi sāra vrajānanda*

**Перевод:**

**They are oceans of mercy. Brother! They will freely give you the essence of Vraja’s joy.**

### Text 5

*yāminī prabhāta haile nityānanda-rāya*  
*jīvere la-iyā dhāma-bhramaṇete yāya*

**Перевод:**

**At dawn, Nityānanda Rāya took Jīva and went out to tour the Dhāma.**

### Text 6

*bale, “dekha jīva ei grāma manohara*  
*ekhana brāhmaṇa-purā ḍāke sarva-nara*

**Перевод:**

**He said, “Jīva, look at this beautiful village. Everyone now calls it Brāhmaṇapura.**

### Text 7

*brāhmaṇa-puṣkara nāma sarva-śāstre kaya*  
*hethā ye rahasya tāhā ati guhya haya*

**Перевод:**

**“All the scriptures call it Brāhmaṇa Puṣkara. A very confidential Pastime took place here.**

### Text 8

*satya-yuge diva-dāsa nāmete brāhmaṇa*  
*gṛha tyaji’ kare sarva-tīrtha daraśana*

**Перевод:**

**“During Satya-yuga, a** ***brāhmaṇa*** **named Diva Dāsa left home and visited all the holy places.**

### Text 9

*puṣkara-tīrthete tāra haila baḍa prīta*  
*tathāpi bhramite navadvīpe upasthita*

**Перевод:**

**“He felt the greatest joy at Puṣkara Tīrtha. Still, he travelled, and eventually he came to Navadvīpa.**

### Text 10

*ei sthāne rātri-yoge dekhila svapana*  
*‘hethā vāsa kara vipra pābe nitya-dhana’*

**Перевод:**

**“Here, during the night, he had a dream in which he was told, ‘O** ***brāhmaṇa*****, reside here and you will attain eternal wealth.’**

### Text 11

*ei sthāne kuṭīra bāṅdhiyā diva-dāsa*  
*vṛddha-kālāvadhi te̐ha karilena vāsa*

**Перевод:**

**“Diva Dāsa built a cottage and resided here into his old age.**

### Text 12

*vṛddha-kāle calite aśakta dvija-vara*  
*icchā haila ‘ebe āmi dekhiba puṣkara’*

**Перевод:**

**“In his old age, the great** ***brāhmaṇa*** **was unable to travel, but he desired to see Puṣkara.**

### Text 13

*calite nā pāre dvija karaya krandana*  
*‘āra nā pāiba āmi puṣkara darśana’*

**Перевод:**

**“Unable to travel, the** ***brāhmaṇa*** **cried, ‘I will never see Puṣkara again.’**

### Text 14

*takhana puṣkara-rāja sadaya ha-ila*  
*dvija-rūpe diva-dāse daraśana dila*

**Перевод:**

**“The Lord of Puṣkara felt compassion and revealed himself to Diva Dāsa in the form of a** ***brāhmaṇa*****.**

### Text 15

*diva-dāse bale, ‘vipra nā kara krandana*  
*tomāra sammukhe ei kuṇḍa suśobhana*

**Перевод:**

**“He said to Diva Dāsa, ‘O** ***brāhmaṇa*****, do not cry. There is a beautiful pond in front of you.**

### Text 16

*ei kuṇḍe snāna tumi kara eka-bāra*  
*pratyakṣa ha-ibe tīrtha puṣkara tomāra’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Bathe in this pond once, and you will see Puṣkara Tīrtha.’**

### Text 17

*tāhā śuni’ kuṇḍe snāna kare dvija-vara*  
*divya-cakṣu labhi’ dekhe sammukhe puṣkara*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing this, the great** ***brāhmaṇa*** **bathed in the pond. Attaining divine eyes, he saw Puṣkara before him.**

### Text 18

*krandana kariyā dvija puṣkare balila*  
*‘āmā lāgi’ baḍa kleśa tomāra ha-ila’*

**Перевод:**

**“Crying, the** ***brāhmaṇa*** **said to Puṣkara, ‘You have taken great trouble for me.’**

### Text 19

*puṣkara balena, ‘śuna dvija bhāgyavān*  
*dūra haite nā āsinu hethā vidyamāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Puṣkara said, ‘Listen, O fortunate** ***brāhmaṇa*****! I have not come from far away. I live here.**

### Text 20

*ei navadvīpa-dhāma sarva-tīrthamaya*  
*navadvīpe sevi’ hethā thāke tīrtha-caya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Navadvīpa Dhāma contains all holy places; all holy places reside here and serve Navadvīpa.**

### Text 21

*āmāra svarūpa eka pāścātye prakāśa*  
*nije āmi ei sthāne nitya kari vāsa*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Although a form of mine exists in the west, I myself live here eternally.**

### Text 22

*śata-bāra keha sei tīrthe kari’ snāna*  
*yei phala pāya hethā se phala vidhāna*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Souls attain here the result attained by bathing a hundred times there.**

### Text 23

*ataeva navadvīpa chāḍi’ yei jana*  
*anya tīrtha āśā kare se mūḍha durjana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Thus, anyone who leaves Navadvīpa desiring to visit other holy places is a foolish, unfortunate soul.**

### Text 24

*sarva-tīrtha bhrami’ yadi haya phalodaya*  
*navadvīpa tabe tāra vāsa-sthāna haya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘When souls attain the result of visiting all other holy places, then they can reside in Navadvīpa.**

### Text 25

*ai dekha uccha-sthāna haṭṭera samāna*  
*kurukṣetra brahmāvarta tathā vidyamāna*

**Перевод:**

**“‘See there the raised area that resembles a market. Kurukṣetra and Brahmāvarta exist there.**

### Text 26

*sarasvatī dṛṣadvatī dui pārśe tāra*  
*ati śobhā pāya puṇya karaye vistāra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘With the Sarasvatī and Dṛṣadvatī Rivers on its two sides, it shines brightly and bestows good fortune.**

### Text 27

*ohe vipra gūḍha kathā baliba tomāya*  
*ati alpa kāle habe ānanda hethāya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O** ***brāhmaṇa*****, I will tell you a secret. Very soon there will be great joy here.**

### Text 28

*māyāpure śacī-gṛhe gaurāṅga-sundara*  
*prakaṭa ha-iyā prema vilābe vistara*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Gaurāṅga-sundara will appear in the home of Śacī in Māyāpura and broadly distribute divine love.**

### Text 29

*ei saba sthāne prabhu bhakta-vṛnda laye*  
*saṅkīrtana-rase nācibena matta haye*

**Перевод:**

**“‘In all these places, the Lord will dance in the ecstasy of** ***saṅkīrtana*** **with the devotees and become mad.**

### Text 30

*sarva-avatāre chilā ye ye bhakta-gaṇa*  
*sakale la-iyā prabhu karibe kīrtana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘The Lord will perform** ***kīrtana*** **with all the devotees of all His previous** ***avatāras*****.**

### Text 31

*prema-vanyā jale sarva-jagat bhāsābe*  
*kutārkika vinā sabe mahāprema pābe*

**Перевод:**

**“‘He will inundate the entire world with a flood of divine love. Everyone except the speculators will attain intense divine love.**

### Text 32

*ei dhāma-niṣṭhā kari’ yebā kare vāsa*  
*tāre mile gaura-pada ohe diva-dāsa*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Diva Dāsa, souls who are faithful to the Dhāma and reside here will reach the feet of Gaura.**

### Text 33

*koṭī koṭī varṣa kari’ śrī-kṛṣṇa-bhajana*  
*tathāpi nāmete rati nā pāya durjana*

**Перевод:**

**“Unfortunate souls worship Kṛṣṇa for billions of years but do not attain attraction to His Name.**

### Text 34

*gaurāṅga bhajile duṣṭa-bhāva dūre yāya*  
*alpa dine vraja-dhāme rādhā-kṛṣṇa pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘If souls worship Gaurāṅga, their wickedness goes away, and they quickly attain Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa in Vraja Dhāma.**

### Text 35

*nija siddha-deha pāya sakhīra āśraya*  
*nija kuñja śrī-yugala-sevā tāra haya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘They attain their spiritual body and the shelter of the** ***sakhīs*****, and serve the Divine Couple in their own grove.**

### Text 36

*ohe vipra hethā thāki’ karaha bhajana*  
*sapārṣade śrī-gaurāṅga pābe daraśana’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O** ***brāhmaṇa*****, stay and serve here. You will see Śrī Gaurāṅga with His associates.’**

### Text 37

*ei kathā bali’ tīrtha-rāja gela cali’*  
*śunila ākāśa-vāṇī āise, ‘dhanya kali*

**Перевод:**

**“Saying this, the Lord of Puṣkara left. The** ***brāhmaṇa*** **then heard a voice come from the sky, ‘Glory to the Age of Kali!**

### Text 38

*tumi vipra sei kāle janmibe ābāra*  
*śrī-gaura-kīrtana preme dibe ta sāṅtāra’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O** ***brāhmaṇa*****, you will be born again at that time and swim in the divine love of Śrī Gaura’s** ***kīrtana*****.’**

### Text 39

*eta śuni diva-dāsa niścinta ha-ila*  
*ei kuṇḍa-tīre vasi’ bhajana karila”*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing this, Diva Dāsa became fearless. He resided on the bank of the pond and performed worship.”**

### Text 40

*e saba purāṇa kathā śrī-jīve kahiyā*  
*uccha-haṭṭa kurukṣetre praveśila giyā*

**Перевод:**

**After telling Śrī Jīva these ancient stories, Nityānanda entered Uccha Haṭṭa, Kurukṣetra.**

### Text 41

*nityānanda bale, “hethā sarva-deva-gaṇa*  
*kurukṣetra tīrtha saha kaila āgamana*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda said, “All the gods came here with Kurukṣetra Tīrtha.**

### Text 42

*brahmāvarte kuru-kṣetre yata tīrtha chila*  
*sarva-tīrtha āsi’ hethā virāja karila*

**Перевод:**

**“All the holy places in Brahmāvarta and Kuru-kṣetra came and resided here.**

### Text 43

*pṛthūdaka ādi kari’ saba hethā vaise*  
*sabe navadvīpa sevā kare anāyāse”*

**Перевод:**

**“Pṛthūdaka and the other holy places resided here and served Navadvīpa without difficulty.”**

### Text 44

*śata-varṣa kuru-kṣetre vāse yei phala*  
*hethā eka-rātra vāse labhe se sakala*

**Перевод:**

**By staying here for one night, souls attain the full result of residing in Kuru-kṣetra for a hundred years.**

### Text 45

*prabhu bale, “hethā vāsa kari’ deva-gaṇa*  
*haṭṭa kari’ gaura-kathā kare ālocana*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu continued, “The gods resided here, established a market, and discussed Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 46

*haṭṭa-ḍāṅgā bali’ nāma ha-ila ihāra*  
*ihāra darśane pāya prema-pārābāra*

**Перевод:**

**“The name of this place thus became Haṭṭaḍāṅgā. By seeing this place, souls enter an ocean of divine love.**

### Text 47

*ei eka sīmā jīva dekha nadīyāra*  
*ebe cala yāi morā bhāgīrathī pāra”*

**Перевод:**

**“O Jīva, see here one of the boundaries of Nadia. Let us now cross the Gaṅgā.”**

### Text 48

*bhāgīrathī pāra haye madhyāhna samaya*  
*koladvīpe nityānanda ha-ila udaya*

**Перевод:**

**Crossing the Gaṅgā, Nityānanda Prabhu arrived in Koladvīpa at midday.**

### Text 49

*kuliyāpāhāḍapure yāite yāite*  
*śrī-jīve nitāi-cāṅda lāgila kahite*

**Перевод:**

**On the way to Kuliyāpāhāḍapura, Nitāi-cāṅda spoke to Śrī Jīva.**

### Text 50

*“ye krame āinu morā haye gaṅgā pāra*  
*sei krama siddha-krama parikramā-sāra*

**Перевод:**

**“The way we have come, crossing the Gaṅgā, is the proper way to circumambulate the Dhāma.**

### Text 51-52

*yabe prabhu śrī-caitanya laye nija-gaṇa*  
*karilena śrī-caudda-mādala saṅkīrtana*

*kājire śodhite prabhu sandhyā āgamane*  
*māyāpura chāḍi’ cale laye bhakta-jane*

**Перевод:**

**“One evening, while performing** ***saṅkīrtana*** **with fourteen** ***mṛdaṅgas*****, Śrī Caitanya left Māyāpura with His associates and came to purify the Kāzī.**

### Text 53

*sei rātra brahma-rātra śīghra nahe śeṣa*  
*ei krame mahāprabhu bhrame nija-deśa*

**Перевод:**

**“That night, like a night of Brahmā, did not end quickly. Mahāprabhu toured His abode in this way.**

### Text 54

*tārapara prati ekādaśī-tithi dhari’*  
*bhramilā āmāra prabhu saṅkīrtana kari’*

**Перевод:**

**“Thereafter, My Lord toured performing** ***saṅkīrtana*** **on every Ekādaśī.**

### Text 55

*kabhu pañca-krośa bhrame antardvīpamaya*  
*kabhu aṣṭa-krośa bhrame yena mane laya*

**Перевод:**

**“Sometimes He toured the ten miles of Antardvīpa, and sometimes, as He desired, He toured for sixteen miles.**

### Text 56

*nija gṛha haite bārakoṇā ghāṭa chāḍi’*  
*dīrghikā veṣṭane yāya śrīdharera bāḍī*

**Перевод:**

**“From His home, He would leave Bārakoṇā Ghāta and go around Ballāl Dīrghikā to Śrīdhara’s house.**

### Text 57

*tathā haite antardvīpa sīmā bhrami’ āse*  
*pañca-krośa parikramā haya anāyāse*

**Перевод:**

**“From there, He would tour around the boundary of Antardvīpa and then return. In this way, He would easily circumambulate for ten miles.**

### Text 58

*simuliyā haye kāji-gṛhe beḍi’ cale*  
*śrīdhare sambhāṣi’ āise gādigāchā sthale*

**Перевод:**

**“Other times, passing Simuliyā, He would go to the house of the Kāzī, speak with Śrīdhara, and then come to Gādigāchā.**

### Text 59

*mājidā haite haya bhāgīrathī pāra*  
*pāraḍāṅgā cināḍāṅgā pulina vistara*

**Перевод:**

**“From Mājidā, He would cross the Gaṅgā and tour Pāraḍāṅgā and Cināḍāṅgā along the broad riverbank.**

### Text 60

*chāḍiyā jāhnavī pāra ha-iyā takhana*  
*aṣṭa-krośa bhrami’ cale āpana bhavana*

**Перевод:**

**“Then He would cross the Gaṅgā again and return to His home, thus touring for sixteen miles.**

### Text 61

*siddha parikramā haya pūrṇa ṣola-krośa*  
*sei parikramā kaile prabhura santoṣa*

**Перевод:**

**“A complete circumambulation is the full thirty-two miles. The Lord is pleased if someone completes this.**

### Text 62

*sei parikramā āmi tomāre karāi*  
*ihāra samāna parikramā āra nāi*

**Перевод:**

**“I am taking you on this complete circumambulation. There is no circumambulation equal to it.**

### Text 63

*vṛndāvana ṣola-krośa dvādaśa kānana*  
*ei parikramā madhye pābe daraśana*

**Перевод:**

**“During this circumambulation, you will see all thirty-two miles of the Dhāma and the twelve forests of Vṛndāvana.**

### Text 64

*nava-rātre ei parikramā śeṣa haya*  
*nava-rātra bali’ nāma śāstre kaya*

**Перевод:**

**“This circumambulation is completed over the course of nine nights; the scriptures call it the ‘*****nava-rātra parikramā’*****.**

### Text 65

*pañca-krośa parikramā eka-dine kare*  
*rātra-traya aṣṭa-krośa parikramā dhare*

**Перевод:**

**“Devotees perform the ten-mile circumambulation in one day and complete the sixteen-mile circumambulation over the course of three nights.**

### Text 66

*eka-rātra māyāpure dvitīya godrume*  
*puline tṛtīya rātra ei krame bhrame”*

**Перевод:**

**“One night is in Māyāpura, the second is in Godrumadvīpa, and the third night is in Śrī Pulina. Devotees tour in this way.”**

### Text 67

*śuni’ parikramā-tattva jīva-mahāśaya*  
*premete adhairya haye kata-kṣaṇa raya*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing about the process of circumambulation, Śrī Jīva Gosvāmī became overwhelmed with divine love for some time.**

### Text 68

*nitāi-jāhnavā-pada-chāyā āśa yāra*  
*nadīyā-mahimā varṇe akiñcana cāra*

**Перевод:**

**This poor, fallen soul, whose aspiration is the shade of Nitāi and Jāhnavā’s feet, describes the glories of Nadīyā.**

## Chapter Eleven – Śrī Śrī Koladvīpa, Śrī Samudragaḍ, Śrī Campāhaṭṭa, and Śrī Jayadeva

### Text 1

*jaya jaya śrī-caitanya jaya nityānanda*  
*jayādvaita-śrīvāsādi gaura-bhakta-vṛnda*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to Śrī Caitanya, Nityānanda, Advaita, Śrīvāsa, and all the devotees of the Lord!**

### Text 2

*jaya jaya gauḍa-bhūmi sarva-bhūmi-sāra*  
*yathā nāma-saha śrī-caitanya avatāra*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to the land of Gauḍa, the best of all lands, where Śrī Caitanya descended with His Name!**

### Text 3

*nityānanda prabhu bale, “śuna sarva-jana*  
*pañcaveṇī-rūpe gaṅgā hethāya milana*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu said, “Everyone, listen! Here, the Gaṅgā convenes with five other rivers.**

### Text 4

*mandākinī alakā sahita bhāgīrathī*  
*gupta-bhāve hethāya āchhena sarasvatī*

**Перевод:**

**“The Gaṅgā flows here with the Mandākinī and the Alakānandā. The Sarasvatī is also hidden here.**

### Text 5

*paścime yamunā saha āise bhogavatī*  
*tāhāte mānasa-gaṅgā mahāvegavatī*

**Перевод:**

**“The Yamunā comes from the west with the Bhogavatī, and the swift Mānasa Gaṅgā also flows within them.**

### Text 6

*mahā mahā-prayāga baliyā ṛṣi-gaṇe*  
*koṭi koṭi yajña hethā kaila brahmā sane*

**Перевод:**

**“Considering this place Mahā-Prayāga, sages have performed billions of sacrifices here with Lord Brahmā.**

### Text 7

*brahma-satra sthāna ei mahimā apāra*  
*hethā snāna karile janama nahe āra*

**Перевод:**

**“The glories of this place of Brahmā’s sacrifices are unlimited. If a soul bathes here, they do not take birth again.**

### Text 8

*ihāra mahimā kebā varṇibāre pāre*  
*śuṣka dhārā-sama kona tīrtha ha-ite nāre*

**Перевод:**

**“Who can describe the glories of this place? It is not a holy place that is now simply a dry river.**

### Text 9

*jale-sthale-antarīkṣe tyajiyā jīvana*  
*sarva-jīva pāya śrī-goloka-vṛndāvana*

**Перевод:**

**“All souls who give up their life in the water, on the earth, or in the air here attain Śrī Goloka Vṛndāvana.**

### Text 10

*kuliyāpāhāḍa bali’ khyāta ei sthāna*  
*gaṅgā-tīre uccha-bhūmi parvata-samāna*

**Перевод:**

**“This place is known as Kuliyāpāhāḍa. It is a raised land on the bank of the Gaṅgā, resembling a mountain.**

### Text 11

*koladvīpa nāma śāstre āchaya varṇana*  
*satya-yuga-kathā eka śuna sarva-jana*

**Перевод:**

**“It is called Koladvīpa in the scriptures. Everyone, listen to a story from Satya-yuga.**

### Text 12

*vāsudeva nāme eka brāhmaṇa-kumāra*  
*varāhadevera sevā kare bāra-bāra*

**Перевод:**

**“There was a** ***brāhmaṇa*** **boy named Vāsudeva. He regularly worshipped Varāhadeva.**

### Text 13

*śrī-varāha-mūrti pūji’ kare upāsanā*  
*sarvadā varāhadevera karaya prārthanā*

**Перевод:**

**“He worshipped a Deity of Śrī Varāha and constantly prayed to Him.**

### Text 14

*‘prabhu more kṛpā kari’ deha daraśana*  
*saphala ha-uka mora nayana jīvana’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Lord! Mercifully reveal Yourself to me so that my eyes and life may be fulfilled.’**

### Text 15

*ei bali’ kaṅde vipra gaḍāgaḍi yāya*  
*‘prabhu nāhi dekhā dile jīvana vṛthāya’*

**Перевод:**

**“Praying in this way, the** ***brāhmaṇa*** **would roll on the ground and cry, ‘O Lord! If You do not reveal Yourself, my life is worthless.’**

### Text 16

*kata-dine śrī-varāha anukampā kari’*  
*dekhā dilā vāsudeve kola-rūpa dhari’*

**Перевод:**

**“Eventually, Śrī Varāha mercifully revealed Himself to Vāsudeva in the form of a boar.**

### Text 17

*nānā-ratna bhūṣaṇe bhūṣita kalevara*  
*pada grīvā nāsā mukha cakṣu manohara*

**Перевод:**

**“His body was ornamented with various jewels and ornaments, and His feet, neck, nose, mouth, and eyes were enchanting.**

### Text 18

*parvata-samāna uccha śarīra taṅhāra*  
*dekhi’ vipra nije dhanya māne bāra bāra*

**Перевод:**

**His body was as tall as a mountain. Seeing Him, the** ***brāhmaṇa*** **again and again felt himself to be fortunate.**

### Text 19

*bhūme paḍi’ vipra praṇamiyā prabhu-pāya*  
*kaṅdiyā ākula haila gaḍāgaḍi yāya*

**Перевод:**

**He fell to the ground, bowed at the feet of the Lord, cried fervently, and rolled on the ground.**

### Text 20

*viprera bhakati dekhi’ varāha takhana*  
*kahilena vāsudeve madhura vacana*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the devotion of the** ***brāhmaṇa*****, Varāha spoke sweet words to Vāsudeva.**

### Text 21

*‘ohe vāsudeva tumi bhakata āmāra*  
*baḍa tuṣṭa hainu pūjā pāiyā tomāra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Vāsudeva! You are My devotee, and I am very pleased with your worship.**

### Text 22

*ei navadvīpe mora prakaṭa bihāra*  
*kali āgamane habe śuna vākya-sāra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘When Kali-yuga arrives, I will perform Pastimes in Navadvīpa. Listen to My conclusive words.**

### Text 23

*navadvīpa-sama dhāma nāhi tribhuvane*  
*ati priya-dhāma mora āchhe saṅgopane*

**Перевод:**

**“‘There is no abode like Navadvīpa in the three worlds; all of My most beloved abodes are hidden within it.**

### Text 24

*brahmāvarta saha āchhe puṇya-tīrtha yata*  
*se saba āchhaye hethā śāstrera sammata*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Brahmāvarta and all other holy places that exist are present here. This is the conclusion of the scriptures.**

### Text 25-26

*ye sthāne brahmāra yajñe prakāśa ha-iyā*  
*nāśilāma hiraṇyākṣa dante vidāriyā*

*sei sthāna puṇya-bhūmi ei sthāne raya*  
*yathāya āmāra ebe ha-ila udaya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘The holy place in which I appeared during Brahmā’s sacrifice and killed Hiraṇyākṣa, ripping him with My tusks, exists here where I have now appeared.**

### Text 27

*navadvīpa sevi’ sarva-tīrtha virājaya*  
*navadvīpa-vāse sarva-tīrtha-vāsa haya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘All holy places exist here and serve Navadvīpa. By residing in Navadvīpa, you reside in all the holy places.**

### Text 28

*dhanya tumi navadvīpe sevile āmāya*  
*śrī-gaura-prakaṭa-kāle janmibe hethāya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘You are fortunate: you have served Me in Navadvīpa. You will take birth here when I appear as Śrī Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 29

*anāyāse dekhibe se mahāsaṅkīrtana*  
*apūrva gaurāṅga-rūpa pābe daraśana’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Easily, you will see His grand** ***saṅkīrtana*** **and extraordinary form.’**

### Text 30

*eta bali’ śrī-varāha haila antardhāna*  
*daiva-vāṇī ha-ila vipre bujhite sandhāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Saying this, Śrī Varāha disappeared. A divine voice then arose to guide the** ***brāhmaṇa*****.**

### Text 31

*parama paṇḍita vāsudeva mahāśaya*  
*sarva-śāstra vicāriyā jānila niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Worshippable Vāsudeva was a great scholar. He studied all the scriptures and understood everything clearly.**

### Text 32

*‘vaivasvata-manvantare kalire sandhyāya*  
*śrī-gaurāṅga-prabhu-līlā habe nadīyāya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘At the beginning of Kali-yuga during the reign of Vaivasvata Manu, the Pastimes of Śrī Gaurāṅga will manifest in Nadīyā.**

### Text 33

*ṛṣi-gaṇa sei tattva rākhila gopane*  
*iṅgite kahila saba bujhe vijña-jane*

**Перевод:**

**“‘The sages kept this truth hidden but also hinted at it in the scriptures. The wise understand all of this.**

### Text 34

*‘prakaṭa ha-ile līlā ha-ibe prakāśa*  
*ebe gopya’ ei tattva pāila ābhāsa*

**Перевод:**

**“‘When the Lord appears, His Pastimes will be revealed, but for now they should be kept hidden.’ In this way, Vāsudeva understood the purport of the scriptures.**

### Text 35

*parama ānande vipra kare saṅkīrtana*  
*gaura-nāma gāya mane mane sarva-kṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“With great joy, the** ***brāhmaṇa*** **performed** ***saṅkīrtana*****, chanting the Name of Gaura constantly within his heart.**

### Text 36-37

*parvata-pramāṇa koladevera śarīra*  
*dekhi’ vāsudeva mane vicārila dhīra*

*koladvīpa parvatākhya ei sthāna haya*  
*sei haite parvatākhya haila paricaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Having seen the mountain-like form of Varāhadeva, the wise Vāsudeva decided that this place would be named Koladvīpa Parvata. Since then, it has been known as Parvata.**

### Text 38

*ohe jīva! nitya-līlāmaya vṛndāvane*  
*giri-govardhana ei jāne bhakta-jane*

**Перевод:**

**“O Jīva! Devotees know this place to be Govardhana Hill of Vṛndāvana, which is filled with the Lord’s eternal Pastimes.**

### Text 39

*śrī-bahulāvana dekha ihāra uttare*  
*rūpera chaṭāya sarva-dik śobhā kare*

**Перевод:**

**“See Śrī Bahulāvana to the north. Its effulgence shines in all directions.**

### Text 40

*vṛndāvane ye ye krame dvādaśa kānana*  
*se krama nāhika hethā vallabha-nandana*

**Перевод:**

**“O Jīva, the twelve forests are not arranged here the way they are in Vṛndāvana.**

### Text 41

*prabhu-icchā-mate hethā krama-viparyaya*  
*ihāra tātparya jāne prabhu icchāmaya*

**Перевод:**

**“By the will of the Lord, their order is reversed here. The whimsical Lord knows the reason for this.**

### Text 42

*yei rūpa āchhe hethā dekha sei rūpa*  
*viparyaye prema-vṛddhi ei aparūpa”*

**Перевод:**

**“See the way they are arranged here. This extraordinary reversal increases everyone’s divine love.”**

### Text 43

*kichu-dūra giyā prabhu balena vacana*  
*“ei ye samudragaḍi kara daraśana*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu went ahead and then said, “See here Samudragaḍa.**

### Text 44

*sākṣāt dvārakāpurī śrī-gaṅgā-sāgara*  
*dui tīrtha āchhe hethā dekha vijña-vara*

**Перевод:**

**“O best of the wise, see here both Dvārakā Purī and Śrī Gaṅgā Sāgara.**

### Text 45

*śrī-samudra-sena rājā chila ei-sthāne*  
*baḍa kṛṣṇa-bhakta kṛṣṇa vinā nāhi jāne*

**Перевод:**

**“Śrī Samudra Sen was a king here. He was a great devotee of Kṛṣṇa—he knew nothing but Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 46

*yabe bhīmasena āila nija sainya laye*  
*gherila samudragaḍi baṅga-digvijaye*

**Перевод:**

**“When Bhīmasena came here with his army to conquer Bengal, he surrounded Samudragaḍa.**

### Text 47

*rājā jāne kṛṣṇa eka pāṇḍavera gati*  
*pāṇḍava vipade paile āise yadupati*

**Перевод:**

**“The king knew Kṛṣṇa was the Pāṇḍavas’ only shelter, and that if the Pāṇḍavas faced danger, Kṛṣṇa would come.**

### Text 48

*‘yadi āmi pāri bhīme dekhāite bhaya*  
*bhīma-ārta-nāde hari habe dayāmaya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘If I can cause Bhīma to show that he is afraid, Kṛṣṇa will feel compassion in response to his cries of distress.**

### Text 49

*dayā kari’ āsibena e dāsera deśe*  
*dekhiba se śyāma-mūrti cakṣe anāyāse’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Then Kṛṣṇa will compassionately come to this servant’s land, and I will easily see His dark form with my eyes.’**

### Text 50

*eta bhāvi’ nija sainya sājāila rāya*  
*gaja bāji padātika laye yuddhe yāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Thinking thus, the king prepared his army. Taking his elephants, horses, and foot soldiers, he went to battle.**

### Text 51

*śrī-kṛṣṇa smariyā rājā bāṇa nikṣepaya*  
*bāṇe jara jara bhīma pāila baḍa bhaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Remembering Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the king fired arrows. Overwhelmed by the arrows, Bhīma became extremely afraid.**

### Text 52

*mane mane kṛṣṇe ḍāke vipada dekhiyā*  
*‘rakṣā kara bhīme nātha śrī-caraṇa diyā*

**Перевод:**

**“Seeing that he was in danger, Bhīma called out to Kṛṣṇa within his mind, ‘O Lord! Protect Bhīma with Your holy feet.**

### Text 53

*samudra-senera saha yujhite nā pāri*  
*bhaṅga dile baḍa lajjā tāhā saite nāri*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I cannot fight with Samudra Sen, but I also cannot tolerate the great shame of retreating.**

### Text 54

*pāṇḍavera nātha kṛṣṇa pāi parājaya*  
*baḍa-i lajjāra kathā ohe dayāmaya’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Kṛṣṇa! You are the Lord of the Pāṇḍavas. O merciful Lord! If we are defeated, it will be a great shame.’**

### Text 55

*bhīmera karuṇā-nāda śuni’ dayāmaya*  
*sei yuddha-sthale kṛṣṇa ha-ila udaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing Bhīma’s sorrowful cry, Kṛṣṇa mercifully appeared on the battlefield.**

### Text 56

*nā dekhe se rūpa keha apūrva ghaṭanā*  
*śrī-samudra-sena mātra dekhe eka-janā*

**Перевод:**

**“An extraordinary event then took place: Śrī Samudra Sen saw the Lord’s form, but no one else did.**

### Text 57

*nava-jala-dhara-rūpa kaiśora murati*  
*gale dole vana-mālā mukutāra bhāti*

**Перевод:**

**“The Lord’s adolescent form was the colour of a fresh rain cloud, a forest garland swung on His neck, and His crown shone.**

### Text 58

*sarva-aṅge alaṅkāra ati suśobhana*  
*pīta-vastra paridhāna apūrva gaṭhana*

**Перевод:**

**“He had extremely beautiful ornaments on all His limbs, and His extraordinary body was clothed in yellow garments.**

### Text 59

*se rūpa dekhiyā rājā preme mūrcchā yāya*  
*mūrcchā samvariyā kṛṣṇe prārthanā jānāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Seeing Kṛṣṇa’s form, the king fainted out of divine love. He then steadied himself and prayed to the Lord.**

### Text 60

*‘tumi kṛṣṇa jagannātha patita-pāvana*  
*patita dekhiyā more tava āgamana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Kṛṣṇa, You are the Lord of the universe and the saviour of the fallen. Having seen this fallen soul, You have come here.**

### Text 61

*tava līlā jagaj-jana karaya kīrtana*  
*śuni’ dekhibāra icchā ha-ila takhana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘The souls of this world glorify Your Pastimes, and hearing that, I desired to see You.**

### Text 62-63

*kintu mora vrata chila ohe dayāmaya*  
*ei navadvīpe tava ha-ibe udaya*

*“hethāya dekhiba tava rūpa manohara*  
*navadvīpa chāḍibāre nā haya antara”*

**Перевод:**

**“‘My vow, however, O merciful Lord, was that You would appear in Navadvīpa: “I will see Your enchanting form here so that I do not have to leave Navadvīpa.”**

### Text 64

*sei vrata rakṣā mora kari’ dayāmaya*  
*navadvīpe kṛṣṇa-rūpe haile udaya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O merciful Lord, You have upheld my vow and appeared as Kṛṣṇa in Navadvīpa.**

### Text 65

*tathāpi āmāra icchā ati gūḍha-tara*  
*gaurāṅga ha-una mora akṣira gocara’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Still, my very confidential desire is that You will appear before my eyes as Gaurāṅga.’**

### Text 66

*dekhite dekhite rājā sammukhe dekhila*  
*rādhā-kṛṣṇa-līlā-rūpa mādhurya atula*

**Перевод:**

**“The king suddenly saw before him the incomparably sweet forms and Pastimes of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 67

*śrī-kumudavane kṛṣṇa sakhī-gaṇa sane*  
*aparāhne kare līlā giyā go-cāraṇe*

**Перевод:**

**“Kṛṣṇa was tending cows and playing with His friends in Śrī Kumudavana in the afternoon.**

### Text 68

*kṣaṇeke ha-ila sei līlā adarśana*  
*śrī-gaurāṅga-rūpa here bhariyā nayana*

**Перевод:**

**“Within a moment, these Pastimes disappeared, and the king saw the form of Śrī Gaurāṅga, filling his eyes.**

### Text 69

*mahāsaṅkīrtanāveśa saṅge bhakta-gaṇa*  
*nāciyā nāciyā prabhu karena kīrtana*

**Перевод:**

**“Immersed in grand** ***saṅkīrtana*****, the Lord was dancing and chanting with His devotees.**

### Text 70

*puraṭa-sundara-kānti ati manohara*  
*nayana mātāya ati kaṅpāya antara*

**Перевод:**

**“The Lord’s extremely beautiful golden lustre made the eyes of the king quiver and his heart palpitate intensely.**

### Text 71

*sei rūpa heri’ rājā nije dhanya māne*  
*bahu stava kare tabe gaurāṅga-caraṇe*

**Перевод:**

**“Seeing the form of the Lord, the king considered himself fortunate. He then offered prayers at the Lord’s feet.**

### Text 72

*kata-kṣaṇe se sakala haila adarśana*  
*kaṅdite lāgila rājā haye anya mana*

**Перевод:**

**“Eventually, everything disappeared. The king became thoughtful and began to cry.**

### Text 73

*bhīmasena ei parva nā dekhe nayane*  
*bhāve rājā yuddhe bhīta haila eta-kṣaṇe*

**Перевод:**

**“Bhīmasena did not see this miracle and thought that the king had suddenly become afraid to fight.**

### Text 74

*atyanta vikrama kare pāṇḍura nandana*  
*rājā tuṣṭa haye kara yāce tata-kṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“Bhīmasena showed his immense strength, and the king happily submitted his tribute.**

### Text 75

*kara peye bhīmasena anya sthāne yāya*  
*bhīma digvijaya sarva-jagatete gāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Bhīmasena accepted the tribute and went elsewhere. Still now, the whole world sings of Bhīma as the conqueror of all directions.**

### Text 76

*ei se samudragaḍi navadvīpa-sīmā*  
*brahmā nāhi jāne ei sthānera mahimā*

**Перевод:**

**“This place, Samudragaḍa, is on the boundary of Navadvīpa. Even Brahmā does not know the glories of this place.**

### Text 77

*samudra āsiyā hethā jāhnavī āśraye*  
*prabhu-pada sevā kare bhakta-bhāva laye*

**Перевод:**

**“The ocean came to the shelter of the Gaṅgā here and, with the mood of a devotee, served the feet of the Lord.**

### Text 78

*jāhnavī balena, ‘sindhu ati alpa-dine*  
*tava tīre prabhu mora rahibe vipine’*

**Перевод:**

**“The Gaṅgā said, ‘O Ocean, very soon my Lord will stay in a forest on your bank.’**

### Text 79

*sindhu bale, ‘śuna devī āmāra vacana*  
*navadvīpa nāhi chāḍe śacīra nandana*

**Перевод:**

**“The ocean said, ‘O goddess, listen to my words! The son of Śacī never leaves Navadvīpa.**

### Text 80

*yadyapio kichu-dina rahe mama tīre*  
*apratyakṣa rahe tabu nadīyā bhitare*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Although He will stay on my bank for some time, He will remain invisibly in Nadīyā.**

### Text 81

*nitya-dhāma navadvīpa prabhura hethāya*  
*prakaṭa o aprakaṭa līlā vede gāya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘The** ***Vedas*** **sing of the manifest and unmanifest Pastimes of the Lord here in His eternal abode of Navadvīpa.**

### Text 82

*hethā tavāśraye āmi rahiba sundari*  
*seviba navadvīpe śrī-gaurāṅga hari’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O beautiful goddess! I will stay here under your shelter and serve Śrī Gaurāṅga in Navadvīpa.’**

### Text 83

*ei bali’ payo-nidhi navadvīpe raya*  
*gaurāṅgera nitya-līlā satata cintaya”*

**Перевод:**

**“Saying this, the ocean stayed in Navadvīpa, meditating on the eternal Pastimes of Gaurāṅga.”**

### Text 84

*tabe nityānanda āila campāhaṭṭa grāma*  
*vāṇīnātha-gṛhe tathā karila viśrāma*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu then came to Campāhaṭṭa Grāma and rested there in the home of Vāṇīnātha.**

### Text 85

*aparāhne campāhaṭṭa karaya bhramaṇa*  
*nityānanda bale, “śuna vallabha-nandana*

**Перевод:**

**In the afternoon, Nityānanda toured through Campāhaṭṭa and said, “O Jīva, listen!**

### Text 86

*ei sthāne chila pūrve campaka-kānana*  
*khadiravanera aṁśa sundara darśana*

**Перевод:**

**“Previously, there was a beautiful** ***campaka*** **forest here that was part of Khadiravana.**

### Text 87

*campakalatā sakhī nitya campaka la-iyā*  
*mālā gaṅthi’ rādhā-kṛṣṇe sevitena giyā*

**Перевод:**

**“Daily, Campakalatā Sakhī would pick** ***campaka*** **flowers here, string garlands, and serve Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 88

*kali vṛddhi haile sei campaka kānane*  
*māli-gaṇa phula laya ati hṛṣṭa-mane*

**Перевод:**

**“When Kali-yuga started, garland-makers eagerly began to take the** ***campaka*** **flowers from the forest.**

### Text 89

*haṭṭa kari’ campaka-kusuma laye vasi’*  
*vikraya karaya laya yata grāma-vāsī*

**Перевод:**

**“They established a market and resided here. They would take the flowers and sell them, and the villagers would buy them.**

### Text 90

*sei haite śrī-campakahaṭṭa haila nāma*  
*caṅpāhāṭi sabe bale manohara dhāma*

**Перевод:**

**“As a result, this beautiful place was named Śrī Campakahaṭṭa, and people began to call it Caṅpāhāṭi.**

### Text 91

*ye kāle lakṣmaṇa-sena nadīyāra rājā*  
*jayadeva navadvīpe hana taṅra prajā*

**Перевод:**

**“When Lakṣmaṇa Sen was the king of Nadīyā, Jayadeva Gosvāmī was one of His subjects in Navadvīpa.**

### Text 92

*ballāla-dīrghikā-kūle baṅdhiyā kuṭīra*  
*padmā-saha vaise tathā jayadeva dhīra*

**Перевод:**

**“The wise Jayadeva built a cottage on the bank of Ballāl Dīrghikā and resided there with his wife Padmāvatī.**

### Text 93

*daśa-avatāra stava racila tathāya*  
*sei stava lakṣmaṇera haste kabhu yāya*

**Перевод:**

**“While there, he wrote the** ***Daśa-avatāra-stotram*****, and this poem once came to the hand of Lakṣmaṇa Sen.**

### Text 94

*parama ānande stava karila paṭhana*  
*jijñāsila rājā, ‘stava kaila kon jana’*

**Перевод:**

**“The king read the poem with great joy and then asked, ‘Who wrote this?’**

### Text 95

*govardhana ācārya rājāre tabe kaya*  
*‘mahākavi jayadeva racayitā haya’*

**Перевод:**

**“Govardhana Ācārya said to the king, ‘The great poet Jayadeva wrote it.’**

### Text 96

*‘kothā jayadeva kavi’ jijñāse bhūpati*  
*govardhana bale, ‘ei navadvīpe sthiti’*

**Перевод:**

**“The king asked, ‘Where is this poet Jayadeva?’ Govardhana replied, ‘He lives in Navadvīpa.’**

### Text 97

*śuniyā gopane rājā kariyā sandhana*  
*rātra-yoge āila tabe jayadeva-sthāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing this, the king secretly searched for Jayadeva’s home and then came there in the evening.**

### Text 98

*vaiṣṇava-veśete rājā kuṭire praveśa*  
*jayadeve nati kari’ baise eka-deśe*

**Перевод:**

**“The king entered the cottage in the dress of a Vaiṣṇava. He bowed to Jayadeva and sat on one side.**

### Text 99

*jayadeva jānilena bhūpati e jana*  
*vaiṣṇava-veśete āila haye akiñcana*

**Перевод:**

**“Jayadeva understood that this man was the king and that he had come to him as a beggar in the dress of a Vaiṣṇava.**

### Text 100

*alpa-kṣaṇe rājā tabe deya paricaya*  
*jayadeve yāce yāite āpana ālaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Within a short time, the king introduced himself and requested Jayadeva to go to his palace.**

### Text 101

*atyanta virakta jayadeva mahāmati*  
*viṣayi-gṛhete yete nā kare sammati*

**Перевод:**

**“The wise Jayadeva was extremely detached, and he did not agree to go to the home of any materialist.**

### Text 102

*kṛṣṇa-bhakta jayadeva balila takhana*  
*‘tava deśa chāḍi’ āmi kariba gamana*

**Перевод:**

**“Jayadeva, the great devotee of Kṛṣṇa, then said, ‘I will leave your province and go elsewhere.**

### Text 103

*viṣayi-saṁsarga kabhu nā deya maṅgala*  
*gaṅgā pāra haye yāba yathā nīlācala’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Association with materialists never results in good fortune, so I will cross the Gaṅgā and go to Nīlācala.’**

### Text 104

*rājā bale, ‘śuna prabhu āmāra vacana*  
*navadvīpa tyāga nāhi kara kadācana*

**Перевод:**

**“The king said, ‘O master, listen to my words. Please do not ever leave Navadvīpa.**

### Text 105

*tava vākya satya habe mora icchā rabe*  
*hena kārya kara deva more kṛpā yabe*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Please be merciful to me and act in such a way that your words will come true and my desire will also be fulfilled.**

### Text 106

*gaṅgā-pāre campāhaṭṭa sthāna manohara*  
*sei sthāne thāka tumi du’ eka vatsara*

**Перевод:**

**“‘On the other side of the Gaṅgā, there is a beautiful place known as Campāhaṭṭa. Please stay there for a couple of years.**

### Text 107

*mama icchā-mate āmi tathā nā yāiba*  
*tava icchā hale tava caraṇa heriba’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I will not go there according to my own desire; I will come to see your feet only when you desire it.’**

### Text 108

*rāyāra vacana śuni’ mahākavi-vara*  
*sammata ha-iyā bale vacana satvara*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing the words of the king, the great poet immediately replied in agreement.**

### Text 109

*‘yadyapi viṣayī tumi e rājya tomāra*  
*kṛṣṇa-bhakta tumi tava nāhika saṁsāra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Although you are a materialist and this kingdom is yours, you are a devotee of Kṛṣṇa and you have no material attachment.**

### Text 110

*parīkṣā karite āmi viṣayī baliyā*  
*sambhāṣiṇu tabu tumi sahile śuniyā*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I called you a materialist to test you, but you heard this and tolerated it.**

### Text 111

*ataeva jānilāma tumi kṛṣṇa-bhakta*  
*viṣaya la-iyā phira haye anāsakta*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I have now understood that you are a devotee of Kṛṣṇa. You engage in material affairs but remain detached.**

### Text 112

*campaka-haṭṭete āmi kichu-dina raba*  
*gopane āsibe tumi chāḍiyā vaibhava’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I will stay in Campakahaṭṭa for some time. In secret, leaving your opulence behind, you may come to see me.’**

### Text 113

*hṛṣṭa-citta haye rājā amātya dvārāya*  
*campaka-haṭṭete gṛha nirmāṇa karāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Through one of his ministers, the king happily had a house constructed in Campakahaṭṭa.**

### Text 114

*tathā jayadeva kavi rahe dina kata*  
*śrī-kṛṣṇa-bhajana kare rāga-mārga mata*

**Перевод:**

**“The poet Jayadeva stayed there for some time and served Śrī Kṛṣṇa according to the path of divine love.**

### Text 115

*padmāvatī devī āne campakera bhāra*  
*jayadeva pūje kṛṣṇa nandera kumāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Padmāvatī Devī would bring loads of** ***campaka*** **flowers, and Jayadeva worshipped Kṛṣṇa, the son of Nanda, with them.**

### Text 116

*mahāpreme jayadeva karaye pūjana*  
*dekhila śrī-kṛṣṇa haila campaka-varaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“Jayadeva offered worship with intense love, and eventually he saw Śrī Kṛṣṇa appear before him in a form the colour of a** ***campaka*** **flower.**

### Text 117

*puraṭa-sundara-kānti ati manohara*  
*koṭi-candra-nindi mukha parama sundara*

**Перевод:**

**“The Lord’s beautiful, golden lustre was completely enchanting, and His extremely beautiful face belittled millions of moons.**

### Text 118

*caṅcara cikura śobhā gale phula-mālā*  
*dīrgha-bāhu rūpe ālo kare parṇa-śālā*

**Перевод:**

**“He had wavy hair, long arms, and a beautiful flower garland around His neck. His body illumined the thatched hut of Jayadeva.**

### Text 119

*dekhiyā gaurāṅga-rūpa mahākavi-vara*  
*preme mūrcchā yāya cakṣe aśru jhara jhara*

**Перевод:**

**“Seeing the form of Gaurāṅga, Jayadeva, the best of poets, fainted in divine love as tears streamed from his eyes.**

### Text 120

*padmāvatī devī sei rūpa nirakhiyā*  
*ha-ila caitanya-hīna bhūmete paḍiyā*

**Перевод:**

**“Seeing the Lord’s form, Padmāvatī Devī fainted and fell to the ground.**

### Text 121

*padma-hasta diyā prabhu tole dui jane*  
*kṛpā kari’ bale tabe amiya-vacane*

**Перевод:**

**“The Lord raised them both with His lotus hands and then mercifully spoke nectarean words.**

### Text 122

*‘tumi doṅhe mama bhakta parama udāra*  
*daraśana dite icchā ha-ila āmāra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘You are both My highly exalted devotees, and I desired to reveal Myself to you.**

### Text 123

*ati alpa-dine ei nadīyā nagare*  
*janama la-iba āmi śacīra udare*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Very soon I will take birth from the womb of Śacī Devī in Nadīyā.**

### Text 124

*sarva-avatāre sakala-bhakta sane*  
*śrī-kṛṣṇa-kīrtane vitariba prema-dhane*

**Перевод:**

**“‘With all the devotees of all My previous** ***avatāras*****, I will distribute the wealth of divine love through** ***śrī kṛṣṇa-kīrtana*****.**

### Text 125

*cabbiśa vatsare āmi kariyā sannyāsa*  
*kariba avaśya nīlācalete nivāsa*

**Перевод:**

**“‘At the age of twenty-four, I will take** ***sannyāsa*** **and then reside in Nīlācala.**

### Text 126

*tathā bhakta-gaṇa saṅge mahāpremāveśe*  
*śrī-gīta-govinda āsvādiba avaśeṣe*

**Перевод:**

**“‘There, immersed in intense divine love, I will deeply relish your** ***Śrī Gīta-govinda*** **with My devotees.**

### Text 127

*tava viracita gīta-govinda āmāra*  
*atiśaya priya-vastu kahilāma sāra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I am telling you the truth: your** ***Gīta-govinda*** **is extremely dear to Me.**

### Text 128

*ei navadvīpa-dhāma parama cinmaya*  
*dehānte āsibe hethā kahinu niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Navadvīpa Dhāma is spiritual and supreme; I promise you that you will come here after leaving your body.**

### Text 129

*ebe tumi doṅhe yāo yathā nīlācala*  
*jagannāthe seva giyā pābe prema-phala’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘For now, you two will go to Nīlācala. Serve Jagannātha there, and you will attain the fruit of divine love.’**

### Text 130

*eta bali’ gaura-candra haila adarśana*  
*prabhura vicchede murcchā haya dui-jana*

**Перевод:**

**“Saying this, Gaura-candra disappeared. In separation from the Lord, Jayadeva and Padmāvatī fainted.**

### Text 131

*mūrcchā-śeṣe anargala kaṅdite lāgila*  
*kaṅdite kaṅdite saba nivedana kaila*

**Перевод:**

**“Regaining consciousness, they began to cry profusely. Crying and crying, they offered prayers.**

### Text 132

*‘hāya kibā rūpa morā dekhinu nayane*  
*kemane vaṅciba ebe taṅra adarśane*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Alas! What a form we have seen! How will we now live in separation from Him?**

### Text 133

*nadīyā chāḍite prabhu kena ājñā kaila*  
*bujhi ei dhāme kichu aparādha haila*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Why did the Lord order us to leave Nadīyā? We understand that we must have made some offence here.**

### Text 134

*ei navadvīpa-dhāma parama cinmaya*  
*chāḍite mānasa ebe vikalita haya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Navadvīpa Dhāma is spiritual and supreme, and our hearts are dismayed at the thought of leaving.**

### Text 135

*bhāla haita navadvīpe paśu pakṣī haye*  
*thākitāma cira-dina dhāma-cintā laye*

**Перевод:**

**“‘It would be better to become an animal or bird and remain here forever, meditating on the Dhāma.**

### Text 136

*parāṇa chāḍite pāri tabu ei dhāma*  
*chāḍite nā pāri ei gūḍha manaskāma*

**Перевод:**

**“‘We can give up our lives, but we cannot give up the Dhāma—our attachment is so intense.**

### Text 137

*ohe prabhu śrī-gaurāṅga kṛpā vitariyā*  
*rākha āmā doṅhe hethā śrī-caraṇa diyā’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Lord! Śrī Gaurāṅga! Be merciful and keep us here at Your holy feet.’**

### Text 138

*balite balite doṅhe kaṅde ucharāya*  
*daiva-vāṇī sei-kṣaṇe śunibāre pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“While speaking in this way, they cried aloud, and then they heard a divine voice.**

### Text 139

*‘duḥkha nāhi kara doṅhe yāo nīlācala*  
*dui kathā habe citta nā kara cañcala*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Go to Nīlācala, and do not be sad or unsteady at heart. I will tell you two things.**

### Text 140

*kichu-dina pūrve doṅhe karile mānasa*  
*nīlācale vāsa kari kataka divasa*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Some time ago, you both desired to stay in Nīlācala for a few days.**

### Text 141

*sei vāñcā jagabandhu pūrāila tava*  
*jagannātha cāhe tava darśana sambhava*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Jagannātha has fulfilled this desire of yours, and He wants to see you.**

### Text 142

*jagannāthe tuṣi’ punaḥ chāḍiyā śarīra*  
*navadvīpe dui-jane nitya habe sthira’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘You two will please Jagannātha, again leave your bodies, and then reside in Navadvīpa eternally.’**

### Text 143

*daiva-vāṇī śuni’ doṅhe cale tata-kṣaṇa*  
*pāche phiri navadvīpa karena darśana*

**Перевод:**

**“Upon hearing the divine voice, Jayadeva and Padmāvatī immediately left, and after some time, they turned and looked back at Navadvīpa.**

### Text 144

*chala chala kare netra jaladhārā vahe*  
*navadvīpa-vāsi-gaṇe dainya-vākya kahe*

**Перевод:**

**“They wept, and tears streamed from their eyes. Humbly, they spoke to the residents of Navadvīpa.**

### Text 145

*‘tomarā kariyā kṛpā ei dui jane*  
*aparādha kariyāchhi karaha mārjane’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Please be merciful to these two souls. Please forgive us for the offences we have committed.’**

### Text 146

*aṣṭa-dala padma-sama navadvīpa bhāya*  
*dekhite dekhite doṅhe kata-dūre yāya*

**Перевод:**

**“They walked away, looking back again and again at the eight-petalled lotus of Navadvīpa.**

### Text 147

*dūre giyā navadvīpa nāhi dekhe āra*  
*kaṅdite kaṅdite gauḍa-bhūmi haya pāra*

**Перевод:**

**“They went ahead and eventually did not see Navadvīpa anymore. Crying, they crossed outside the land of Gauḍa.**

### Text 148

*kata-dine nīlācale pauṅchiyā dui jane*  
*jagannātha daraśana kaila hṛṣṭa-mane*

**Перевод:**

**“Some time later, they arrived in Nīlācala and saw Jagannātha with cheerful hearts.**

### Text 149

*ohe jīva ei jayadeva-sthāna haya*  
*uccha-bhūmi mātra āchhe vṛddha-loke kaya”*

**Перевод:**

**“O Jīva, this is the place of Jayadeva. The elders say that only this raised land is what remains of it.”**

### Text 150

*jayadeva-sthāna dekhi’ śrī-jīva takhana*  
*preme gaḍāgaḍi yāya karaya rodana*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the place of Jayadeva, Śrī Jīva rolled on the ground and wept in divine love.**

### Text 151

*“dhanya jayadeva kavi dhanya padmāvatī*  
*śrī-gīta-govinda dhanya dhanya kṛṣṇa-rati*

**Перевод:**

**“Glory to the poet Jayadeva! Glory to Padmāvatī! Glory to** ***Śrī Gīta-govinda*****! Glory to love for Kṛṣṇa!**

### Text 152

*jayadeva bhoga kaila yei prema-sindhu*  
*kṛpā kari’ deha more tāra eka-bindu”*

**Перевод:**

**“O Lord, mercifully give me a drop of the ocean of divine love that Jayadeva relished.”**

### Text 153

*ei kathā bali’ jīva daraṇī loṭāya*  
*nityānanda-śrī-caraṇe gaḍāgaḍi yāya*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this, Jīva tumbled to the ground and rolled at the holy feet of Nityānanda Prabhu.**

### Text 154

*sei rātra sabe raya vāṇīnātha-ghare*  
*vaṁśa-saha vaṇī nityānanda-sevā kare*

**Перевод:**

**That night they stayed at the house of Vāṇīnātha, and he and his family served Nityānanda Prabhu.**

### Text 155

*nitāi-jāhnavā-pada-chāyā āśa yāra*  
*nadīyā-māhātmya gāya akiñcana chāra*

**Перевод:**

**This poor, fallen soul, whose aspiration is the shade of Nitāi and Jāhnavā’s feet, chants the glories of Nadīyā.**

## Chapter Twelve – Śrī Śrī Ṛtudvīpa and Śrī Rādhā Kuṇḍa

### Text 1

*jaya śrī-caitanya-candra jaya prabhu nityānanda*  
*jayādvaita jaya gadādhara*  
*śrīvāsādi bhakta jaya jaya jagannāthālaya*  
*jaya navadvīpa-dhāma-vara*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to the moon-like Śrī Caitanya, Nityānanda Prabhu, Advaita, Gadādhara, Śrīvāsa, and all the devotees! All glory to the home of Jagannātha Miśra and the best of abodes, Śrī Navadvīpa!**

### Text 2

*prabhāta ha-ila rātra bhakta-gaṇa tule gātra*  
*śrī-gaura nitāicāṅde ḍāke*  
*bhakta-saha nityānanda cale bhaji’ parānanda*  
*campāhaṭṭa paścātete rākhe*

**Перевод:**

**At dawn, the devotees arose and called out to Śrī Gaura and Nitāi-cāṅda. Feeling great joy, Nityānanda and the devotees then departed, leaving Campāhaṭṭa behind.**

### Text 3

*tathā haite vāṇīnātha cale nityānanda-sātha*  
*bale, “hena dina kabe pāba*  
*nitāi-cāṅdera saṅge parikramā kari’ raṅge*  
*māyāpura prabhu-gṛhe yāba”*

**Перевод:**

**From then on, Vāṇīnātha came with Nityānanda. He considered, “When else will I get the chance to joyfully circumambulate Māyāpura with Nitāi-cāṅda and go to the home of the Lord?”**

### Text 4

*dekhite dekhite tabe rātupura cale sabe*  
*dekhi’ sei nagarera śobhā*  
*prabhu nityānanda bale, “ṛtudvīpe āila cale*  
*ei sthāna ati mano-lobhā*

**Перевод:**

**Eventually, they came to Rātupura. Upon seeing the beauty of the town, Nityānanda Prabhu said, “We have come to Ṛtudvīpa, which is extremely enchanting.**

### Text 5

*vṛkṣa saba nata-śira pavana vahaye dhīra*  
*kusuma phuṭechhe cāri-bhita*  
*bhṛṅgera jhaṅkāra rava kusumera gandhāsaba*  
*mātāya pathika-gaṇa-citta”*

**Перевод:**

**“The trees bow their heads, the breeze blows gently, and the flowers bloom in all directions. The humming of the bees and the fragrant nectar of the flowers intoxicate the hearts of travellers.”**

### Text 6

*balite balite rāya haila pāgalera prāya*  
*bale, “śiṅgā āne śīghra-gati*  
*vatsa-gaṇa yāya dūre kānāi nidrita pure*  
*ekhana nā āise śiśu-mati*

**Перевод:**

**As He spoke, Nityānanda Rāya became almost mad and said, “Quickly bring My horn! The calves have run off, and Kānāi is asleep. Like a child, He does not come now.**

### Text 7

*kothāya subala dāma āmi ekā balarāma*  
*gocāraṇe yāite nā pāri”*  
*“kānāi kānāi” bali’ ḍāka chāḍe mahābalī*  
*lāpha māre hāta dui cāri*

**Перевод:**

**“Where are Subala and Śrīdāma? I, Balarāma, cannot go out to tend the cows alone.” With great power, Nityānanda called out, “Kānāi! Kānāi!” and jumped a couple of metres.**

### Text 8

*se bhāva darśana kari’ bhakta-gaṇa tvarā kari’*  
*nivedaya nitāiyera pāya*  
*“ohe prabhu nityānanda bhāi tava gaura-candra*  
*nāhi ebe āchhena hethāya*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing His ecstasy, the devotees quickly submitted at His feet, “O Nityānanda Prabhu! Your brother Gaura-candra is not here now.**

### Text 9

*sannyāsa kariyā hari gela nīlācalopari*  
*āmādera kāṅgāla kariyā”*  
*tāhā śuni’ nityānanda ha-ilena nirānanda*  
*kāṅdi loṭe bhūmete paḍiyā*

**Перевод:**

**“The Lord has taken** ***sannyāsa*** **and gone to Nīlācala, leaving us destitute.” Hearing this, Nityānanda became dismayed. He fell to the ground and wept.**

### Text 10

*“ki duḥkhe kānāi bhāi āmā sabe chāḍi’ yāi’*  
*sannyāsī ha-ila nīlācale*  
*e jīvana nā rākhiba yamunāya jhāṅpa diba”*  
*bali’ acetana sei sthale*

**Перевод:**

**“What sorrow! Brother Kānāi! You left us and became a** ***sannyāsī*** **in Nīlācala! I cannot continue this life; I will dive into the Yamunā.” Saying this, Nityānanda lost consciousness.**

### Text 11

*nityānande mahābhāva kari’ sabe anubhava*  
*hari-nāma saṅkīrtana kare*  
*cāri-daṇḍa dina haila nityānanda nā uṭhila*  
*bhakta saba gaura-gīta dhare*

**Перевод:**

**Everyone saw the intense ecstasy of Nityānanda and chanted the Lord’s Name. An hour and a half passed, but Nityānanda did not get up. Finally, the devotees sang songs about Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 12

*gaurāṅgera nāma śuni’ nitāi uṭhe amani*  
*bale, “ei rādhā-kuṇḍa-sthāna*  
*hethā bhakta saṅge kari’ aparāhne gaurahari*  
*karitena kīrtana vidhāna*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the Name of Gaurāṅga, Nitāi immediately arose and said, “This is the place of Rādhā Kuṇḍa. Here, Gaurahari would perform** ***kīrtana*** **with the devotees in the afternoon.**

### Text 13

*hethā chaya ṛtu meli’ gaurāṅga-kīrtana-keli*  
*puṣṭa kaila śobhā vistāriyā*  
*rādhā-kuṇḍa vraje yei ṛtudvīpa hethā sei*  
*bhakta hethā maje prema piyā*

**Перевод:**

**“Here, the six seasons meet and enhance the beauty of Gaurāṅga’s** ***kīrtana*** **Pastimes. Rādhā Kuṇḍa in Vraja is present here in Ṛtudvīpa, where devotees drink and relish divine love.**

### Text 14

*dekha śyāma-kuṇḍa-śobhā jagaj-jana-mano-lobhā*  
*sakhī-gaṇa-kuñja nānā-sthāne*  
*hethā aparāhne gorā saṅkīrtane haye bhorā*  
*tuṣilena sabe prema-dāne*

**Перевод:**

**“See also the beauty of Śyāma Kuṇḍa, which enchants the mind of every soul in this world, and the groves of the** ***sakhīs*** **nearby. Gorā would become immersed in** ***saṅkīrtana*** **here in the afternoon and satisfy everyone with the gift of divine love.**

### Text 15

*e sthāna samāna bhāi trijagate nāhi pāi*  
*bhaktera bhajana-sthāna jāna*  
*hethāya vasati yāṅra prema-dhana lābha tāṅra*  
*suśītala haya tāṅra prāṇa”*

**Перевод:**

**“Brother! We find no place like this in the three worlds. Know it to be a place for the devotees to serve. Souls who reside here attain the wealth of divine love, and their hearts become serene.”**

### Text 16

*se dina se sthāne thāki’ śrī-gaurāṅga-nāma ḍāki’*  
*preme magna sarva-bhakta-gaṇa*  
*ṛtudvīpe sabe vasi’ bhaje śrī-caitanya-śaśī*  
*rātra-dina karila yāpana*

**Перевод:**

**Remaining there for the day, calling the Name of Śrī Gaurāṅga, all the devotees became immersed in divine love. They spent the day and night in Ṛtudvīpa and worshipped the moon-like Śrī Caitanya.**

### Text 17

*nācite nācite tabe nityānanda cale yabe*  
*śrī-vidyā-nagare upanīta*  
*vidyā-nagarera śobhā muni-jana-mano-lobhā*  
*bhakta-gaṇa dekhi’ praphullita*

**Перевод:**

**Then, dancing as He went, Nityānanda Prabhu arrived in Śrī Vidyā Nagara. The beauty of the town attracts the minds of sages, and upon seeing it, the devotees were jubilant.**

### Text 18

*nitāi-jāhnavā-pada ye janāra susampada*  
*se bhakti-vinoda akiñcana*  
*nadīyā-māhātmya gāya dhari’ bhakta-jana-pāya*  
*yāche mātra kṛṣṇa-bhakti-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**The poor Bhaktivinoda, whose true wealth is the feet of Nitāi and Jāhnavā, chants the glories of Nadīyā. Grasping the feet of the devotees, he prays only for the wealth of devotion to Kṛṣṇa.**

## Chapter Thirteen – Śrī Vidyā Nagara and Śrī Jahnudvīpa

### Text 1

*jaya gaura nityānandādvaita gadādhara*  
*śrīvāsa śrī-navadvīpa kīrtana-sāgara*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to Gaura, Nityānanda, Advaita, Gadādhara, Śrīvāsa, and the ocean of** ***kīrtana*****, Śrī Navadvīpa Dhāma!**

### Text 2

*śrī-vidyā-nagare āsi’ nityānanda-rāya*  
*vidyā-nagarera tattva śrī-jīve śikhāya*

**Перевод:**

**Coming to Vidyā Nagara, Nityānanda Rāya taught Śrī Jīva the importance of the town.**

### Text 3

*“nitya-dhāma navadvīpa pralaya-samaye*  
*aṣṭa-dala padma-rūpe thāke śuddha haye*

**Перевод:**

**“During the destruction of the universe, the eternal abode of Navadvīpa remains undisturbed as an eight-petalled lotus.**

### Text 4

*sarva-avatāra āra dhanya-jīva yata*  
*kamalera ekadeśe thāke kata śata*

**Перевод:**

**“All the** ***avatāras*** **and thousands of fortunate souls stay in one part of that lotus.**

### Text 5

*ṛtudvīpa antargata e vidyā-nagare*  
*matsya-rūpī bhagavān sarva-veda dhare*

**Перевод:**

**“In Vidyā Nagara within Ṛtudvīpa, the Lord, as Matsya, holds all the** ***Vedas*****.**

### Text 6

*sarva-vidyā thāke veda āśraya kariyā*  
*śrī-vidyā-nagara nāma ei sthāne diyā*

**Перевод:**

**“The** ***Vedas*****, which contain all knowledge, took shelter and stayed here. They named this place Śrī Vidyā Nagara.**

### Text 7

*punaḥ yabe sṛṣṭi-mukhe brahmā mahāśaya*  
*ati bhīta hana dekhi’ sakala pralaya*

**Перевод:**

**“When Brahmā desired to create the world again, he saw the full devastation and became extremely afraid.**

### Text 8

*sei kāle prabhu-kṛpā haya tāṅra prati*  
*ei sthāne peye bhagavāne kare stuti*

**Перевод:**

**“At that time, the Lord bestowed mercy upon him. He received that mercy here and then prayed to the Lord.**

### Text 9

*mukha khulibāra kāle devī sarasvatī*  
*brahma-jihvā haite janme ati rūpavatī*

**Перевод:**

**“As he opened his mouths, the extremely beautiful goddess Sarasvatī manifested from his tongues.**

### Text 10

*sarasvatī-śakti peye deva-catur-mukha*  
*śrī-kṛṣṇe karena stava peye baḍa sukha*

**Перевод:**

**“Becoming empowered by Sarasvatī, four-headed Brahmā prayed to Kṛṣṇa and felt great satisfaction.**

### Text 11

*sṛṣṭi yabe haya māyā sarva-dika gheri’*  
*virajāra pāre thāke guṇa-traya dhari’*

**Перевод:**

**“When creation takes place, Māyā remains on the bank of the Virajā River and envelops all directions with the three modes of material nature.**

### Text 12

*māyā prakāśita viśve vidyāra prakāśa*  
*kare ṛṣi-gaṇa tabe kariyā prayāsa*

**Перевод:**

**“Then, within that world manifested by Māyā, the sages reveal knowledge.**

### Text 13

*ei ta sāradā-pīṭha kariyā āśraya*  
*ṛṣi-gaṇa kare avidyāra parājaya*

**Перевод:**

**“They take shelter in this abode of Sarasvatī and conquer ignorance.**

### Text 14

*cauṣaṭṭi vidyāra pāṭha laye ṛṣi-gaṇa*  
*dharā-tale sthāne sthāne kare vijñāpana*

**Перевод:**

**“They study the sixty-four branches of knowledge and then teach them at various places on the earth.**

### Text 15

*ye ye ṛṣi ye ye vidyā kare adhyayana*  
*ei pīṭhe se sabāra sthāna anukṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“The residences of all the sages who study each and every type of knowledge are present here eternally.**

### Text 16

*śrī-vālmīki kāvya-rasa ei sthāne pāya*  
*nārada-kṛpāya te̐ha āilā hethāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Śrī Vālmīki came here by the grace of Nārada and learned the art of poetry.**

### Text 17

*dhanvantari āsi’ hethā āyurveda pāya*  
*viśvāmitra ādi dhanur-vidyā śikhi’ yāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Dhanvantari came here and learned** ***āyurveda*****. Viśvāmitra and others learned archery.**

### Text 18

*śaunakādi ṛṣi-gaṇa paḍe veda-mantra*  
*deva-deva mahādeva ālocaya tantra*

**Перевод:**

**“Śaunaka and other sages studied Vedic mantras. The god of gods, Lord Śiva, studied** ***tantra*****.**

### Text 19

*brahmā-cāri-mukha haite veda catuṣṭaya*  
*ṛṣi-gaṇa prārthanāya karila udaya*

**Перевод:**

**“At the request of the sages, the four** ***Vedas*** **manifested from the four mouths of Lord Brahmā.**

### Text 20

*kapila racila sāṅkhya ei sthāne vasi’*  
*nyāya tarka prakāśila śrī-gautama ṛṣi*

**Перевод:**

**“Kapila resided here and wrote about** ***sāṅkhya*****, and Śrī Gautama Ṛṣi wrote texts about logic and argument.**

### Text 21

*vaiśeṣika prakāśila kaṇabhuk muni*  
*pātañjali yoga-śāstra prakāśe āpani*

**Перевод:**

**“Kaṇāda wrote about** ***vaiśeṣika*****, and Pātañjali wrote texts on yoga.**

### Text 22

*jaiminī mīmāṁsā śāstra karila prakāśa*  
*purāṇādi prakāśila ṛṣi vedavyāsa*

**Перевод:**

**“Jaiminī wrote texts about** ***mīmāṁsā*****, and Vedavyāsa wrote the** ***Purāṇas*** **and other texts.**

### Text 23

*pañcarātra nāradādi ṛṣi pañca-jana*  
*prakāśiyā jīva-gaṇe śikhāya sādhana*

**Перевод:**

**“Nārada and four other sages wrote the** ***Pañcarātras*** **and taught souls spiritual practices.**

### Text 24

*ei upavane sarva-upaniṣad-gaṇa*  
*bahu-kāla śrī-gaurāṅga kare ārādhana*

**Перевод:**

**“In this garden, all the** ***Upaniṣads*** **worshipped Śrī Gaurāṅga for a long time.**

### Text 25

*alakṣye śrī-gaura se sabe kahila*  
*‘nirākāra-buddhi tava hṛdaya dūṣila*

**Перевод:**

**“Invisibly, Śrī Gaura said to them, ‘The conception of formlessness has polluted your hearts.**

### Text 26-27

*tumi sabe śruti-rūpe more nā pāibe*  
*āmāra pārṣada-rūpe yabe janma labe*

*prakaṭa-līlāya tabe dekhibe āmāya*  
*mama guṇa kīrtana karibe ubharāya’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘As scriptures, you will not attain Me, but when you take birth as My associates in My manifest Pastimes, you will see Me and loudly chant My glories.’**

### Text 28

*tāhā śuni’ śruti-gaṇa nistabdha ha-iyā*  
*gopane āchhila hethā kāla apekṣiyā*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing this, the** ***Upaniṣads*** **became silent and secretly waited here.**

### Text 29

*ei dhanya kali-yuga sarva-yuga-sāra*  
*yāhāte ha-ila śrī-gaurāṅga avatāra*

**Перевод:**

**“This glorious Age of Kali, in which Śrī Gaurāṅga has descended, is the best of all ages.**

### Text 30

*vidyā-līlā karibena gaurāṅga-sundara*  
*gaṇa-saha bṛhaspati janme ataḥpara*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing that Gaurāṅgasundara would perform Pastimes of scholarship, Bṛhaspati and his associates took birth before the Lord.**

### Text 31

*vāsudeva sārvabhauma sei bṛhaspati*  
*gaurāṅge tuṣite yatna karilena ati*

**Перевод:**

**“As Vāsudeva Sārvabhauma, Bṛhaspati endeavoured greatly to please Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 32-33

*‘prabhu mora navadvīpe śrī-vidyā-vilāsa*  
*karibena’ jāni’ mane ha-iyā udāsa*

*indra-sabhā parihari’ nija-gaṇa laye*  
*janmilena sthāne sthāne ānandita haye*

**Перевод:**

**“Knowing within his heart, ‘My Lord will perform Pastimes of scholarship in Navadvīpa’, and becoming detached, Bṛhaspati left the assembly of Indra with his associates. Joyfully, he and his associates took birth in various places.**

### Text 34

*ei vidyā-nagarīte kari’ vidyālaya*  
*vidyā pracārila sārvabhauma mahāśaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Bṛhaspati made a school in Vidyā Nagara and taught here as Sārvabhauma Bhaṭṭācārya.**

### Text 35

*‘pāchhe vidyā-jāle ḍube hārāi gaurāṅga’*  
*ei mane kari’ eka karilena raṅga*

**Перевод:**

**“Thinking, ‘Later, I may sink into the net of knowledge and forget Gaurāṅga’, he performed a trick.**

### Text 36

*nija śiṣya-gaṇe rākhi’ nadīyā-nagare*  
*gaura-janma pūrve te̐ha gelā deśāntare*

**Перевод:**

**“Keeping his disciples in Nadīyā, he went elsewhere before the birth of Gaura.**

### Text 37

*mane bhāve, ‘yadi āmi ha-i gaura-dāsa*  
*kṛpā kari’ more prabhu la-ibena pāśa’*

**Перевод:**

**“He thought, ‘If I am Gaura’s servant, then my Lord will mercifully bring me to His side.’**

### Text 38

*ei bali’ sārvabhauma yāya nīlācala*  
*māyāvāda śāstra tathā karila prabala*

**Перевод:**

**“Thinking this, Sārvabhauma went to Nīlācala and propagated texts on** ***māyāvāda*** **there.**

### Text 39

*hethā prabhu gaura-candra śrī-vidyā-vilāse*  
*sārvabhauma-śiṣya-gaṇe jine parihāse*

**Перевод:**

**“Here, during His Pastimes of scholarship, Gaura-candra Prabhu defeated and joked with the disciples of Sārvabhauma.**

### Text 40

*nyāya phāṅki kari’ prabhu sakale hārāya*  
*kabhu vidyā-nagarete āise gaura-rāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Sometimes the Lord came to Vidyā Nagara, posed trick questions, and defeated everyone.**

### Text 41

*adhyāpaka-gaṇa āra paḍuyāra gaṇa*  
*parājita haye sabe kare palāyana*

**Перевод:**

**“Defeated, all the teachers and students would flee.**

### Text 42

*gaurāṅgera vidyā-līlā apūrva kathana*  
*avidyā chāḍaye tāra ye kare śravaṇa”*

**Перевод:**

**“Ignorance leaves those who hear about Gaurāṅga’s extraordinary Pastimes of scholarship.”**

### Text 43

*śuni’ jīva premānande se veda-nagare*  
*vyāsa-pīṭhe gaḍāgaḍi yāya prema-bhare*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this with the joy of divine love, Jīva rolled on the ground at the school in Vidyā Nagara, filled with divine love.**

### Text 44

*nityānanda-śrī-caraṇe kare nivedana*  
*“āmāra saṁśaya chedana karaha ekhana*

**Перевод:**

**At the holy feet of Nityānanda, he submitted, “I have a doubt. Please cut it away.**

### Text 45

*sāṅkhya-vidyā tarka-vidyā amaṅgalamaya*  
*kemane nitya-dhāme se sakala raya”*

**Перевод:**

**“Knowledge of analysis and argument are inauspicious, so how do they reside in the eternal Dhāma?”**

### Text 46

*śuni’ prabhu nityānanda jīve deya kola*  
*ādara kariyā bale, “hari hari bola*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Nityānanda Prabhu embraced Jīva and affectionately said, “Hari! Haribol!**

### Text 47

*prabhura pavitra dhāme nāhi amaṅgala*  
*tarka sāṅkhya svataḥ nahe hethāya prabala*

**Перевод:**

**“Nothing is inauspicious in the holy abode of the Lord. Here, argument and analysis have no power of their own.**

### Text 48

*bhaktira adhīna saba bhakti-dāsya kare*  
*karma-doṣe duṣṭa jane viparyaya dhare*

**Перевод:**

**“Here, everything is dependent on devotion and serves devotion. As a result of their sins, the wicked think the opposite.**

### Text 49

*bhakti mahādevī hethā āra saba dāsa*  
*sakale karaya bhakti-devīra prakāśa*

**Перевод:**

**“Here, the goddess of devotion is supreme: everyone is her servant, and everything reveals her.**

### Text 50

*navadvīpe nava-vidhā bhakti adhiṣṭhāna*  
*bhaktire sevaya sadā karma āra jñāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Navadvīpa is the abode of the ninefold practice of devotion. Here, action and knowledge always serve devotion.**

### Text 51

*bahirmukha-jane śāstra deya duṣṭa-mati*  
*śiṣṭa-jane sei śāstra deya kṛṣṇa-rati*

**Перевод:**

**“The scriptures give misconceptions to those who are averse, and love for Kṛṣṇa to those who are gentle.**

### Text 52

*prauḍhā-māyā gaura-dāsī adhiṣṭhātrī devī*  
*sarva-yuge ei sthāne thāke gaura-sevī*

**Перевод:**

**“Prauḍhā Māyā, a servant of Gaurāṅga, is the presiding goddess here. In every age, she stays here and serves Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 53

*ati karma-doṣa yāra vaiṣṇavete dveṣa*  
*tāre māyā andha kari’ deya nānā kleśa*

**Перевод:**

**“Māyā blinds those who are very sinful and inimical to Vaiṣṇavas, and subjects them to various miseries.**

### Text 54

*sarva-pāpa sarva-karma hethā haya kṣaya*  
*prauḍhā-māyā vidyā-rūpe kare karma laya*

**Перевод:**

**“All sin and** ***karma*** **are annihilated here. In the form of knowledge, Prauḍhā Māyā destroys karma.**

### Text 55

*kintu yadi śrī-vaiṣṇave aparādha thāke*  
*tabe dūra kare tāre karmera vipāke*

**Перевод:**

**“If, however, someone offends a Vaiṣṇava, Prauḍhā Māyā drives them further into the miseries of** ***karma*****.**

### Text 56

*vidyā paḍi’ nadīyāya se saba durjana*  
*kabhu nāhi pāya kṛṣṇa-pade prema-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**“Such sinners may study in Nadīyā, but they never attain the wealth of divine love for the feet of Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 57

*vidyāra avidyā lābha kare sei saba*  
*nāhi dekhe śrī-gaurāṅga nadīyā-vaibhava*

**Перевод:**

**“They attain only ignorance of true knowledge and do not see the glory of Śrī Gaurāṅga in Nadīyā.**

### Text 58

*ataeva vidyā nahe amaṅgalamaya*  
*vidyāra avidyā chāyā amaṅgala haya*

**Перевод:**

**“Thus, knowledge is not inauspicious; its shadow, ignorance, is inauspicious.**

### Text 59

*e saba sphuribe jīva gaurāṅga-kṛpāya*  
*likhibe āpana śāstre prabhura icchāya*

**Перевод:**

**“O Jīva, you will reveal all this by the mercy of Gaurāṅga. By His desire, you will write scriptures about it.**

### Text 60

*tomāra dvārā karibena śāstra-parakāśa*  
*ebe cala yāi morā jahnura āvāsa”*

**Перевод:**

**“Actually, He will write scriptures through you. Now, let us go to the residence of Jahnu Muni.”**

### Text 61

*balite balite sabe jānnagara yāya*  
*jahnu-tapovana-śobhā dekhibāre pāya*

**Перевод:**

**While speaking, they went to Jān Nagara and saw the beauty of the hermitage of Jahnu Muni.**

### Text 62

*nityānanda bale, “ei jahnudvīpa nāma*  
*bhadravana nāme khyāta manohara dhāma*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu said, “This charming abode is known as Jahnudvīpa and Bhadravana.**

### Text 63

*ei sthāne jahnu-muni tapa ācarila*  
*suvarṇa pratimā gaura darśana karila*

**Перевод:**

**“Jahnu Muni performed austerities here and saw the golden form of Gaura.**

### Text 64

*hethā jahnu-muni baise sandhyā karibāre*  
*bhāgīrathī vege kośā-kuśī paḍe dhāre*

**Перевод:**

**“Once, when Jahnu Muni sat here to perform his evening meditation, His copper vessel fell into the swiftly flowing River Gaṅgā.**

### Text 65

*dhāre paḍi’ kośā-kuśī bhāsiyā chalila*  
*gaṇḍuṣe gaṅgāra jala saba pāna kaila*

**Перевод:**

**“The copper vessel fell into the river and floated away. Jahnu Muni then drank all the water of the Gaṅgā with his palm.**

### Text 66

*bhagīratha mane bhāve, ‘kothā gaṅgā gela’*  
*vihvala ha-iyā tabe bhāvite lāgila*

**Перевод:**

**“Mahārāja Bhagīratha thought, ‘Where did the Gaṅgā go?’ Bewildered, he began to think over the situation.**

### Text 67

*jahnu-muni pāna kaila saba gaṅgā-jala*  
*jāni’ bhagīratha mane ha-ila vikala*

**Перевод:**

**“Understanding that Jahnu Muni had drunk all the water of the Gaṅgā, Mahārāja Bhagīratha became concerned.**

### Text 68

*kata-dine munire pūjila mahādhīra*  
*aṅga vidāriyā gaṅgā karila bāhira*

**Перевод:**

**Sober Mahārāja Bhagīratha worshipped the sage for some time, and eventually the Gaṅgā broke out of the sage’s body.**

### Text 69

*sei haite jāhnavī ha-ila nāma tāṅra*  
*‘jāhnavī’ baliyā ḍāke sakala saṁsāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Thereafter, she became known as Jāhnavī. The whole world now calls her ‘Jāhnavī’.**

### Text 70

*kata-dina pare hethā gaṅgāra nandana*  
*bhīṣmadeva kaila mātāmaha daraśana*

**Перевод:**

**“Some time later, Gaṅgā’s son, Bhīṣmadeva, met his grandfather Jahnu Muni here.**

### Text 71

*bhīṣmere ādara kare jahnu-mahāśaya*  
*bahu-dina rākhe tāre āpana ālaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Jahnu Muni honoured Bhīṣma and kept Bhīṣma at his home for a long time.**

### Text 72

*jahnu-sthāne bhīṣma dharma śikhila apāra*  
*yudhiṣṭhire śikṣā dila sei dharma-sāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Bhīṣma learned profound religious principles from Jahnu Muni and later taught the essence of them to Yudhiṣṭhira Mahārāja.**

### Text 73

*navadvīpe thāki’ bhīṣma pāila bhakti-dhana*  
*vaiṣṇava-madhyete bhīṣma ha-ila gaṇana*

**Перевод:**

**“Residing in Navadvīpa, Bhīṣma attained the wealth of devotion and became recognised as a Vaiṣṇava.**

### Text 74

*ataeva jahnudvīpa parama pāvana*  
*hethā vāsa kare sadā bhāgyavāna jana”*

**Перевод:**

**“Thus, Jahnudvīpa is supremely purifying. Fortunate souls reside here eternally.”**

### Text 75

*sei dina jahnudvīpe nityānanda-rāya*  
*bhakta-gaṇa saha rahe bhaktera ālaya*

**Перевод:**

**That day Nityānanda and the devotees stayed at the home of a devotee in Jahnudvīpa.**

### Text 76

*para-dina prāte prabhu laye bhakta-gaṇa*  
*modadrumadvīpe tabe karila gamana*

**Перевод:**

**The next morning, Nityānanda Prabhu took the devotees and went to Modadrumadvīpa.**

### Text 77

*jāhnavā-nitāi-pada yāhāra garimā*  
*e bhaktivinoda gāya nadīyā-mahimā*

**Перевод:**

**Bhaktivinoda, whose pride is the feet of Jāhnavā and Nitāi, chants the glories of Nadīyā.**

## Chapter Fourteen – Śrī Modadrumadvīpa and Śrī Rāma-Līlā

### Text 1

*jaya jaya pañca-tattvātmaka gaurahari*  
*jaya jaya navadvīpa-dhāma sarvopari*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to the life of the Pañca Tattva, Śrī Gaurahari, and the best of all abodes, Śrī Navadvīpa!**

### Text 2

*māmagācchi-grāme giyā nityānanda-rāya*  
*bale, “ei modadruma, ayodhyā hethāya*

**Перевод:**

**Proceeding to Māmgācchi Grāma, Nityānanda Rāya said, “This is Modadrumadvīpa. Ayodhyā is present here.**

### Text 3-4

*pūrva-kalpe yabe rāma haila vana-vāsī*  
*lakṣaṇa jānakī laye ei sthāne āsi’*

*mahāvaṭa vṛkṣa tale kuṭīra bāṅdhiyā*  
*kata-dina vāsa kaila ānandita haiyā*

**Перевод:**

**“In a previous manifestation of the universe, while He was residing in the forest, Rāma came here with Lakṣmaṇa and Sītā, built a cottage under a massive banyan tree, and lived here joyfully for some time.**

### Text 5

*navadvīpa-prabhā rāma kari’ daraśana*  
*alpa alpa hāsya kare śrī-raghu-nandana*

**Перевод:**

**“Seeing the lustre of Navadvīpa, Rāma smiled slightly.**

### Text 6

*kibā durvā-dala-śyāma-rūpa manohara*  
*rājīva-locana haste dhanuka sundara*

**Перевод:**

**“His lotus eyes, His beautiful bow in hand—how enchanting was His dark grass-green form!**

### Text 7

*brahmacārī-veśa śire jaṭā śobhā kare*  
*darśane sakala prāṇī-gaṇa manohare*

**Перевод:**
