**“He wore the dress of a** ***brahmacārī*****, and dreadlocks shone atop His head. Seeing Him captivated all living beings.**

### Text 8

*hāsi’ hāsi’ mukha dekhi’ jānakī takhana*  
*jijñāse śrī-rāme devī hāsyera kāraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“Seeing His smiling face, Sītā Devī asked Śrī Rāma about the reason for His smile.**

### Text 9

*rāma bale, ‘śuna sītā janaka-nandini*  
*ati gopanīya eka āchhe ta kāhinī*

**Перевод:**

**“Rāma replied, ‘O Sītā, listen! There is a very confidential answer.**

### Text 10

*dhanya kali yabe haya ei nadīyāya*  
*pīta-varṇa-rūpa mora dekhibāre pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘When the glorious Kali-yuga takes place, souls will see My golden form here in Nadīyā.**

### Text 11

*jagannātha-miśra-gṛhe śrī-śacī-udare*  
*gaurāṅga-rūpete janma labhiba satvare*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I will soon take birth as Gaurāṅga from the womb of Śacī Devī in the home of Jagannātha Miśra.**

### Text 12

*bālya-līlā dekhibe ye saba bhāgyavāna*  
*kariba se sabe āmi parā prema dāna*

**Перевод:**

**“‘The fortunate souls will see My childhood Pastimes, and I will give them all the highest form of divine love.**

### Text 13

*kariba se kāle priye vidyāra vilāsa*  
*śrī-nāma-māhātmya āmi kariba prakāśa*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Dear Sītā, at that time, I will perform Pastimes of scholarship and reveal the glories of My Name.**

### Text 14

*sannyāsa kariyā āmi yāba nīlācale*  
*kāṅdibe jananī svīya vadhū laye kole’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Later, I will take** ***sannyāsa*** **and go to Nīlācala. My mother will embrace My wife and cry.’**

### Text 15

*ei kathā śuni’ sītā balena vacana*  
*‘jananī kāṅdābe kena rājīva-locana*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing this, Sītā said, ‘O lotus-eyed Lord, why will You make Your mother cry?**

### Text 16

*sannyāsa karibe kena chāḍiyā gṛhiṇī*  
*patnī duḥkha diyā sukha kibā nāhi jāni’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Why will You take** ***sannyāsa*** **and leave Your wife? What happiness is there in making Your wife unhappy? I do not understand.’**

### Text 17

*śrī-rāma balena, ‘priye tumi saba jāna*  
*jīvere śikhāte ebe ha-ila ajñāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Śrī Rāma answered, ‘My dear, You actually understand everything, but to teach the souls, You now act as if You were unaware.**

### Text 18

*āmāte ye prema-bhakti tāra āsvādana*  
*dui mate haya sītā śunaha vacana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Loving devotion to Me is relished in two ways. O Sītā, please hear My words.**

### Text 19

*āmāra saṁyoge sukha sambhoga bolaya*  
*āmāra viyoge sukha vipralambha haya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘The joy felt during union with Me is called** ***sambhoga*****. The joy felt during separation from Me is called** ***vipralambha*****.**

### Text 20

*bhakta mora nitya-saṅgī sambhoga vāñchaya*  
*mama kṛpā-vaśe tāra vipralambha haya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘My devotees, My eternal associates, desire** ***sambhoga*****, and by My grace, they experience** ***vipralambha*****.**

### Text 21

*vipralambhe duḥkha yei āmāra kāraṇa*  
*parama ānanda tāhā jāne bhakta-jana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Devotees know that the sadness felt for My sake during** ***vipralambha*** **is actually the greatest joy.**

### Text 22

*vipralambha śeṣe yabe sambhoga udaya*  
*pūrvāpekṣā koṭi-guṇa sukha tāhe haya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘When** ***sambhoga*** **occurs after** ***vipralambha*****, the happiness felt is ten million times greater than before.**

### Text 23

*sei ta sukhera hetu āmāra viccheda*  
*svīkāra karaha tumi bale cāri veda*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Separation from Me is the cause of that happiness. Accept this as truth; the four** ***Vedas*** **affirm it.**

### Text 24

*śrī-gaurāṅga-avatāre kauśalyā jananī*  
*śacī-devī aditi vedete yāra dhvani*

**Перевод:**

**“‘During My Pastimes as Śrī Gaurāṅga, Mother Kauśalyā, who is known in the** ***Vedas*** **as Aditi, will appear as Śacī Devī.**

### Text 25

*tumi viṣṇu-priyā-rūpe sevibe āmāre*  
*vicchede śrī-gaura-mūrti karibe pracāre*

**Перевод:**

**“‘You will serve Me as Viṣṇu Priyā. In separation from Me, You will make a Deity of My golden form.**

### Text 26

*tomāra vicchede kabhu svarṇa-sītā kari’*  
*bhajiba tomāre āmi ayodhyā nagarī*

**Перевод:**

**“‘In separation from You, I will make a golden Sītā and worship You in Ayodhyā.**

### Text 27

*tāra vinimaye tumi nadīyā-nagare*  
*gaurāṅga-pratimā kari’ pūjibe āmāre*

**Перевод:**

**“‘In exchange, You will make a Deity of My golden form and worship Me in Nadīyā.**

### Text 28

*ei gūḍha kathā sītā gopanīya ati*  
*lokete prakāśa nāhi ha-ibe samprati*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Sītā, these profound topics are very confidential. They will not be revealed to the public now.**

### Text 29

*ei navadvīpa mora baḍa priya sthāna*  
*ayodhyādi nāhi haya ihāra samāna*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Navadvīpa is very dear to Me: neither Ayodhyā nor any other place is equal to it.**

### Text 30

*ei rāma-vaṭa vṛkṣa kali āgamane*  
*adarśana haye sītā rabe saṅgopane’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Sītā, when Kali-yuga arrives, this banyan tree, Rāma Vaṭa, will disappear but invisibly remain.’**

### Text 31

*ei rūpe rāma sītā lakṣaṇa sahita*  
*ei sthāne kata-dina haye avasthita*

**Перевод:**

**“In this way Rāma, Sītā, and Lakṣmaṇa resided here for some time.**

### Text 32

*daṇḍaka araṇye gelā kārya sādhibāre*  
*rāmera kuṭīra sthāna pāo dekhibāre*

**Перевод:**

**“Eventually, They went to the Daṇḍaka forest to perform Their Pastimes. See here the place of Rāma’s cottage.**

### Text 33

*rāma-mitra guhaka prabhura icchā-vaśe*  
*ei sthāne janmilena viprera aurase*

**Перевод:**

**“By the will of the Lord, Rāma’s friend Guhaka took birth here as the son of a** ***brāhmaṇa*****.**

### Text 34

*sadānanda vipra bhaṭṭācārya nāma tāṅra*  
*rāma vinā trijagate nāhi jāne āra*

**Перевод:**

**“His name became Sadānanda Vipra Bhaṭṭācārya. He knew nothing other than Rāma within the three worlds.**

### Text 35

*yei dina prabhu mora janme māyāpure*  
*sei dina sadānanda chila miśra ghare*

**Перевод:**

**“On the day that our Lord took birth in Māyāpura, Sadānanda was present in the home of Jagannātha Miśra.**

### Text 36

*prabhura janama-kāle yata deva-gaṇa*  
*miśrera bhavane śiśu kare daraśana*

**Перевод:**

**“At the time of the Lord’s birth, all the gods observed the child in the home of Jagannātha Miśra.**

### Text 37

*parama sādhaka vipra cine deva-gaṇe*  
*jānila āmāra prabhu janmila ekhāne*

**Перевод:**

**“The** ***brāhmaṇa*****, a great devotee, recognised the gods and understood that our Lord had taken birth there.**

### Text 38

*parama kautuke vipra āila nija ghare*  
*iṣṭa-dhyāne dekhe vipra gaurāṅgasundare*

**Перевод:**

**“With great delight, the** ***brāhmaṇa*** **returned to his home. In meditation on his worshippable Lord, he saw Gaurāṅga-sundara.**

### Text 39

*siṁhāsane basiyāchhe śrī-gaurāṅga-rāya*  
*brahmā ādi deva-gaṇe cāmara ḍhulāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Śrī Gaurāṅga Rāya was sitting on a throne, and Brahmā and the other gods were waving** ***cāmaras*** **beside Him.**

### Text 40

*punaḥ dekhe rāmacandra durvādala-śyāma*  
*nikaṭe lakṣmaṇa-vīra śrī-ananta-dhāma*

**Перевод:**

**“Then, the** ***brāhmaṇa*** **saw the dark, grass-green Rāmacandra and heroic Lakṣmaṇa, the abode of Ananta, nearby.**

### Text 41

*bāme sītā sammukhe bhakata hanumāna*  
*dekhiyā viprera haila prabhu-tattva-jñāna*

**Перевод:**

**“On His left was Sītā, and His devotee Hanumān sat in front of Him. Seeing this, the** ***brāhmaṇa*** **understood the identity of the Lord.**

### Text 42

*parama ānande vipra māyāpure giyā*  
*ānande gaurāṅga dekhe nayana bhariyā*

**Перевод:**

**“With great joy, the** ***brāhmaṇa*** **went to Māyāpura and joyfully saw Gaurāṅga to the satisfaction of his eyes.**

### Text 43

*‘dhanya āmi dhanya āmi’ bale bāra-bāra*  
*‘gaura-rūpe rāmacandra sammukhe āmāra’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I am blessed! I am blessed!’ said the** ***brāhmaṇa*** **again and again. ‘Rāmacandra has appeared before me as Gaurāṅga!’**

### Text 44

*kata-dine saṅkīrtana ārambha ha-ila*  
*sadānanda ‘gaura’ bali’ tāhāte nācila*

**Перевод:**

**“Later, when the** ***saṅkīrtana*** **Pastimes of the Lord started, Sadānanda would dance and chant the Name of Gaura.**

### Text 45

*ohe jīva ei sthāne śrī-bhāṇḍīravana*  
*nirmala bhakata-gaṇa kare daraśana”*

**Перевод:**

**“O Jīva, here pure devotees see Śrī Bhāṇḍīravana.”**

### Text 46

*sei saba kathā śuni’ nitya-dhāme heri’*  
*nācena bhakata-gaṇa nityānande gheri’*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this and seeing the eternal abode, the devotees surrounded Nityānanda and danced.**

### Text 47

*śrī-jīvera aṅge haya sāttvika vikāra*  
*“hā gaurāṅga” bali’ jīva karena citkāra*

**Перевод:**

**Divine ecstasy manifested in the body of Śrī Jīva, and he exclaimed, “O Gaurāṅga!”**

### Text 48

*sei grāme sei dina nārāyaṇī-ghare*  
*rahilena nityānanda praphulla antare*

**Перевод:**

**With a jubilant heart, Nityānanda stayed in that village in the home of Nārāyaṇī that day.**

### Text 49

*parama pavitra satī vyāsera jananī*  
*śrī-vaiṣṇava-gaṇe sevā karila āpani*

**Перевод:**

**The perfectly pure and chaste Nārāyaṇī, the mother of Vyāsa, personally served the devotees.**

### Text 50

*para-dina prāte sabe cali’ kata dūra*  
*praveśila anāyāse śrī-vaikuṇṭha-pura*

**Перевод:**

**The next day in the morning, the devotees walked some distance and peacefully entered Śrī Vaikuṇṭhapura.**

### Text 51

*nitāi-jāhnavā-ājñā karite pālana*  
*nadīyā-māhātmya gāya dīna akiñcana*

**Перевод:**

**To fulfil the order of Nitāi and Jāhnavā, this poor, lowly soul chants the glories of Nadīyā.**

## Chapter Fifteen – Śrī Vaikuṇṭhapura, Śrī Rudradvīpa, and Śrī Pulina

### Text 1

*pañca-tattva sahita gaurāṅga jaya jaya*  
*jaya jaya navadvīpa gaurāṅga-ālaya*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to Śrī Gaurāṅga, the Pañca Tattva, and the abode of Śrī Gaurāṅga, Śrī Navadvīpa!**

### Text 2

*śrī-vaikuṇṭha-pure āsi’ prabhu nityānanda*  
*śrī-jīve kahena tabe hāsi’ manda manda*

**Перевод:**

**Arriving in Śrī Vaikuṇṭhapura, Nityānanda Prabhu smiled gently and spoke to Śrī Jīva.**

### Text 3

*“navadvīpa aṣṭa-dala eka-pārśve haya*  
*ei ta vaikuṇṭha-purī śunaha niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Please listen carefully. Here, on one side of the eight-petalled lotus of Navadvīpa, is Vaikuṇṭhapura.**

### Text 4

*paravyoma śrī-vaikuṇṭha nārāyaṇa-sthāna*  
*virajāra pāre sthiti ei ta sandhāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Śrī Vaikuṇṭha, the spiritual world, the abode of Nārāyaṇa, is situated beyond the Virajā River. This is its position.**

### Text 5

*māyāra nāhika tathā gati kadācana*  
*śrī-bhū-līlā-śakti-sevya tathā nārāyaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“Māyā never enters there, where Nārāyaṇa is served by the** ***śrī*****,** ***bhū*****, and** ***līlā*** **energies.**

### Text 6

*cinmaya bhūmira brahma haya ta kiraṇa*  
*carma-cakṣe jaḍa-dṛṣṭi kare sarva-jana*

**Перевод:**

**“The effulgence of this spiritual land is Brahma. With fleshy eyes, everyone sees it to be material.**

### Text 7

*ei nārāyaṇa-dhāme nitya nirañjane*  
*nārada dekhila kabhu cinmaya locane*

**Перевод:**

**“Nārada once saw this pure, eternal abode of Nārāyaṇa with his spiritual eyes.**

### Text 8

*nārāyaṇe dekhe punaḥ gaurāṅga-sundara*  
*dekhi’ hethā kata-dine rahe muni-vara*

**Перевод:**

**“He saw Nārāyaṇa, and then saw Him as Gaurāṅga-sundara. Thereafter, Nārada, the best of the sages, stayed here for some time.**

### Text 9

*āra eka kathā gūḍha āchhe purātana*  
*jagannātha-kṣetre āilā ācārya lakṣmaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“There is another ancient, confidential subject. Once, Rāmānuja Ācārya came to Jagannātha Kṣetra.**

### Text 10

*bahu stave tuṣṭa kaila deva jagannāthe*  
*kṛpā kari’ jagannātha āila sākṣāte*

**Перевод:**

**“He pleased Lord Jagannātha with numerous prayers, and Jagannātha mercifully came before him.**

### Text 11

*sākṣāte āsiyā prabhu balila vacana*  
*‘navadvīpa-dhāma tumi karaha darśana*

**Перевод:**

**“Coming before him, the Lord said, ‘Visit Navadvīpa Dhāma.**

### Text 12

*ati alpa-dine āmi nadīyā-nagare*  
*prakaṭa ha-iba jagannātha-miśra-ghare*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Very soon, I will appear in Nadīyā in the home of Jagannātha Miśra.**

### Text 13

*navadvīpa haya mora ati priya-sthāna*  
*paravyoma tāra eka-deśe adhiṣṭhāna*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Navadvīpa is My dearmost abode. The spiritual world of Vaikuṇṭha is situated within one portion of it.**

### Text 14

*tumi mora nitya-dāsa bhakata pradhāna*  
*avaśya dekhibe tumi navadvīpa-sthāna*

**Перевод:**

**“‘You are My eternal servant, and foremost amongst My devotees. You must see Navadvīpa Dhāma.**

### Text 15

*tava śiṣya-gaṇa dāsya-rasete magana*  
*hethāya thākuka tumi karaha gamana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Let your disciples remain here immersed in** ***dāsya-rasa*****. You must go.**

### Text 16

*navadvīpa nā dekhe ye pāiyā śarīra*  
*mithyā tāra janma ohe rāmānuja dhīra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O wise Rāmānuja! The birth of those who obtain a body but do not see Navadvīpa is meaningless.**

### Text 17

*raṅga-sthāna śrī-veṅkaṭa yādava acala*  
*navadvīpa-kalā mātra haya se sakala*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Śrī Raṅgam, Śrī Veṅkaṭa, and Yādavācala are simply minor parts of Navadvīpa.**

### Text 18

*ataeva navadvīpa kariyā gamana*  
*dekhe gaurāṅgera rūpa keśava-nandana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Therefore, O Rāmānuja, go to Navadvīpa and see the form of Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 19

*bhakti pracārite tumi āile dharā-tale*  
*sārthaka ha-uka janma gaura-kṛpā-bale*

**Перевод:**

**“‘You came to the earth to preach devotion. May your birth be successful by the mercy of Gaura.**

### Text 20

*navadvīpa dekhi’ tumi yāo kūrma-sthāna*  
*śiṣya-gaṇa sane tathā ha-ibe milana’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘See Navadvīpa and then go to Kūrma Sthāna. You will meet your disciples there.’**

### Text 21

*eta śuni’ lakṣmaṇācārya yuḍi’ dui kara*  
*jagannāthe nivedana kare ataḥpara*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing this, Rāmānuja Ācārya joined his palms and prayed to Jagannātha.**

### Text 22

*‘tomāra kṛpāya prabhu gaura-kathā śuni’*  
*kona tattva gaura-candra tāhā nāhi jāni’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Lord, by Your mercy I have now heard about Gaurāṅga, but I do not know who He is.’**

### Text 23

*rāmānuje kṛpā kari’ jagabandhu bale*  
*‘golokera nātha kṛṣṇa jānena sakale*

**Перевод:**

**“Jagannātha mercifully replied to Rāmānuja, ‘Everyone knows that Kṛṣṇa is the Lord of Goloka.**

### Text 24

*yāṅhāra vilāsa-mūrti prabhu nārāyaṇa*  
*sei kṛṣṇa para-tattva dhāma vṛndāvana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Lord Nārāyaṇa is a form Kṛṣṇa assumes to perform Pastimes. Kṛṣṇa is the supreme being, and His abode is Vṛndāvana.**

### Text 25

*sei kṛṣṇa pūrṇa rūpe nitya gaurahari*  
*sei vṛndāvana-dhāma navadvīpa-purī*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Kṛṣṇa also exists fully and eternally as Gaurahari, and Vṛndāvana also exists fully and eternally as Navadvīpa.**

### Text 26

*navadvīpe āmi nitya gaurāṅga-sundara*  
*navadvīpa śreṣṭha-dhāma jagata bhitara*

**Перевод:**

**“‘In Navadvīpa, I am eternally present as Gaurāṅga-sundara. Navadvīpa is the best abode in the world.**

### Text 27

*āmāra kṛpāya dhāma āchhe bhū-maṇḍale*  
*māyā-gandha nāhi tathā sarva-śāstre bale*

**Перевод:**

**“‘By My grace, Navadvīpa Dhāma is present on the earth, and all the scriptures say that there is no trace of** ***māyā*** **there.**

### Text 28

*bhū-maṇḍale āchhe bali’ yadi bhāva hīna*  
*tabe tava bhakti kṣaya habe dina dina*

**Перевод:**

**“‘If you think that Navadvīpa is degraded because it is present on the earth, then your devotion will diminish day by day.**

### Text 29

*āmāra acintya-śakti se cinmaya-dhāme*  
*āmāra icchāya rākhiyāchhe māyāśrame*

**Перевод:**

**“‘By My will, My inconceivable energy has manifested this spiritual abode within the realm of Māyā.**

### Text 30

*yuktira atīta tattva śāstra nāhi pāya*  
*kevala jānena bhakta āmāra kṛpāya’*

**Перевод:**

**“The scriptures do not ascertain truth that is beyond reason. Only devotees understand it, by My grace.’**

### Text 31

*jagannātha-vākya śuni’ rāmānuja dhīra*  
*śrī-gaurāṅga-preme tabe ha-ila asthira*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing the words of Jagannātha, the sober Rāmānuja became overwhelmed with divine love for Śrī Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 32

*bale, ‘prabhu baḍa-i āścarya līlā tava*  
*veda-śāstra nāhi jāne tomāra vaibhava*

**Перевод:**

**“He said, ‘O Lord, Your Pastimes are extremely astonishing. The Vedic scriptures do not know Your glories.**

### Text 33

*śāstrete viśeṣa-rūpe śrī-gaurāṅga-līlā*  
*kena prabhu jagannātha vyakta nā karilā*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Lord Jagannātha, why did the scriptures not specifically describe the Pastimes of Śrī Gaurāṅga?**

### Text 34

*gāḍha-rūpe śruti-purāṇādi dekhi yabe*  
*kabhu gaura-tattva sphurti citte pāi tabe*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Only when I look at the** ***Vedas, Purāṇas*****, and other scriptures in a deeper way do I find revelation of Gaurāṅga.**

### Text 35

*tava ājñā prāpta haye chāḍila saṁśaya*  
*gaura-līlā-rasa hṛde ha-ila udaya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Receiving Your order, I have left behind my doubts. The ecstasy of the Pastimes of Gaura has arisen in my heart.**

### Text 36

*ājñā haya navadvīpa kariyā gamana*  
*pracāriba gaura-līlā e tina bhuvana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘If You order me, I will go to Navadvīpa and then preach about the Pastimes of Gaura throughout the three worlds.**

### Text 37

*gūḍha-śāstra vyakta kari’ jānāba sabāre*  
*gaura-bhakta kari’ bala e tina saṁsāre’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I will reveal hidden scriptures, enlighten everyone, and make every soul within the three worlds a devotee of Gaura! Please speak.’**

### Text 38

*rāmānuja-āgraha dekhiyā jagannātha*  
*bale, ‘rāmānuja nāhi bala aichhe bāta*

**Перевод:**

**“Seeing the eagerness of Rāmānuja, Jagannātha said, ‘O Rāmānuja, do not speak this way.**

### Text 39

*gaura-līlā ati gūḍha rākhibe gopane*  
*se līlāra aprakaṭe pābe sarva-jane*

**Перевод:**

**“‘My Pastimes as Gaura are very confidential; keep them secret. Only after they are unmanifest will everyone receive them.**

### Text 40

*tumi dāsya-rasa mora karaha pracāra*  
*nije nije citte gaura bhaja anibāra’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Preach** ***dāsya-rasa*****, and always worship Gaura within your heart.’**

### Text 41

*saṅketa pāiyā rāmānuja mahāśaya*  
*gopane śrī-navadvīpe ha-ila udaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Receiving this instruction, Rāmānuja Ācārya secretly came to Navadvīpa.**

### Text 42-43

*pāche vyakta haya gaura-līlā asamaye*  
*se kāraṇe rāmānuje viśvaksena laye*

*paravyoma śrī-vaikuṇṭha-purīte rākhaya*  
*ei sthāna dekhi’ rāmānuja mugdha haya*

**Перевод:**

**“So that the Pastimes of Gaura were not manifest prematurely, Nārāyaṇa took Rāmānuja and kept him in Śrī Vaikuṇṭhapura. Seeing this place, Rāmānuja was charmed.**

### Text 44

*śrī-bhū-līlā-niṣevita paravyoma-pati*  
*dekhā dila rāmānuje kṛpā kari’ ati*

**Перевод:**

**“Nārāyaṇa, who is served by His** ***śrī, bhū*****, and** ***līlā*** **energies, then very mercifully revealed Himself to Rāmānuja.**

### Text 45

*rāmānuja nija iṣṭa-devera darśane*  
*āpanāre dhanya māni’ gaṇe mane mane*

**Перевод:**

**“Seeing His worshippable Lord, Rāmānuja felt himself to be most fortunate.**

### Text 46

*kṣaṇeke lakṣmaṇa dekhe puraṭa-sundara*  
*jagannātha-miśra-suta-rūpa manohara*

**Перевод:**

**“Rāmānuja then suddenly saw the beautiful, enchanting, golden form of the son of Jagannātha Miśra.**

### Text 47

*rūpera chaṭāya rāmānuja mūrcchā yāya*  
*śrī-gaura dharila pada tāṅhāra māthāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Rāmānuja fainted in the lustre of the Lord’s form, and Śrī Gaura put His feet on Rāmānuja’s head.**

### Text 48

*divya-jñāne rāmānuja karila stavana*  
*‘nadīyā prakaṭa-līlā pāba daraśana’*

**Перевод:**

**“With divine knowledge, Rāmānuja then prayed, ‘May I behold Your manifest Pastimes in Nadīyā.’**

### Text 49

*ei bali’ preme kāṅde rāmānuja-svāmī*  
*bale, ‘navadvīpa chāḍi’ nāhi yāba āmi’*

**Перевод:**

**“Saying this, Rāmānuja cried with divine love. Then he said, ‘I will not leave Navadvīpa.’**

### Text 50

*kṛpā kari’ gaurahari balila vacana*  
*‘pūrṇa habe icchā tava keśava-nandana*

**Перевод:**

**“Mercifully, Gaurahari said, ‘O Rāmānuja, your desires will be fulfilled.**

### Text 51

*ye kāle nadīyā-līlā prakaṭa ha-ibe*  
*takhana dvitīya janma navadvīpe pābe’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘When My Pastimes in Nadīyā manifest, you will be born again in Navadvīpa.’**

### Text 52

*ei bali’ gaurahari haila antardhāna*  
*svastha haye rāmānuja karila prayāṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“Saying this, Gaurahari disappeared. Satisfied, Rāmānuja departed.**

### Text 53

*kata-dine kūrma-sthāne haila upasthita*  
*tathā dekhā haila śiṣya-gaṇera sahita*

**Перевод:**

**“Eventually, Rāmānuja arrived in Kūrma Sthāna and met his disciples there.**

### Text 54

*dākṣiṇātye giyā dāsya-rasa vyakta kare*  
*navadvīpa śrī-gaurāṅga bhāviyā antare*

**Перевод:**

**“He proceeded south and preached about** ***dāsya*****–*****rasa*** **while thinking of Navadvīpa and Śrī Gaurāṅga within His heart.**

### Text 55

*gaurāṅgera kṛpā-vaśe ei nitya-dhāme*  
*janamila rāmānuja śrī-ananta nāme”*

**Перевод:**

**“By the mercy of Gaurāṅga, Rāmānuja took birth in this eternal abode as Śrī Ananta Ācārya.”**

### Text 56

*vallabha-ācārya-gṛhe kariyā gamana*  
*lakṣmī-gaurāṅgera vibhā kare daraśana*

**Перевод:**

**The party then went to the home of Vallabha Ācārya and saw the site of Lakṣmī and Gaurāṅga’s marriage.**

### Text 57

*“anantera gṛha-sthāna dekha bhakta-gaṇa*  
*hethā nārāyaṇa-bhakta chila bahu-jana*

**Перевод:**

**“O devotees, see the site of Ananta Ācārya’s home. Here, there were many devotees of Nārāyaṇa.**

### Text 58

*tāt-kālika rāja-gaṇa ei pīṭha-sthāne*  
*nārāyaṇa-sevā prakāśila sabe jāne”*

**Перевод:**

**“Everyone knows that the king of that time established worship of Nārāyaṇa in this place.”**

### Text 59

*niḥśreyasa vana ei virajāra pāra*  
*bhakta-gaṇa dekhi’ pāya ānanda apāra*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the Niḥśreyasa forest on the bank of the Virajā River, the devotees felt unlimited joy.**

### Text 60

*ei rūpa pūrva-kathā balite balite*  
*sabe upanīta mahatpura sannihite*

**Перевод:**

**While discussing this history, the devotees arrived in Mahatpura.**

### Text 61

*prabhu bale, “ei sthāne āchhe kāmyavana*  
*parama bhakati saha kara daraśana*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu said, “Kāmyavaan is present here. Visit it with great devotion.**

### Text 62

*pañca-vaṭa ei sthāne chila pūrva kāle*  
*prabhura icchāya ebe gela antarāle*

**Перевод:**

**“Previously, there were five banyan trees here, but by the will of the Lord, they have since disappeared.**

### Text 63

*ebe ei sthāna mātāpura nāma kaya*  
*pūrva nāma śāstra-siddha mahatpura haya*

**Перевод:**

**“This place is now called Mātāpura. The scriptures state that its previous name was Mahatpura.**

### Text 64

*draupadīra saha pāṇḍu-putra pañca-jana*  
*ajñāta-vāsete gauḍe kaila āgamana*

**Перевод:**

**“Once, the five Pāṇḍavas came to Gauḍa with Draupadī to live incognito.**

### Text 65

*ekacakrā-grāme svapne rājā yudhiṣṭhira*  
*nadīyā-māhātmya jāni’ ha-ila asthira*

**Перевод:**

**“Understanding the glories of Nadīyā through a dream in Ekacakrā, Mahārāja Yudhiṣṭhira became amazed.**

### Text 66

*para-dina navadvīpa darśanera āśe*  
*ei sthāne āila sabe parama ullāse*

**Перевод:**

**“Desiring to see Navadvīpa, the Pāṇḍavas came here the next day with great joy.**

### Text 67

*navadvīpa-śobhā heri’ pāṇḍu-putra-gaṇa*  
*gauḍa-vāsi-gaṇa-bhāgya kare praśaṁsana*

**Перевод:**

**“Seeing the beauty of Navadvīpa, the Pāṇḍavas praised the fortune of the residents of Gauḍa.**

### Text 68

*kata-dina karilena ei sthāne vāsa*  
*asura-rākṣasa-gaṇe karila vināśa*

**Перевод:**

**“They stayed here for some time and killed various demons.**

### Text 69

*yudhiṣṭhira-ṭilā ei dekha sarva-jana*  
*draupadīra kuṇḍa hethā kare daraśana*

**Перевод:**

**“Everyone, see there Yudhiṣṭhira Ṭilā. See here the pond of Draupadī.**

### Text 70

*sthānera māhātmya jāni’ rājā yudhiṣṭhira*  
*ei sthāne kata-dina ha-ilena sthira*

**Перевод:**

**“Understanding the glories of this place, Mahārāja Yudhiṣṭhira stayed here for some time.**

### Text 71

*eka-dina svapne dekhe gaurāṅgera rūpa*  
*sarva-dika ālo kare ati aparūpa*

**Перевод:**

**“One day, in a dream, he saw the extraordinary form of Gaurāṅga illuminate all directions.**

### Text 72

*hāsite hāsite gaura balila vacana*  
*‘ati gopya rūpa ei kara daraśana*

**Перевод:**

**“Smiling, Gaurāṅga said, ‘Behold this very secret form.**

### Text 73

*āmi kṛṣṇa nanda-suta tomāra ālaye*  
*mitra-bhāve thāki sadā nija-jana haye*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I am Kṛṣṇa, the son of Nanda, and I always stay in your house as your friendly companion.**

### Text 74

*ei navadvīpa-dhāma sarva-dhāma-sāra*  
*kalite prakaṭa haye nāśe andhakāra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Navadvīpa Dhāma is the best of all abodes. It will manifest during Kali-yuga and dispel all darkness.**

### Text 75

*tumi sabe āchha cira-kāla dāsa mama*  
*āmāra prakaṭa-kāle pāibe janama*

**Перевод:**

**“‘You are all My eternal servants, and when I appear, you will also take birth.**

### Text 76

*utkala deśete sindhu-tīre tomā saha*  
*ekatre puruṣottame raba ahaḥ-rahaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I will live with you permanently in Puruṣottama Kṣetra on the bank of the ocean in Odisha.**

### Text 77

*ei sthāna haite ebe yāha oḍra-deśa*  
*se deśa pavitra kari’ nāśa jīva-kleśa’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘From here, go to Odisha, purify the country, and remove the sorrow of the people.’**

### Text 78

*svapna dekhi’ yudhiṣṭhira bhrātṛ-gaṇe bale*  
*yukti kari’ chaya-jane oḍra-deśe cale*

**Перевод:**

**“After having this dream, Yudhiṣṭhira spoke to his brothers. They consulted with Draupadī and then went to Odisha.**

### Text 79

*navadvīpa chāḍite haila baḍa kleśa*  
*tathāpi pālana kare prabhura ādeśa*

**Перевод:**

**“They felt great sorrow to leave Navadvīpa. Still, they followed the order of the Lord.**

### Text 80

*ei sthāne madhva-muni śiṣya-gaṇa laye*  
*rahilena kata-dina dhāma-vāsī haye*

**Перевод:**

**“Madhva Muni also stayed here with his disciples for some time as a resident of the Dhāma.**

### Text 81

*madhvere kariyā kṛpā gaurāṅga-sundara*  
*svapne dekhāila rūpa ati manohara*

**Перевод:**

**“Gaurāṅga-sundara mercifully revealed His most enchanting form to Madhva Ācārya in a dream.**

### Text 82

*hāsi’ hāsi’ gaura-candra madhvācārye bale*  
*‘tumi nitya-dāsa mama jāne ta sakale*

**Перевод:**

**“Smiling, Gaura-candra said to Madhvācārya, ‘Everyone knows that you are My eternal servant.**

### Text 83

*navadvīpe yabe āmi prakaṭa ha-iba*  
*tava sampradāya āmi svīkāra kariba*

**Перевод:**

**“‘When I appear in Navadvīpa, I will follow your lineage.**

### Text 84-85

*ebe sarva-deśe tumi kariyā yatana*  
*māyāvāda asac-chāstra kara utpāṭana*

*śrī-mūrti-māhātmya tumi kara parakāśa*  
*tava śuddha mata āmi kariba vikāśa’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Now, carefully eradicate illusionism and false scriptures from all regions, and establish the glory of the Lord’s Deity form. Later, I will develop your pure conception.’**

### Text 86

*eta bali’ gaura-candra haila antardhāna*  
*nidrā bhāṅgi’ madhva-muni ha-ila ajñāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Saying this, Gaura-candra disappeared. Madhva Muni awoke and then fainted.**

### Text 87

*‘āra ki dekhiba rūpa puraṭa-sundara’*  
*baliyā krandana kare madhva ataḥpara*

**Перевод:**

**“Madhvācārya then cried, wondering, ‘Will I ever see that beautiful golden form again?’**

### Text 88

*daiva-vāṇī haila tabe nirmala ākāśe*  
*‘āmāre gopane bhaji’ āisa mama pāśe’*

**Перевод:**

**“Then a divine voice came from the clear sky, ‘Serve Me secretly, and you will come to Me.’**

### Text 89

*susthira ha-iyā madhvācārya mahāśaya*  
*māyāvādī digvijaye karila vijaya”*

**Перевод:**

**“Madhvācārya became resolute and defeated the** ***māyāvādīs*** **in all directions.”**

### Text 90

*ei saba pūrva-kathā balite balite*  
*rudradvīpe upanīta dekhite dekhite*

**Перевод:**

**As Nityānanda Prabhu spoke about all this history, the devotees suddenly arrived in Rudradvīpa.**

### Text 91

*prabhu nityānanda bale, “ei rudra-khaṇḍa*  
*bhāgīrathī prabhāve ha-ila dui khaṇḍa*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu said, “This is Rudradvīpa. It has been divided into two parts by the Gaṅgā.**

### Text 92

*loka-vāsa nāhi hethā prabhura icchāya*  
*paśchimera dvīpa dekha pūrva-pāre yāya*

**Перевод:**

**“By the will of the Lord, people do not live here. See that this western island has moved to the east bank of the river.**

### Text 93

*hethā haite dekha ai śrī-śaṅkarapura*  
*śobhā pāya gaṅgā-tīre dekha kata dūra*

**Перевод:**

**“From here, see Śrī Śaṅkarpura. See how far it shines along the bank of the Gaṅgā.**

### Text 94

*śaṅkara ācārya yabe kare digvijaya*  
*navadvīpa jaye tathā upasthita haya*

**Перевод:**

**“When Śaṅkarācārya was conquering all directions, he came there to conquer Navadvīpa.**

### Text 95

*manete vaiṣṇava-rāja ācārya śaṅkara*  
*bāhire advaitavādī māyāra kiṅkara*

**Перевод:**

**“At heart, Śaṅkarācārya was a great Vaiṣṇava; only externally was he acting as a nondualist and servant of Māyā.**

### Text 96

*nije rudra-aṁśa sadā pratāpe pracura*  
*pracchanna bauddhera mata pracārete śūra*

**Перевод:**

**“He was a highly empowered manifestation of Śiva who was expert at preaching a conception of veiled Buddhism.**

### Text 97-98

*prabhura ājñāya rudra ei kārya kare*  
*āilena yabe te̐ha nadīyā-nagare*

*svapne prabhu gaura-candra dilā daraśana*  
*kṛpā kari’ bale tāre madhura vacana*

**Перевод:**

**“According to the order of the Lord, Śiva performs this duty. When Śaṅkarācārya came to Nadīyā, Gaura-candra revealed Himself to Śaṅkarācārya in a dream and mercifully spoke sweet words to him.**

### Text 99

*‘tumi ta āmāra dāsa mama ājñā dhari’*  
*pracāricha māyāvāda bahu yatna kari’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘You are My servant, and following My order, you are very cleverly preaching illusionism.**

### Text 100

*ei navadvīpa-dhāma mama priya ati*  
*hethā māyāvāda kabhu nā pāibe gati*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Navadvīpa Dhāma is very dear to Me, and illusionism will never have a place here.**

### Text 101

*vṛddha-śiva hethā prauḍhā-māyāre la-iyā*  
*kalpita āgama-gaṇe dena pracāriyā*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Here, Vṛddha Śiva and Prauḍhā Māyā propagate imaginary interpretations of the scriptures.**

### Text 102

*mama bhakta-gaṇe dveṣa kare yei jana*  
*tāhāre kevala te̐ha karena vañcana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘They deceive, however, only those who oppose My devotees.**

### Text 103

*ei sthāne sādhāraṇe mama bhakta haya*  
*duṣṭa-mata pracārera sthāna ihā naya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘In general, My devotees reside here. This place is not for preaching misconception.**

### Text 104

*ataeva tumi kara anyatra gamana*  
*navadvīpa-vāsi-gaṇe nā kara pīḍana’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Therefore, you should go elsewhere. Do not trouble the residents of Navadvīpa.’**

### Text 105

*svapne navadvīpa-tattva jāniyā takhana*  
*bhaktyāveśe anya deśe karila gamana*

**Перевод:**

**“Understanding the position of Navadvīpa through this dream, Śaṅkarācārya went elsewhere, inspired with devotion.**

### Text 106

*ei rudradvīpe haya rudra-gaṇa-sthāna*  
*hethā rudra-gaṇa gaura-guṇa kare gāna*

**Перевод:**

**“The eleven Rudras reside here in Rudradvīpa and chant the glories of Gaura.**

### Text 107

*śrī-nīla-lohita-rudra-gaṇa-adhipati*  
*mahānande nṛtya hethā kare niti niti*

**Перевод:**

**“Śrī Nīlalohita Śiva, the leader of the Nīlalohita Rudras, always dances here with great joy.**

### Text 108

*rudra-nṛtya dekhi’ ākāśete deva-gaṇa*  
*ānandete kare sabe puṣpa-variṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“Seeing the dancing of the Rudras, the gods in the sky joyfully shower flowers upon them.**

### Text 109

*kadācit viṣṇu-svāmī āsi’ digvijaye*  
*rudradvīpe rahe rātre śiṣya-gaṇa laye*

**Перевод:**

**“Once, Viṣṇu Svāmī, while conquering all directions, came to Rudradvīpa with his disciples and stayed the night.**

### Text 110

*‘hari hari’ bali’ nṛtya kare śiṣya-gaṇa*  
*viṣṇu-svāmī śruti-stuti karena paṭhana*

**Перевод:**

**“The disciples danced, chanting, ‘Hari! Hari!’, and Viṣṇu Svāmī recited prayers from the scriptures.**

### Text 111

*bhakti ālocanā dekhi’ haye haraṣita*  
*kṛpā kari’ dekhā dila śrī-nīla-lohita*

**Перевод:**

**“Seeing this devotional discussion, Śrī Nīlalohita Śiva was pleased and mercifully revealed himself.**

### Text 112

*vaiṣṇava-sabhāya rudra haila upanīta*  
*dekhi’ viṣṇu-svāmī ati haila camakita*

**Перевод:**

**“Upon seeing that Śiva had arrived at a Vaiṣṇava assembly, Viṣṇu Svāmī became completely amazed.**

### Text 113

*kara yuḍi’ stava kare viṣṇu tata-kṣaṇa*  
*dayārdra ha-iyā rudra balena vacana*

**Перевод:**

**“He immediately joined his palms and prayed to Śiva. Melting with compassion, Śiva spoke to him.**

### Text 114

*‘tomarā vaiṣṇava-jana mama priya ati*  
*bhakti ālocanā dekhi’ tuṣṭa mama mati*

**Перевод:**

**“‘All you Vaiṣṇavas are very dear to me, and my heart is delighted to see your discussion of devotion.**

### Text 115

*vara māga diba āmi ha-iyā sadaya*  
*vaiṣṇave adeya mora kichu nāhi haya’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Request a boon, and I will kindly give it to you. There is nothing that I do not give to Vaiṣṇavas.’**

### Text 116

*daṇḍavat praṇamiyā viṣṇu mahāśaya*  
*kara yuḍi’ vara māge premānandamaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Filled with divine love, Viṣṇu Svāmī bowed down, joined his palms, and requested a boon.**

### Text 117

*‘ei vara deha prabhu āmā sabākāre*  
*bhakti-sampradāya siddhi labhi ataḥpare’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O Lord, give us the boon that hereafter we will establish a school of devotion.’**

### Text 118

*parama ānande rudra vara kari’ dāna*  
*nija sampradāya bali’ karila ākhyāna*

**Перевод:**

**“With great joy, Rudra granted this boon and named the school after himself.**

### Text 119

*sei haite viṣṇu-svāmī svīya sampradāya*  
*śrī-rudra nāmete khyāti diyā nāce gāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Viṣṇu Svāmī thus named his school the Śrī Rudra sampradāya, and danced and sang.**

### Text 120

*rudra-kṛpā-bale viṣṇu e sthāne rahiyā*  
*bhajile śrī-gaura-candra premera lāgiyā*

**Перевод:**

**“By Śiva’s mercy, Viṣṇu Svāmī stayed here and worshipped Śrī Gaura-candra, aspiring for divine love.**

### Text 121

*svapne āsi’ śrī-gaurāṅga viṣṇure balila*  
*‘mama bhakta rudra kṛpā tomāre ha-ila*

**Перевод:**

**“Śrī Gaurāṅga came to Viṣṇu Svāmī in a dream and said, ‘My devotee Śiva blessed you.**

### Text 122

*dhanya tumi navadvīpe pāile bhakti-dhana*  
*śuddhādvaita-mata pracāraha ei-kṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“‘You are fortunate to have attained the wealth of devotion in Navadvīpa. For now, preach pure non-dualism.**

### Text 123

*kata-dine habe mora prakaṭa samaya*  
*śrī-vallabha-bhaṭṭa rūpe hai-be udaya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Later, at the time of My appearance, you will appear as Śrī Vallabha Bhaṭṭa.**

### Text 124

*śrī-kṣetre āmāre tumi kari’ daraśane*  
*sampradāye siddhi pābe giyā mahāvane’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘You will meet Me in Śrī Kṣetra, later go to Mahāvana, and establish your school.’**

### Text 125

*ohe jīva śrī-vallabha gokule ekhana*  
*tumi tathā gele pābe tāṅra daraśana”*

**Перевод:**

**“O Jīva, Śrī Vallabha is now in Gokula. When you go there, you will meet him.”**

### Text 126

*eta bali’ nityānanda dakṣiṇābhimukhe*  
*pāraḍāṅgā śrī-puline chalilena sukhe*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this, Nityānanda happily walked southbound towards Pāraḍāṅgā through Śrī Pulina.**

### Text 127

*puline yāiyā prabhu nityānanda-rāya*  
*śrī-rāsa-maṇḍala dhīra-samīra dekhāya*

**Перевод:**

**Passing through Śrī Pulina, Nityānanda Prabhu showed the devotees Śrī Rāsa Maṇḍala and Dhīra Samīra.**

### Text 128

*bale, “jīva ei dekha nitya-vṛndāvana*  
*vṛndāvana-līlā hethā pāya daraśana”*

**Перевод:**

**He said, “O Jīva, see here eternal Vṛndāvana and its Pastimes.”**

### Text 129

*vṛndāvana śuni’ jīva premete vihvala*  
*nayanete vahe dara-dara prema-jala*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing of Vṛndāvana, Jīva became overwhelmed with divine love, and tears of love streamed from his eyes.**

### Text 130

*prabhu bale, “śrī-gaurāṅga laye bhakta-jana*  
*ei sthāne rāsa-padya karila kīrtana*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu said, “Śrī Gaurāṅga chanted verses about the Rāsa-līlā here with the devotees.**

### Text 131

*mahārāsa-līlā-sthāna yathā vṛndāvane*  
*tathā ei sthāna jīva jāhnavī-puline*

**Перевод:**

**“O Jīva, this place on the bank of the Gaṅgā is the place of the great Rāsa-līlā in Vṛndāvana.**

### Text 132

*nitya-rāsa haya hethā gopī-gaṇa sane*  
*daraśana kare kabhu bhāgyavāna jane*

**Перевод:**

**“Daily, the Rāsa-līlā happens here with the** ***gopīs*****. Sometimes fortunate souls see this.**

### Text 133

*ihāra paścime dekha śrī-dhīra-samīra*  
*bhajanera sthāna ei śuna ohe dhīra*

**Перевод:**

**“See Śrī Dhīra Samīra to the west. O wise one, hear about this place of worship.**

### Text 134

*vraje dhīra-samīra ye yamunāra tīre*  
*sei sthāna hethā gaṅgā-pulina bhitare*

**Перевод:**

**“The place known as Dhīra Samīra on the bank of the Yamunā in Vraja is present here on the bank of the Gaṅgā.**

### Text 135

*dekhite gaṅgāra tīra vastutaḥ tā naya*  
*gaṅgāra paścima-dhāre śrī-yamunā vaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Actually, you do not see the bank of the Gaṅgā here. The Yamunā flows on the west side of the Gaṅgā.**

### Text 136

*yamunāra tīre ei pulina sundara*  
*ataeva vṛndāvana bale viśvambhara*

**Перевод:**

**“This beautiful bank is on the shore of the Yamunā. Thus, Viśvambhara called this place Vṛndāvana.**

### Text 137

*vṛndāvane yata sthāna līlāra āchaya*  
*se saba jānaha jīva ei sthāne haya*

**Перевод:**

**“O Jīva, know that all the places of the Pastimes in Vṛndāvana are present here.**

### Text 138

*vṛndāvane navadvīpe kichu nāhi bheda*  
*gaura-kṛṣṇe kabhu nāhi karibe prabheda”*

**Перевод:**

**“There is no difference between Vṛndāvana and Navadvīpa, and one should never differentiate between Gaura and Kṛṣṇa.”**

### Text 139

*mahābhāve gara-gara nityānanda-rāya*  
*vṛndāvana dekhāiyā jīve laye yāya*

**Перевод:**

**Exuberant with intense ecstasy, Nityānanda Rāya revealed Vṛndāvana to Jīva and then took him ahead.**

### Text 140

*kata-dūre uttarete kariyā gamana*  
*rudradvīpe sei rātri karila yāpana*

**Перевод:**

**They went a little ways north and spent that night in Rudradvīpa.**

### Text 141

*nitāi-jāhnavā-pada yāhāra sampada*  
*nadīyā-māhātmya gāya se bhaktivinoda*

**Перевод:**

**Bhaktivinoda, whose wealth is the feet of Nitāi and Jāhnavā, chants the glories of Nadīyā.**

## Chapter Sixteen – Śrī Bilvapakṣa and Śrī Bharadvāja Ṭilā

### Text 1

*jaya jaya nadīyā-bihārī gaura-candra*  
*jaya ekacakrā-pati prabhu-nityānanda*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to the reveller of Nadīyā, Gaura-candra! All glory to the Lord of Ekacakrā, Nityānanda Prabhu!**

### Text 2

*jaya śāntipura-nātha advaita īśvara*  
*rāmacandrapura-vāsī jaya gadādhara*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to the Lord of Śāntipura, Advaita Ācārya, and the resident of Rāmacandrapura Gadādhara Paṇḍita!**

### Text 3

*jaya jaya gauḍa-bhūmi cintāmaṇi-sāra*  
*kali-yuge kṛṣṇa yathā karilā bihāra*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to the land of Gauḍa, made of pure spiritual gemstone, where Kṛṣṇa performed Pastimes during Kali-yuga!**

### Text 4

*śrī-jāhnavī pāra haye padmāra nandana*  
*kichu-dūre giyā bale, “dekha bhakta-gaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu crossed the Gaṅgā, went a little ways ahead, and said, “O devotees, look!**

### Text 5

*bilvapakṣa nāma ei sthāna manohara*  
*bela-pukhariyā bali’ bale sarva-nara*

**Перевод:**

**“This enchanting place is named Bilvapakṣa. Everyone calls it Bel Pukhariyā.**

### Text 6

*vraja-dhāme yāre śāstre bale bilvavana*  
*navadvīpe sei sthāna kara daraśana*

**Перевод:**

**“See here in Navadvīpa the place which the scriptures call Bilvavan in Vraja Dhāma.**

### Text 7-8

*pañca-vaktra bilvakeśa āchhila hethāya*  
*eka-pakṣa bilva-dale ārādhiyā taṅya*

*brāhmaṇa sajjana-gaṇe tuṣila taṅhāre*  
*kṛṣṇa-bhakti vara dila tāhā sabākāre*

**Перевод:**

**“Śiva, the lord of** ***bael*****, resides here. After a group of noble** ***brāhmaṇas*** **satisfied him, having worshipped him with** ***bael*** **leaves for a fortnight, Śiva gave them the boon of devotion to Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 9

*sei vipra-gaṇa madhye nimbāditya chila*  
*viśeṣa kariyā pañca-vaktre ārādhila*

**Перевод:**

**“Amongst the** ***brāhmaṇas*** **was Nimbāditya, who worshipped Śiva exceptionally well.**

### Text 10

*kṛpā kari’ pañca-vaktra kahila takhana*  
*‘ei grāma-prānte āchhe divya bilvavana*

**Перевод:**

**“Mercifully, Śiva said to him, ‘On the edge of this village is a divine** ***bael*** **forest.**

### Text 11

*sei vana madhye catuḥsana āchhe dhyāne*  
*taṅdera kṛpāya tava habe divya-jñāne*

**Перевод:**

**“‘The four Kumāras are meditating in that forest, and by their grace, you will attain divine knowledge.**

### Text 12

*catuḥsana guru tava taṅdera sevāya*  
*sarva-artha lābha tava ha-ibe hethāya’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘The four Kumāras are your Gurus, and through their service, you will attain everything here.’**

### Text 13

*eta bali’ maheśvara haila antardhāna*  
*nimbāditya anveṣaṇa kari’ pāya sthāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Saying this, Śiva disappeared. Nimbāditya then searched and found the place.**

### Text 14-16

*bilvavana madhye dekhe vedī manohara*  
*catuḥsana basiyāchhe tāhāra upara*

*sanaka sananda āra ṛṣi sanātana*  
*śrī-sanat-kumāra ei ṛṣi cāra jana*

*vṛddha-keśa sannidhāne anya alakṣita*  
*vastra-hīna sukumāra udāra carita*

**Перевод:**

**“In the** ***bael*** **forest, he saw the four Kumāras—the sages Sanaka, Sananda, Sanātana, and Sanat Kumāra—seated on a beautiful platform beside Śiva, unseen by others. They were unclothed, very young, and of noble character.**

### Text 17

*dekhi’ nimbādityācārya parama kautuke*  
*‘hare kṛṣṇa hare kṛṣṇa’ ḍāki’ bale sukhe*

**Перевод:**

**“Greatly excited upon seeing them, Nimbāditya Ācārya happily called out, ‘Hare Kṛṣṇa! Hare Kṛṣṇa!’**

### Text 18

*hari-nāma śuni’ kāne dhyāna bhaṅga haila*  
*sammukhe vaiṣṇava-mūrti dekhite pāila*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing the Name of the Lord, their meditation broke, and they saw the form of a Vaiṣṇava in front of them.**

### Text 19

*vaiṣṇava dekhiyā sabe haye hṛṣṭa-mana*  
*nimbāditye krame krame deya āliṅgana*

**Перевод:**

**“Seeing the Vaiṣṇava, they were pleased, and one by one they embraced Nimbāditya.**

### Text 20

*‘ke tūmi kena vā hethā bala paricaya*  
*tomāra prārthanā morā purāba niścaya’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Who are you? Why have you come here? Please introduce yourself. We will certainly fulfil your prayers.’**

### Text 21

*śuni’ nimbāditya daṇḍavat praṇamiyā*  
*nija paricaya deya vinīta ha-iyā*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing their words, Nimbāditya prostrated and humbly introduced himself.**

### Text 22

*nimbārkera paricaya kariyā śravaṇa*  
*śrī-sanat-kumāra kaya sahāsya vadana*

**Перевод:**

**“Upon hearing Nimbārka’s introduction, Śrī Sanat Kumāra spoke with a smiling face.**

### Text 23

*‘kali ghora ha-ibe jāniyā kṛpāmaya*  
*bhakti pracārite citte karila niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Knowing that Kali-yuga would be dark, the merciful Lord decided to preach devotion.**

### Text 24

*cāri-jana bhakte śakti kariyā arpaṇa*  
*bhakti pracārite viśve karila preraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“‘He empowered four devotees and sent them to preach devotion throughout the world.**

### Text 25

*rāmānuja, madhva, viṣṇu—ei tina jana*  
*tumi ta caturtha hao bhakta mahājana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Rāmānuja, Madhvācārya, and Viṣṇu Swāmī are three of them. You are the fourth of these great devotees.**

### Text 26

*śrī-devī karila rāmānuje aṅgīkāra*  
*brahmā madhvācārye rudra viṣṇuke svīkāra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Lakṣmī Devī accepted Rāmānuja, Brahmā accepted Madhvācārya, and Śiva accepted Viṣṇu Svāmī.**

### Text 27

*āmarā tomāke āja jāninu āpana*  
*śiṣya kari’ dhanya ha-i ei prayojana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Today, we have met you personally. We will make you our disciple and thus become fortunate. This is our purpose.**

### Text 28

*pūrve morā abheda-cintāya cinu rata*  
*kṛpā-yoge sei pāpa haila dūra-gata*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Previously, we meditated on non-duality, but by the grace of the Lord such sin has left us.**

### Text 29

*ebe śuddha-bhakti ati upādeya jāni*  
*saṁhitā racanā kariyāchhi eka-khāni*

**Перевод:**

**“‘We now understand that pure devotion is supremely relishable, and we have written a scripture about it.**

### Text 30

*sanat-kumāra-saṁhitā ihāra nāma haya*  
*ei-mate dīkṣā tava ha-ibe niścaya’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Its name is** ***Sanat-kumāra-saṁhitā*****. You will certainly be initiated into its teachings.’**

### Text 31

*guru-anugraha dekhi’ nimbārka dhīmāna*  
*avilambe āilā kari’ bhāgīrathī snāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Seeing the grace of his guru, the wise Nimbārka immediately bathed in the Gaṅgā and returned.**

### Text 32

*sāṣṭāṅge paḍiyā bale sadainya vacana*  
*‘e adhame tāra nātha patita-pāvana’*

**Перевод:**

**“He prostrated himself and humbly said, ‘O lord! Saviour of the fallen! Deliver this lowly soul.’**

### Text 33

*catuḥsana kaila śrī-yugala-mantra dāna*  
*bhāva-mārge upāsanā karila vidhāna*

**Перевод:**

**“The four Kumāras gave him a mantra to worship the Divine Couple and taught him how to worship on the path of love.**

### Text 34

*mantra labhi’ nimbāditya siddha pīṭha-sthāne*  
*upāsanā karilena saṁhitā-vidhāne*

**Перевод:**

**“Receiving the mantra, Nimbāditya performed worship in this holy place according to the teachings of the** ***Sanat-kumāra-saṁhitā*****.**

### Text 35

*kṛpā kari’ rādhā-kṛṣṇa tāre dekhā dila*  
*rūpera chaṭāya catur-dika ālo haila*

**Перевод:**

**“Mercifully, Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa revealed Themselves to him, and the lustre of Their forms illuminated the four directions.**

### Text 36

*mṛdu mṛdu hāsi’ mukhe balena vacana*  
*‘dhanya tumi nimbāditya karile sādhana*

**Перевод:**

**“Gently smiling, They said, ‘O Nimbāditya, you are fortunate: you have performed worship here.**

### Text 37

*ati priya navadvīpa āmā doṅhākāra*  
*hethā doṅhe eka-rūpa śacīra kumāra’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Navadvīpa is very dear to Us. Here, We are present in one form as the son of Śacī Devī.’**

### Text 38

*balite balite gaura-rūpa prakāśila*  
*rūpa dekhi’ nimbāditya vihvala ha-ila*

**Перевод:**

**“As They spoke, They revealed Their form as Gaurāṅga. Seeing this form, Nimbāditya was overwhelmed.**

### Text 39

*bale, ‘kabhu nāhi dekhi nāhi śuni kāne*  
*e hena apūrva rūpa āchhe kona-khāne’*

**Перевод:**

**“He said, ‘I have never seen or heard of this extraordinary form anywhere.’**

### Text 40

*kṛpā kari’ mahāprabhu balila takhana*  
*e-rūpa gopana ebe kara mahājana*

**Перевод:**

**“Mahāprabhu then mercifully said, ‘O great soul, for now, keep this form a secret.**

### Text 41

*pracāraha kṛṣṇa-bhakti yugala-vilāsa*  
*yugala-vilāse mora atyanta ullāsa*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Preach devotion to Kṛṣṇa and the Pastimes of the Divine Couple. Their Pastimes are My greatest joy.**

### Text 42

*ye samaye gaura-rūpa prakaṭa ha-ibe*  
*śrī-vidyā-vilāse tabe baḍa raṅga habe*

**Перевод:**

**“‘When I appear in the form of Gaura, I will find great pleasure in Pastimes of scholarship.**

### Text 43

*se samaye kāśmīra pradeśe janma laye*  
*bhramibe bhārata-varṣa digvijayī haye*

**Перевод:**

**“‘At that time, you will take birth in Kāśmīr and travel throughout the land of Bhārata, conquering all directions.**

### Text 44

*keśava kāśmīrī nāme sakale tomāya*  
*mahāvidyāvāna bali’ sarvatrete gāya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Everyone, everywhere, will call you Keśava Kāśmīrī and praise you for being highly learned.**

### Text 45

*bhramite bhramite ei navadvīpa-dhāme*  
*āsiyā thākibe tumi māyāpura-grāme*

**Перевод:**

**“‘While travelling, you will come to Navadvīpa Dhāma and stay in the village of Māyāpura.**

### Text 46

*navadvīpe baḍa baḍa adhyāpaka-gaṇa*  
*tava nāma śuni’ karibeka palāyana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Hearing your name, the greatest scholars in Navadvīpa will flee.**

### Text 47

*āmi ta takhana vidyā-vilāse mātiba*  
*parājiyā tomā sabe ānanda labhiba*

**Перевод:**

**“‘At that time, immersed in Pastimes of scholarship, I will defeat you and feel great pleasure.**

### Text 48

*sarasvatī-kṛpā-bale jāni’ mama tattva*  
*āśraya karibe more chāḍiyā mahattva*

**Перевод:**

**“‘By the grace of Sarasvatī, you will understand My identity, take shelter of Me, and give up your pride.**

### Text 49

*bhakti dāna kari’ āmi tomāre takhana*  
*bhakti pracārite punaḥ kariba preraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Then, I will give you devotion and send you to preach devotion again.**

### Text 50

*ataeva dvaitādvaita-mata pracāriyā*  
*tuṣṭa kara ebe more gopana kariyā*

**Перевод:**

**“‘So for now, satisfy Me by preaching the conception of dualistic non-dualism and keep My identity secret.**

### Text 51

*yabe āmi saṅkīrtana ārambha kariba*  
*tomādera mata-sāra nije pracāriba*

**Перевод:**

**“‘When I start My** ***saṅkīrtana*** **Pastimes, I will preach the essence of your conception.**

### Text 52

*madhva ha-ite sāra-dvaya kariba grahaṇa*  
*eka haya kevala-advaita nirasana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I will accept two essential principles from Madhva Ācārya. One is his refutation of exclusive non-dualism.**

### Text 53

*kṛṣṇa-mūrti nitya jāni’ taṅhāra sevana*  
*sei ta dvitīya sāra jāna mahājana*

**Перевод:**

**“‘O great soul, know the second essential principle to be serving Kṛṣṇa’s Deity form, knowing the Deity to be eternal.**

### Text 54

*rāmānuja haite āmi la-i dui sāra*  
*ananya bhakati bhakta-jana sevā āra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘From Rāmānuja, I will take two essential principles: exclusive devotion and service to the devotees.**

### Text 55

*viṣṇu haite dui sāra kariba svīkāra*  
*tadīya sarvasva bhāva rāga-mārga āra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘From Viṣṇu Svāmī, I will accept two essential principles: surrender to the Lord and the path of love.**

### Text 56

*tomā haite laba āmi dui mahāsāra*  
*ekānta rādhikāśraya gopī-bhāva āra’*

**Перевод:**

**“‘From you, I will accept two most essential principles: taking shelter of Rādhārāṇī exclusively and serving in the mood of the** ***gopīs*****.’**

### Text 57

*eta bali’ gaura-candra haila adarśana*  
*preme nimbāditya kata karila rodana*

**Перевод:**

**“Saying this, Gaura-candra disappeared, and Nimbāditya cried profusely with divine love.**

### Text 58

*guru-pāda-padma nami’ cale deśāntara*  
*kṛṣṇa-bhakti pracārite ha-ilā tatpara”*

**Перевод:**

**“Nimbāditya then bowed at the lotus feet of his Guru and travelled elsewhere, intent upon preaching devotion to Kṛṣṇa.”**

### Text 59-60

*dūra haite rāma-tīrtha jīvere dekhāya*  
*kolāsure haladhara badhila yathāya*

*karilena gaṅgā-snāna laye yadu-gaṇa*  
*rukmapura bali’ nāma prakāśa ekhana*

**Перевод:**

**From afar, Nityānanda Prabhu showed Rāma Tīrtha to Jīva, where Balarāma killed Kolāsura and bathed in the Gaṅgā with the Yadus. This place is now known as Rukmapuar.**

### Text 61

*navadvīpa-parikramā ei eka-śeṣa*  
*kārtika māsete tathā māhātmya viśeṣa*

**Перевод:**

**It is at one end of the route to circumambulate Navadvīpa. It is especially glorious during the month of Kārttika.**

### Text 62

*bilvapakṣa chāḍi’ prabhu laye bhakta-gaṇa*  
*bharadvāja-ṭilā grāme kare ārohaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Leaving Bilvapakṣa, Nityānanda Prabhu and the devotees went to Bharadvāja Ṭilā.**

### Text 63

*nityānanda bale, “ei sthāne muni-vara*  
*āilena dekhi’ tīrtha śrī-gaṅgā-sāgara*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda said, “The best of the sages, Bharadvāja Muni, visited Śrī Gaṅgā Sāgara and then came here.**

### Text 64

*hethā śrī-gaurāṅga-candra kari’ ārādhana*  
*rahilena kata-dina muni mahājana*

**Перевод:**

**“The great sage stayed here for some time worshipping Śrī Gaurāṅga-candra.**

### Text 65

*taṅra ārādhane tuṣṭa haye viśvambhara*  
*nija-rūpe dekhā dilā sadaya antara*

**Перевод:**

**“Satisfied by the worship of the sage, Viśvambhara revealed His form to the sage with a compassionate heart.**

### Text 66

*munire balila, ‘tava iṣṭa siddha habe*  
*āmāra prakaṭa-kāle āmāre dekhibe’*

**Перевод:**

**“He said to the sage, ‘Your desire will be fulfilled: you will see Me at the time of My appearance.’**

### Text 67

*ei kathā bali’ prabhu haila antardhāna*  
*bharadvāja mahāpreme ha-ila ajñāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Saying this, the Lord disappeared, and Bharadvāja Muni fainted with intense divine love.**

### Text 68

*kata-dina thāki’ ei ṭilāra upara*  
*anya-tīrtha daraśane gelā muni-vara*

**Перевод:**

**“The great sage stayed here for some time on this hill and then went to visit other holy places.**

### Text 69

*lokete bhāruiḍāṅgā bale ei sthāne*  
*mahātīrtha haya ei śāstrera vidhāne”*

**Перевод:**

**“People call this place Bhāruiḍāṅgā. The scriptures say that it is a great holy place.”**

### Text 70

*balite balite sabe yāya māyāpura*  
*āgubāḍi laya sabe īśāna ṭhākura*

**Перевод:**

**While speaking, the party arrived in Māyāpura. Īśāna Ṭhākura came forward and received them.**

### Text 71

*mahāpreme nityānanda karena nartana*  
*sakala vaiṣṇava meli’ karena kīrtana*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda Prabhu danced with intense divine love, and all the Vaiṣṇavas joined together and chanted.**

### Text 72

*jagannātha-miśrālaya sarva-pīṭha-sāra*  
*nāma saha yathā śrī-gaurāṅga-avatāra*

**Перевод:**

**The home of Jagannātha Miśra, where Śrī Gaurāṅga descended with His Name, is the best of all holy places.**

### Text 73

*sei dina prabhu-gṛhe prabhura jananī*  
*vaiṣṇava-gaṇere anna khāoyāna āpani*

**Перевод:**

**That day, the Lord’s mother personally fed the devotees in the Lord’s home.**

### Text 74

*ki ānanda haila tathā nā haya varṇana*  
*mahāsamārohe haya nāma-saṅkīrtana*

**Перевод:**

**What sort of joy arose there? It was indescribable, and the** ***nāma-saṅkīrtana*** **erupted tumultuously.**

### Text 75

*nitāi-jāhnavā-pada-chāyā yāra āśa*  
*e bhaktivinoda gāya nadīyā-vilāsa*

**Перевод:**

**Bhaktivinoda, whose aspiration is the shade of Nitāi and Jāhnavā’s feet, chants the Pastimes of the Lord in Nadīyā.**

## Chapter Seventeen – Śrī Jīva Goswāmī’s Questions and Śrīla Nityānanda Prabhu’s Answers

### Text 1

*jaya jaya gorā-cāṅda jaya nityānanda*  
*jayādvaita gadādhara prema-rasānanda*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to Gorā-cāṅda, Nityānanda, Advaita, Gadādhara, and the ecstatic joy of divine love!**

### Text 2

*jaya śrīvāsādi bhakta navadvīpa jaya*  
*jaya nāma-saṅkīrtana premera nilaya*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to Śrīvāsa, the devotees, and Navadvīpa! All glory to** ***nāma-saṅkīrtana*****, the abode of divine love!**

### Text 3

*basiyāchhe nityānanda śrīvāsa-aṅgane*  
*gaura-preme vāridhārā vahe du’nayane*

**Перевод:**

**Nityānanda sat in the courtyard of Śrīvāsa, and in divine love for Gaura, streams of tears flowed from His eyes.**

### Text 4

*cāri-dike vaiṣṇava sajjana agaṇana*  
*gaura-prema-pārābāre magna sarva-jana*

**Перевод:**

**There were countless Vaiṣṇava** ***sādhus*** **in all directions, and everyone was immersed in the ocean of divine love for Gaura.**

### Text 5

*kata-kṣaṇe śrī-jīva gosvāmī mahāśaya*  
*śrī-yugala-preme matta, ha-ila udaya*

**Перевод:**

**At that time, Śrī Jīva Gosvāmī became mad with love for the Divine Couple.**

### Text 6

*daṇḍavat praṇamiyā nityānanda-pāya*  
*śrīvāsa-aṅgane tabe gaḍāgaḍi yāya*

**Перевод:**

**He prostrated himself at the feet of Nityānanda and rolled on the ground in the courtyard of Śrīvāsa.**

### Text 7

*yatane śrī-nityānanda jijñāsena vacana*  
*“kata-dina pare yābe tumi vṛndāvana”*

**Перевод:**

**Affectionately, Nityānanda Prabhu asked, “When will you go to Vṛndāvana?”**

### Text 8

*jīva bale, “prabhu-ājñā sarvopari haya*  
*ājñā pāile kari āmi vṛndāvanāśraya*

**Перевод:**

**Jīva answered, “The order of the Lord is above all. When I receive Your order, I will take shelter in Vṛndāvana.**

### Text 9

*dui eka kathā mora āchhe jijñāsite*  
*uttara dāo he prabhu e dāsera hite*

**Перевод:**

**“I have one or two questions to ask You. O Lord, please answer them for the benefit of this servant.**

### Text 10

*ei navadvīpa-dhāma haya vṛndāvana*  
*tabe kena vṛndāvana gamane yatana”*

**Перевод:**

**“Navadvīpa Dhāma is Vṛndāvana. So, why should I endeavour to go to Vṛndāvana?”**

### Text 11

*jīva-praśna śuni’ prabhu karena uttara*  
*“baḍa guhya-kathā ei śuna ataḥpara*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing Jīva’s question, Nityānanda Prabhu answered, “This is a very confidential subject. Now listen.**

### Text 12

*prabhura prakaṭa-līlā yata-dina raya*  
*dekha yena bahirmukha jane nā jānaya*

**Перевод:**

**“As long as the Pastimes of the Lord remain manifest, see to it that averse souls do not know about this.**

### Text 13

*navadvīpa vṛndāvana haya eka tattva*  
*paraspara kichu nāhi hīnatva mahattva*

**Перевод:**

**“Navadvīpa and Vṛndāvana are one; neither one is lesser or greater than the other.**

### Text 14

*sei vṛndāvana-dhāma rasera ādhāra*  
*se rasa nā pāya yāra nāhi adhikāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Vṛndāvana Dhāma is an abode of** ***rasa*****, but unqualified souls do not attain that** ***rasa*****.**

### Text 15

*kṛpā kari’ sei dhāma navadvīpa haya*  
*hethā rasa-adhikāra jīve upajaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Vṛndāvana Dhāma mercifully manifests as Navadvīpa, and here, souls develop the qualification to attain** ***rasa*****.**

### Text 16

*rādhā-kṛṣṇa-līlā haya sarva-rasa-sāra*  
*sahasā tāhāte nāhi haya adhikāra*

**Перевод:**

**“The Pastimes of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa are the highest of all** ***rasas*****, and qualification for them does not come immediately.**

### Text 17

*kata janma tapasyā kariyā haya jñāna*  
*jñāna paripakke pāya rasera sandhāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Performing austerities for many births results in knowledge, and when knowledge matures, souls discover** ***rasa*****.**

### Text 18

*tāhāte vyāghāta bahu āchhe sarva-kṣaṇa*  
*ataeva sudurlabha rasa mahādhana*

**Перевод:**

**“There are always many obstacles involved, and thus, the supreme wealth of** ***rasa*** **is very difficult to attain.**

### Text 19

*yei sei vraje giyā nāhi pāya rasa*  
*aparādha-vaśe rasa haya ta virasa*

**Перевод:**

**“Not everyone who goes to Vraja attains** ***rasa*****, and as a result of offences,** ***rasa*** **becomes tasteless.**

### Text 20

*ghora kali-kāle aparādha sarva-kāla*  
*jīvera jīvana svalpa baḍa-i jañjāla*

**Перевод:**

**“During the darkness of Kali-yuga, offences are constant, and the life of a soul is short and extremely troublesome.**

### Text 21

*icchā karileo vraja-rasa labhya naya*  
*ataeva kṛṣṇa-kṛpā rasa-hetu haya*

**Перевод:**

**“Even if souls desire it, the** ***rasa*** **of Vraja is not attainable. The mercy of Kṛṣṇa is therefore the only cause of attaining** ***rasa*****.**

### Text 22

*rādhā-kṛṣṇa kṛpā kari’ jīvera upara*  
*vṛndāvana saha samudita ataḥpara*

**Перевод:**

**“Being merciful to the souls, Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa have again appeared with Vṛndāvana.**

### Text 23-24

*eka-mūrti rādhā-kṛṣṇa prabhu gaurahari*  
*śacī-garbhe navadvīpe ebe avatari’*

*rasa-adhikāra jīve karena pradāna*  
*aparādha bādhā kabhu nāhi pāya sthāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Descending in Navadvīpa through the womb of Śacī Devī in one form, Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, as Lord Gaurahari, now give souls qualification for** ***rasa*****. Offences are never an obstacle in Navadvīpa.**

### Text 25

*hethā vāsa kari’ nāma karile āśraya*  
*rase adhikāra janme aparādha kṣaya*

**Перевод:**

**“If souls reside here and take shelter of the Name, their qualification for** ***rasa*** **develops and their offences are eradicated.**

### Text 26

*svalpa-dine kṛṣṇa-prema haya ta ujjvala*  
*yugala-rasera vārtā haya ta prabala*

**Перевод:**

**“Within a short time, divine love for Kṛṣṇa shines within them, and they become captivated by discussion of the Divine Couple’s** ***rasa*****.**

### Text 27

*tabe jīva gaura-kṛpā kariyā arjana*  
*yugala-rasera pīṭha pāya vṛndāvana*

**Перевод:**

**“Attaining the mercy of Gaura, souls then reach Vṛndāvana, the abode of the Divine Couple’s rasa.**

### Text 28

*gūḍha-tattva ei nāhi kaha yāre tāre*  
*navadvīpa-vṛndāvane bheda ha-ite nāre*

**Перевод:**

**“Do not tell anyone this confidential truth. There is no difference between Navadvīpa and Vṛndāvana.**

### Text 29

*tomāra āśraya ebe rasa-pīṭha haya*  
*ataeva vṛndāvana karaha āśraya*

**Перевод:**

**“Your shelter is now that abode of** ***rasa*****. Therefore, take shelter in Vṛndāvana.**

### Text 30

*ei dhāme vṛndāvana haya ta udaya*  
*tabu vraja-dhāma tava ha-uka āśraya*

**Перевод:**

**“Vṛndāvana is present here. Still, let Vraja Dhāma be your shelter.**

### Text 31

*vraja-rasa adhikāre navadvīpāśraya*  
*jīvera kartavya sadā vallabha-tanaya*

**Перевод:**

**“O Jīva, the duty of the soul is always to take shelter in Navadvīpa to become qualified for the** ***rasa*** **of Vraja.**

### Text 32

*vraja-rasa prāpti-sthale vṛndāvana vāsa*  
*jīvera yathāya haya rasera ullāsa*

**Перевод:**

**“In the place to attain the** ***rasa*** **of Vraja (Navadvīpa), souls reside in Vraja and experience the joy of its** ***rasa*****.**

### Text 33

*navadvīpa-kṛpā yabe labhe sādhu-jana*  
*tabe anāyāse labhe dhāma vṛndāvana”*

**Перевод:**

**“When** ***sādhus*** **attain the grace of Navadvīpa, they easily attain Vṛndāvana Dhāma.”**

### Text 34

*prabhura siddhānta śuni’ jīva mahāśaya*  
*parama ānande prabhu-caraṇa dharaya*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the conclusions of Nityānanda Prabhu, Jīva grasped His feet with great joy.**

### Text 35

*caraṇa dhariyā bale, “kathā eka āra*  
*āchhe mora śuna prabhu sarva-sārātsāra*

**Перевод:**

**Grasping His feet, Jīva said, “I have another question. Please hear it, O Supreme Lord!**

### Text 36

*ei navadvīpe vāsa kare bahu-jana*  
*sabe kena kṛṣṇa-bhakti nā kare arjana*

**Перевод:**

**“Why do many souls who reside in Navadvīpa not attain devotion to Kṛṣṇa?**

### Text 37

*dhāme vaise tabu kena aparādha raya*  
*āmāra ha-ila ebe viṣama saṁśaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Why do souls reside in the Dhāma but commit offences? This is now my troubling doubt.**

### Text 38

*kise tabe niścinta ha-ibe viṣṇu-jana*  
*bala prabhu viśva-dhāma nitya nirañjana”*

**Перевод:**

**“How will devotees of the Lord become free from anxiety about this? Please answer me, O Lord, O pure, eternal abode of the universe!”**

### Text 39

*nitāi-jāhnavā-pada-chāyā āśa yāra*  
*se bhaktivinoda kahe akiñcana chāra*

**Перевод:**

**So speaks the poor and fallen Bhaktivinoda, whose aspiration is the shade of Nitāi and Jāhnavā’s feet.**

## Chapter Eighteen – The Removal of Śrī Jīva Gosvāmī’s Doubts and His Travelling to Vṛndāvana

### Text 1

*jaya jaya śrī-gaurāṅga śacira nandana*  
*jaya padmāvatī-suta jāhnavā-jīvana*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to the son of Śacī, Śrī Gaurāṅga, and the son of Padmāvatī, the life of Jāhnavā!**

### Text 2

*jaya sītāpati jaya jaya gadādhara*  
*jaya śrīvāsādi yata gaura-parikara*

**Перевод:**

**All glory to Advaita, Gadādhara, Śrīvāsa, and all the associates of the Lord!**

### Text 3

*śuniyā jīvera praśna nityānanda-rāya*  
*balena nigūḍha tattva vaiṣṇava-sabhāya*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing Jīva’s question, Nityānanda Rāya explained a highly confidential truth in the assembly of Vaiṣṇavas.**

### Text 4

*“śuna jīva vṛndāvana navadvīpa-dhāma*  
*ajasra ānandamaya jīvera viśrāma*

**Перевод:**

**“O Jīva, listen! Vṛndāvana Dhāma and Navadvīpa Dhāma are shelters for countless joyful souls.**

### Text 5

*śuddha-jīva-gaṇa jaḍā-prakṛtira pāra*  
*sadā vāsa kare hethā kṛṣṇa-paribāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Pure souls are beyond material nature; they reside here in the Dhāma eternally as associates of Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 6

*ei dhāma nitya-dhāma viśuddha cinmaya*  
*jaḍa-deśa-kāla hethā pāya parājaya*

**Перевод:**

**“The Dhāma is eternal, pure, and spiritual. Material time and space are suppressed here.**

### Text 7

*e dhāmera deśa-kāla cid-ānandamaya*  
*jaḍa-dharma viparyaya sadā lakṣya haya*

**Перевод:**

**“Time and space in the Dhāma are spiritual and joyful. Everything here always appears the opposite of material nature.**

### Text 8

*gṛha-dvārā nada-nadī kānana catvara*  
*cinmaya sakala jāna ati manohara*

**Перевод:**

**“Know that all the homes, gates, rivers, streams, forests, and courtyards here are spiritual and extremely beautiful.**

### Text 9

*sei ta ānanda-dhāma prakṛtira pāra*  
*acintya kṛṣṇera śakti parama udāra*

**Перевод:**

**“This is the abode of joy beyond material nature, the grand manifestation of Kṛṣṇa’s inconceivable energy.**

### Text 10

*sei śakti-krame dhāma hethā avatāra*  
*jīvera nistāra janya kṛṣṇa-icchā-sāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Kṛṣṇa’s greatest desire is for the souls’ deliverance, and thus the Dhāma has descended here by Kṛṣṇa’s inconceivable energy.**

### Text 11

*dhāma madhye kabhu nahe jaḍa avasthiti*  
*jaḍa-baddha jīva nāhi pāya hethā gati*

**Перевод:**

**“Matter is never present within the Dhāma, and conditioned souls do not receive entrance here.**

### Text 12

*dhāmera upare jaḍa-māyā pāti’ jāla*  
*ācchādiyā rākhe ei dhāma cira-kāla*

**Перевод:**

**“The material energy spreads a covering over the Dhāma and keeps it covered forever.**

### Text 13

*śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya yāra nāhika sambandha*  
*jālera upare vāsa kare sei andha*

**Перевод:**

**“Souls who have no relationship with Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya blindly reside on the surface of this covering.**

### Text 14

*mane bhāve, ‘āmi āchhi navadvīpa-pure’*  
*prauḍhā-māyā mugdha kari’ rākhe tāre dūre*

**Перевод:**

**“They think, ‘I am in Navadvīpa Dhāma’, but Prauḍhā Māyā bewilders them and keeps them far away.**

### Text 15

*yadi kona bhāgyodaye sādhu-saṅga pāya*  
*tabe kṛṣṇa-caitanya-sambandha āse tāya*

**Перевод:**

**“If, by some fortune, souls attain the association of the** ***sādhus*****, then their relationship with Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya develops.**

### Text 16

*sambandha nigūḍha tattva vallabha-nandana*  
*sahaje nā bujhe baddha-jīva sei dhana*

**Перевод:**

**“O Jīva, such relationships are a highly confidential subject, a fortune that conditioned souls do not easily understand.**

### Text 17

*mukhe bale, ‘śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya prabhu mora*  
*hṛdaya sambandha hīna sadā māyā-bhora*

**Перевод:**

**“Someone may say, ‘Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya is my Lord’, but have no relationship with Him at heart and be constantly engrossed in** ***māyā*****.**

### Text 18

*sei saba loka vaise māyā-jālopari*  
*kabhu śuddha-bhakti nāhi pāya hari hari*

**Перевод:**

**“Such persons reside on the surface of Māyā’s covering and never attain pure devotion. O Lord! O Lord!**

### Text 19

*dharma-dhvaji sukapaṭī sadā dainya-hīna*  
*dambha-guṇe āpanāke bhāve samīcīna*

**Перевод:**

**“They are religious pretenders. They are always deceitful and devoid of humility. Because of their pride, they consider themselves wise.**

### Text 20

*sei dambha chāḍe sādhu-caraṇa-prasāde*  
*tṛṇa haite āpanāke dīna kari’ sādhe*

**Перевод:**

**“By the grace of the** ***sādhus*****, however, they abandon their pride and willingly consider themselves lower than grass.**

### Text 21

*vṛkṣāpekṣā haya tāra sahiṣṇutā-guṇa*  
*amānī āpani anye sammāne nipuṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“Their tolerance becomes greater than that of a tree, and they become prideless and expert at honouring others.**

### Text 22

*ei cāri guṇe guṇī kṛṣṇa-guṇa gāya*  
*caitanya-sambandha tāra vasena hiyāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Enriched with these four qualities, they chant the glories of Kṛṣṇa, and their relationship with Śrī Caitanya develops within the heart.**

### Text 23

*śrī-kṛṣṇa-sambandha śānta dāsya sakhya āra*  
*vātsalya madhura iti pañca-parakāra*

**Перевод:**

**“There are five relationships with Śrī Kṛṣṇa: those of adoration, servitorship, friendship, affectionate guardianship, and paramour love.**

### Text 24

*śānta dāsya bhāve kari’ gaurāṅga bhajana*  
*labhe vātsalyādi-rasa kṛṣṇe sādhu-jana*

**Перевод:**

**“*****Sādhus*** **serve Gaurāṅga in the relationships of adoration and servitorship and thereby attain relationships of affectionate guardianship and paramour love with Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 25

*yāra yei sambandha-janita siddha-bhāva*  
*tāhāra bhajane sei bhāvera prabhāva*

**Перевод:**

**“The soul’s perfected mood that arises from their particular relationship with the Lord predominates in their service.**

### Text 26

*gaura-kṛṣṇe bheda yāra sei jīva chāra*  
*śrī-kṛṣṇa-sambandha kabhu nā haya tāhāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Souls who differentiate between Gaura and Kṛṣṇa are wicked, and they never develop a relationship with Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 27

*sādhu-saṅge dainya ādi guṇa yāra haya*  
*sei jīva dāsya-rase gaurāṅga bhajaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Souls who develop, through the association of the** ***sādhus*****, the qualities of humility, tolerance, pridelessness, and respectfulness, serve Gaurāṅga in a relationship of servitorship.**

### Text 28

*dāsya-rasa parākāṣṭhā gaurāṅga-bhajane*  
*‘mahāprabhu’ śrī-gaurāṅga bale sādhu-jane*

**Перевод:**

**“The relationship of servitorship is full-fledged in the service of Gaurāṅga; the** ***sādhus*** **call Śrī Gaurāṅga ‘Mahāprabhu’.**

### Text 29

*madhura-premete yāra haya adhikāra*  
*rādhā-kṛṣṇa-rūpe gaura-bhajana tāhāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Souls who are qualified for a relationship of paramour love serve Gaura as Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 30

*rādhā-kṛṣṇa aikya mora śrī-gaurāṅga-rāya*  
*yugala-vilāsa aikye svataḥ nāhi bhāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa are united as our Śrī Gaurāṅga Rāya. The Pastimes of the Divine Couple, however, do not manifest in Him.**

### Text 31

*dāsya paripakke yabe jīvera hṛdaye*  
*śrī-madhura-rasa ude mūrtimāna haye*

**Перевод:**

**“When the relationship of servitorship matures in the heart of the soul, then the relationship of paramour love emerges.**

### Text 32-33

*se samaye bhajanīya tattva gaurahari*  
*rādhā-kṛṣṇa-rūpa haye vraje avatari’*

*nitya-līlā-rase sei bhaktake ḍubāya*  
*rādhā-kṛṣṇa-nitya-līlā vraja-dhāma pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“At that time, the worshippable Lord, Gaurahari, appears in Vraja as Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa and immerses the devotee in the ecstasy of the eternal Pastimes. In this way, the devotee attains the eternal Pastimes of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa in Vraja Dhām.**

### Text 34

*navadvīpe vraje sei nigūḍha sambandha*  
*eka haye dui haya nāhi dekhe andha*

**Перевод:**

**“This is the highly confidential relationship between Navadvīpa and Vraja: they are one, and yet they are also two, but the blind do not see this.**

### Text 35

*sei ta sambandha gaure kṛṣṇe jāna sāra*  
*madhura-rasete gaura yugala ākāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Know this to be the essence of the relationship between Gaura and Kṛṣṇa. In the relationship of paramour love, Gaura’s form is the Divine Couple.**

### Text 36

*sei saba tattva tore rūpa-sanātana*  
*jānāibe alpa-dine vallabha-nandana*

**Перевод:**

**“O Jīva, soon Rūpa and Sanātana will teach you all these truths.**

### Text 37

*tore vṛndāvane prabhu dila adhikāra*  
*vilamba nā kara jīva vraje yete āra”*

**Перевод:**

**“The Lord has given you the qualification to stay in Vṛndāvana. O Jīva, do not delay going to Vraja any longer!”**

### Text 38

*eta bali’ prabhu taṅra mastake caraṇa*  
*arpaṇa kariyā śakti kare sañcāraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this, Nityānanda Prabhu placed His feet on Jīva’s head and empowered him.**

### Text 39

*mahāpreme śrī-jīva gosvāmī kata-kṣaṇa*  
*nityānanda-pada-tale rahe acetana*

**Перевод:**

**In intense divine love, Śrī Jīva Gosvāmī remained unconscious at the feet of Nityānanda for some time.**

### Text 40

*śrīvāsa-aṅgane jīva gaḍāgaḍi yāya*  
*sāttvika vikāra saba dehe śobhā pāya*

**Перевод:**

**He rolled on the ground in the courtyard of Śrīvāsa, and all the symptoms of divine ecstasy shone in his body.**

### Text 41

*kaṅdiyā kaṅdiyā bale, “durbhāgya āmāra*  
*nā dekhinu e nayane nadīyā-bihāra*

**Перевод:**

**Crying, he said, “My misfortune is that I did not see the Pastimes of the Lord in Nadīyā with these eyes.**

### Text 42

*jīva nistārite līlā kaila gaura-rāya*  
*se līlā nā dekhi’ mora dina vṛthā yāya”*

**Перевод:**

**“Gaura Rāya performed Pastimes to deliver all souls. Having not seen those Pastimes, my days have passed in vain.”**

### Text 43

*śrī-jīva yāibe vraje kariyā śravaṇa*  
*śrīvāsa-aṅgane āila yata sādhu-jana*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing that Śrī Jīva would go to Vraja, all the** ***sādhus*** **came to the courtyard of Śrīvāsa.**

### Text 44

*vṛddha-saba śrī-jīve karena āśīrvāda*  
*kaniṣṭha vaiṣṇava māge śrī-jīva-prasāda*

**Перевод:**

**The elder devotees blessed Jīva, and the junior devotees prayed for his grace.**

### Text 45

*kara yuḍi’ bale jīva sakala vaiṣṇave*  
*“mama aparādha kichu-mātra nāhi labe*

**Перевод:**

**Joining his palms, Jīva said to all the devotees, “Please do not take any of my offences.**

### Text 46

*tomarā caitanya-dāsa jagatera guru*  
*e kṣudra jīvere dayā kara kalpa-taru*

**Перевод:**

**“You are all servants of Śrī Caitanya and gurus of this world. O wish-fulfilling trees, please bless this insignificant soul.**

### Text 47

*śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanye mora thākuk rati mati*  
*nityānanda prabhu ha-uk janme janme gati*

**Перевод:**

**“May I have love and devotion to Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya, and may Nityānanda Prabhu be my shelter, birth after birth.**

### Text 48

*nāhi bujhi’ bālya-kāle chāḍilāma ghara*  
*tumi saba jīvanera bandhu ataḥpara*

**Перевод:**

**“I left home in my youth without understanding anything, and since then, you all have been the well-wishers in my life.**

### Text 49

*vaiṣṇavānukampā vinā kṛṣṇa nāhi pāi*  
*vaiṣṇava-caraṇa-dhūli deha sabe bhāi”*

**Перевод:**

**“Without the grace of the Vaiṣṇavas, I cannot attain Kṛṣṇa. O brothers, please give me the dust of the Vaiṣṇavas’ feet.”**

### Text 50-51

*eta bali’ sakale kariyā stuti nati*  
*nityānanda prabhura la-iyā anumati*

*jagannātha-gṛhe giyā śacīra caraṇe*  
*vraje yāite ājñā laya vikalita mane*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this, Jīva bowed and prayed to everyone, and took permission from Nityānanda Prabhu. Then he went to the feet of Śacī in the home of Jagannātha Miśra and with an eager heart took permission to go to Vraja.**

### Text 52

*śrī-caraṇa-reṇu diyā śacī-devī tāya*  
*āśīrvada kari’ jīve karila vidāya*

**Перевод:**

**Śacī Devī gave Jīva dust from her holy feet, blessed him, and bade him farewell.**

### Text 53

*kaṅdite kaṅdite jīva bhāgīrathī pāra*  
*“hā gaurāṅga” bali’ yāya ājñā jāni’ sāra*

**Перевод:**

**Crying, Jīva crossed the Gaṅgā chanting, “O Gaurāṅga!”, and departed, knowing the order of the Lord to be all-in-all.**

### Text 54

*kata-kṣaṇa cali’ cali’ navadvīpa-sīmā*  
*pāra haye yāya jīva ananta mahimā*

**Перевод:**

**Walking ahead, Jīva soon crossed the border of Navadvīpa and left that infinitely glorious abode.**

### Text 55

*navadvīpa-dhāma chāḍi’ śrī-jīva takhana*  
*sāṣṭāṅga praṇami’ cale yathā vṛndāvana*

**Перевод:**

**As he left Navadvīpa Dhāma, Jīva prostrated himself fully and then departed for Vṛndāvana.**

### Text 56

*vraja-dhāma śrī-yamunā rūpa-sanātana*  
*jāgite lāgila hṛde jīvera takhana*

**Перевод:**

**Vraja Dhāma, the Yamunā, and Rūpa and Sanātana then arose in his heart.**

### Text 57

*pathi-madhye rātre svapne dekhe gaura-rāya*  
*jīvere balena, “tumi yāo mathurāya*

**Перевод:**

**At night along the path, Jīva saw Gaura Rāya in a dream. The Lord said to Jīva, “Go to Mathurā.**

### Text 58

*ati priya tumi āra rūpa-sanātana*  
*ekatre karaha bhakti-śāstra prakaṭana*

**Перевод:**

**“You, Rūpa, and Sanātana are very dear to Me, and together you will write scriptures on devotion.**

### Text 59

*āmāra yugala-sevā tomāra jīvana*  
*śrī-vraja-vilāsa sadā karaha darśana”*

**Перевод:**

**“Service to Me as the Divine Couple will be your life, and you will always see Their Pastimes in Vraja.”**

### Text 60

*svapna dekhi’ jīvera ānanda haila ati*  
*vraja-dhāma prati dhāya susatvara gati*

**Перевод:**

**After this dream, Jīva felt intense joy and swiftly ran towards Vraja Dhāma.**

### Text 61

*vraje giyā śrī-jīva gosvāmī mahāśaya*  
*ye ye kārya sādhila tā varṇana nā haya*

**Перевод:**

**The services Śrī Jīva Gosvāmī performed after arriving in Vraja cannot be described.**

### Text 62

*bhāgyavāna jana pare karibe varṇana*  
*śunibe ānanda-citte yata sādhu-jana*

**Перевод:**

**Later, a fortunate soul will describe them, and the** ***sādhus*** **will hear about them with joy in their hearts.**

### Text 63

*chāra-buddhi e bhaktivinoda abhājana*  
*śrī-dhāma-bhramaṇa-vārtā karila varṇana*

**Перевод:**

**The unfit and wicked-minded Bhaktivinoda has described the subject of visiting the Holy Dhāma.**

### Text 64

*vaiṣṇava-caraṇe mora ei se prāthanā*  
*śrī-gaura-sambandha mora ha-uka yojanā*

**Перевод:**

**This is my prayer at the feet of the Vaiṣṇavas: may I have a relationship with Śrī Gaura.**

### Text 65

*śrī-gaura-sambandha-saha navadvīpa vāsa*  
*ha-uka acire mora ei abhilāṣa*

**Перевод:**

**May I soon reside in Navadvīpa and have a relationship with Śrī Gaura. This is my desire.**

### Text 66

*viṣaya-gartera kīṭa ati durācāra*  
*bhakti-hīna kāma-rata krodhe matta āra*

**Перевод:**

**I am a worm in the ditch of mundanity and very misbehaved. I have no devotion. I am lustful and mad with anger.**

### Text 67

*e hena durjana āmi māyāra kiṅkara*  
*śrī-gaura-sambandha kise pāi ataḥpara*

**Перевод:**

**How will a fallen soul and servant of** ***māyā*** **like me develop a relationship with Śrī Gaura?**

### Text 68

*navadvīpa-dhāma more anugraha kari’*  
*udaya ha-una hṛde tabe āmi tari*

**Перевод:**

**May Navadvīpa Dhāma bless me and appear in my heart. Then I will be delivered.**

### Text 69

*prauḍhā-māyā kuladevī kṛpā akapaṭa*  
*bharasā tarite mātra avidyā-saṅkaṭa*

**Перевод:**

**The genuine grace of Prauḍhā Māyā, the protectress of the Dhāma, is my only hope to overcome the dangers of ignorance.**

### Text 70

*vṛddha-śiva kṣetra-pāla ha-una sadaya*  
*cid-dhāma āmāra cakṣe ha-una udaya*

**Перевод:**

**May Vṛddha Śiva, the protector of the Dhāma, be merciful to me. May the divine Dhāma appear before my eyes.**

### Text 71

*navadvīpa-vāsī yata gaura-bhakta-gaṇa*  
*e pāmara śire sabe dāo śrī-caraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**O residents of Navadvīpa and devotees of Gaurāṅga! Place your holy feet on the head of this sinner.**

### Text 72

*ei ta prārthanā mora śuna sarva-jana*  
*acirete yena pāi caitanya-caraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Everyone, please hear this prayer of mine so that I may quickly attain the feet of Śrī Caitanya.**

### Text 73

*nityānanda-śrī-jāhnavā-ādeśa pāiyā*  
*varṇilāma navadvīpa ati dīna haiyā*

**Перевод:**

**Receiving the order of Nityānanda and Śrī Jāhnavā, I have described Navadvīpa although I am very fallen.**

### Text 74

*navadvīpa gaura nityānanda nāmamaya*  
*ei grantha viracita ha-ila niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**Certainly this book has been written so as to be filled with the names ‘Navadvīpa’, ‘Gaura’, and ‘Nityānanda’.**

### Text 75

*ataeva ei grantha parama pāvana*  
*racanā-doṣete doṣī nahe kadācana*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, this book is greatly purifying, and I will not be to blame for any faults in its composition.**

### Text 76

*ei grantha pāṭha kari’ gaura-bhakta-jana*  
*parikramā phala sadā karuna arjana*

**Перевод:**

**May the devotees of Gaurāṅga always study this book and attain the result of circumambulating the Dhāma.**

### Text 77

*parikramā-kāle grantha kaile ālocanā*  
*śata-guṇa phala haya śāstrera vacana*

**Перевод:**

**When devotees discuss this book while circumambulating the Dhāma, they attain a result a hundred times greater. This is stated by the scriptures.**

### Text 78

*nitāi-jāhnavā-pada-chāyā āśa yāra*  
*nadīyā-māhātmya gāya dīna-hīna chāra*

**Перевод:**

**This insignificant, lowly, and humble soul, whose aspiration is the shade of Nitāi and Jāhnavā’s feet, chants the glories of Nadīyā.**

Thus ends the *Parikramā Khaṇḍa* of *Śrī Navadvīpa-dhāma-māhātmya* by Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura.
